### [1] [Spectral properties of deterministic matrices multiplied by rotationally invariant random non-Hermitian ensembles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03618)
*Pierre Bousseyroux,Marc Potters*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 本文研究非厄米随机矩阵乘性变形（multiplicative deformations）的谱性质，考虑确定性矩阵A与旋转不变随机矩阵B的乘积AB，证明在大矩阵极限下其特征值复分布的边界由B的R1和R2变换的简单方程所决定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在理解确定性矩阵与非厄米随机矩阵相乘后复数特征值分布的边界行为。此前非厄米随机矩阵谱研究主要集中于加性扰动，乘性变形情形下的边界刻画尚缺乏系统性理论结果。

Method: 考虑乘积矩阵AB，其中A为确定性N×N矩阵（不必为厄米矩阵），B为旋转不变（rotationally invariant）随机矩阵；通过分析当N→∞时A与B的R1变换和R2变换所满足的边界方程来刻画复数特征值分布。

Result: 证明在大N极限下，乘积矩阵AB的复数特征值分布边界由仅涉及B的R1与R2变换的简单方程控制，说明边界结构具有普适的代数形式。

Conclusion: 该结果表明非厄米随机矩阵乘积谱边界的解析刻画可通过较为简便的变换方程实现，为乘性扰动情形下自由概率方法（free probability）的进一步推广提供了理论依据。

Abstract: In this paper, we study spectral properties of multiplicative deformations of non-Hermitian random matrices. We consider matrices of the form $\mathbf{A}\mathbf{B}$, where $\mathbf{A}$ is a deterministic $N\times N$ matrix (not necessarily Hermitian) and $\mathbf{B}$ is a rotationally invariant random matrix. We show that, as $N\to\infty$, the boundary of the complex eigenvalue distribution of $\mathbf{A}\mathbf{B}$ is governed by simple equations involving the $\mathcal{R}_1$ and $\mathcal{R}_2$ transforms of $\mathbf{B}$.

</details>


### [2] [Design Principles for Reproducible Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03852)
*Jasper van der Kolk,Cory Glover,Albert-Lásló Barabási*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 本文提出了网络设计（network design）框架，通过设计集（design set）编码系统构件的局部约束，并推导出唯一图设计定理（Unigraphical Design Theorem），用以判定系统是否能唯一可复制地装配。作者将此理论应用于3,618个可复制系统（包括蛋白质复合物、分子和机器人），识别出唯一图装配（unigraphical assembly）与引导装配（guided assembly）两类路径，并通过3D打印实验验证了理论的可操作性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从蛋白质复合物到电子电路，许多自然与工程系统只有在精确、可复现的装配方式下才能正常运作。然而，现有网络科学（network science）侧重于生成网络系综（network ensembles），缺乏系统性地复现精确拓扑（exact topology）的机制，因此需要一种能检验系统是否具有可复制装配能力的理论框架。

Method: 本文提出网络设计框架，以设计集编码系统构件所遵守的局部约束，进而推导出唯一图设计定理，判定这些约束是否保证了唯一结构的可复制装配。对于设计集无法唯一确定结果的系统，文中提出引导装配路径，通过时间顺序分解构造过程为多个唯一图步骤。作者将该理论应用于蛋白复合物、分子和机器人等3,618个可再现系统进行分类，并用3D打印构件进行实验验证。

Result: 研究将3,618个可复制系统分类为经历唯一图装配和需要引导装配的两类，并识别出多样性-冗余边界（diversity-redundancy boundary），解释了系统如何在保持唯一装配的同时在构件多样性与结构可互换部分之间进行权衡。3D打印实验表明，可以通过重新设计生成性构造集（generative construction sets）使系统唯一图地装配为目标拓扑。

Conclusion: 网络设计框架将可复制性重构为真实网络（real networks）的一个可在数学上检验的性质，为复杂系统的理性工程（rational engineering）开辟了路径。

Abstract: From protein complexes to electronic circuits, many natural and engineered systems function only if assembled in an exact, reproducible fashion. The structure of each of these systems can be understood as a network, yet network science lacks the mechanisms to consistently reproduce exact topologies, focusing instead on generating network ensembles. We introduce the framework of network design where we encode the local constraints obeyed by a system's building blocks in a design set, and derive the Unigraphical Design Theorem, which determines when these constraints guarantee reproducible assembly into a unique structure, a process we call unigraphical assembly. For systems whose design sets do not specify a unique outcome, we identify guided assembly as a second route to reproducibility, in which temporal ordering decomposes construction into unigraphical steps. Applying these results to 3,618 reproducible systems, including protein complexes, molecules, and robots, we classify those that undergo unigraphical assembly and those that require guided assembly. We further identify a diversity-redundancy boundary that explains how systems trade component variety for structurally interchangeable parts while retaining unique assembly. Finally, we experimentally test the theory using 3D-printed components to re-engineer generative construction sets into systems that assemble unigraphically into prescribed topologies. Network design thus reframes reproducibility as a mathematically testable property of real networks, opening a route to the rational engineering of complex systems.

</details>


### [3] [Quenched complexity of marginal states in the Sherrington--Kirkpatrick spin glass](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04006)
*Tiziana De Chirico,Luca Leuzzi*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 本文在Sherrington-Kirkpatrick模型中计算了热退火（quenched）下边缘亚稳态（marginal metastable states）的计数，采用一步复制对称性破缺（one-step replica symmetry breaking）方案，解决了四十年来仅在退火近似（annealed approximation）下已知的不稳定问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在Sherrington-Kirkpatrick（SK）模型中，指数多个边缘亚稳态的计数需要破缺BRST超对称性（BRST supersymmetry）或采用两组复制子（two-group replica）方案，但四十年来该复杂度仅在退火近似下已知。退火近似在低自由能区域不稳定，并且预测的状态位于Parisi平衡自由能（Parisi equilibrium free energy）之下，因此需要精确的淬火计算。

Method: 采用一步复制对称性破缺（one-step replica symmetry breaking）方案对亚稳态计数进行淬火计算，以替代不稳定的退火近似。

Result: 淬火计算结果在退火曲线产生不稳定之处与之分离，并在完全复制对称破缺（full-RSB）平衡自由能附近消失。

Conclusion: 最低能量的边缘亚稳态很可能就是平衡态本身。

Abstract: In the Sherrington-Kirkpatrick model the exponentially many metastable states are marginal, so counting them requires breaking the BRST supersymmetry or a two-group replica Ansatz. For four decades this complexity was known only in the annealed approximation, which is unstable at low free energy and predicts states below the Parisi equilibrium free energy. We compute it quenched, with one step of replica symmetry breaking. It leaves the annealed curve where it becomes unstable and vanishes next to the full-RSB equilibrium free energy: the lowest marginal states are thus plausibly the equilibrium states themselves.

</details>

### [4] [Formation and interaction of two-dimensional electron-acoustic solitons and breathers in superthermal plasmas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03539)
*Jayshree Mondal,Prasanta Chatterjee,Biswajit Sahu*

Main category: physics.plasm-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了超热等离子体环境中二维电子声（EA）非线性结构的非线性演化与相互作用。利用扩展的庞加莱-黎曼-拉克-郭（PLK）约化微扰技术推导出控制左右传播电子声孤立波（EASW）的双侧卡多姆采夫-彼得维阿什维利（KP）方程，并通过广田双线性方法获得精确解析解。研究重点考察了孤立子与呼吸子之间的迎面碰撞动力学，揭示了伴有相移的准弹性碰撞、瞬态振幅调制和局域能量集中等现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超热空间等离子体中的非线性波动力学对理解行星磁层（如土星环区）的能量重分布机制至关重要。然而，二维电子声非线性结构的相互作用与碰撞动力学尚未得到充分研究。

Method: 以惯性冷电子、由kappa（κ）分布描述的超热电子及提供整体电荷中性条件的静止离子构建等离子体模型。采用扩展的庞加莱-黎曼-拉克-郭（PLK）约化微扰技术推导双向卡多姆采夫-彼得维阿什维利（KP）方程，并通过广田双线性方法获得包括单孤立子、多孤立子、呼吸子和团块结构在内的精确解析解。

Result: 研究发现孤立子-孤立子、呼吸子-孤立子及呼吸子-呼吸子相互作用表现为伴随相移的准弹性碰撞，并出现瞬态振幅调制与局域能量集中现象。振荡与非振荡模式相互作用之间存在显著差异；热电子浓度、温度比和超热性指数等关键等离子体参数对非线性激发特性有显著影响。

Conclusion: 该研究为超热空间等离子体中多维电子声波动力学和能量重分布机制提供了新见解，对理解行星磁层环境（如土星环区域）中的波粒相互作用具有直接意义。

Abstract: The nonlinear evolution and mutual interaction of two-dimensional electron acoustic (EA) nonlinear structures in superthermal plasma environment are studied. The plasma model consists of inertial cold electrons, superthermal hot electrons described by kappa ($κ$) distribution, and stationary ions providing overall charge neutrality. Using the extended Poincaré-Lighthill-Kuo (PLK) reductive perturbation technique, a pair of two-sided Kadomtsev-Petviashvili (KP) equations governing right- and left-propagating EA solitary waves (EASWs) is derived. Exact analytical solutions of the KP equations, including single soliton, multisoliton, breather, and lump structures, are obtained via the Hirota bilinear method. The effects of key plasma parameters such as hot electron concentration, temperature ratio, and superthermality index on the characteristics of these nonlinear excitations are examined. Particular attention is devoted to the head-on collision dynamics between solitons, breather-soliton, and breather-breather interactions. The results reveal quasi-elastic collisions accompanied by phase shifts, transient amplitude modulation, and localized energy concentration, with clear distinctions between oscillatory and non-oscillatory mode interactions. The present study provides new insights into multidimensional electron acoustic wave (EAW) dynamics and energy redistribution mechanisms in superthermal space plasmas, with direct relevance to planetary magnetospheric environments such as Saturn's ring region.

</details>

### [5] [Spectral properties of the magnetic Robin Laplacian on curvilinear half-plane](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03783)
*Diana Barseghyan,Baruch Schneider,Yifan Zhang*

Main category: math.SP

TL;DR: 本文研究具有光滑边界曲线半平面上的磁Robin拉普拉斯算子（magnetic Robin Laplacian），证明了在保守的磁场的紧凑支持下，其谱在小且局部的边界变形下保持稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 众所周知，Robin拉普拉斯算子的谱对边界变形具有不稳定性（unstable）：当边界为直线时，谱纯本质；而边界扰动会在本质谱之下产生离散特征值。作者希望探究在引入磁场的条件下，这种谱不稳定性是否会被抑制。

Method: 考虑具有光滑边界的曲线半平面（curvilinear half-plane）上的磁Robin拉普拉斯算子，其中磁场具有紧致支撑（compact support）。通过分析与直线边界情形的偏差，考察算子谱在小型、局部的边界扰动的行为。

Result: 证明了磁Robin拉普拉斯算子的谱在小且局部的边界 deformation 条件下保持稳定（stable），即与无磁场 Robin Laplacian 在直线边界时的剧烈不稳定行为形成对比。

Conclusion: 磁场（尤其具有紧致支撑的磁场）能够改变Robin拉普拉斯算子对边界变形的谱响应特性，使谱在局部扰动下保持稳定，拓展了对磁场中椭圆算子谱稳定性的理论理解。

Abstract: We analyse magnetic Robin Laplacian in curvilinear half-plane with a smooth boundary. It is well known that the spectrum of the Robin Laplacian is unstable with respect to boundary deformations. This means that if the boundary is a straight line then the spectrum of the Robin Laplacian is purely essential. From the other hand, the perturbation of the boundary produces eigenvalues below the essential spectrum. In this paper, the Robin-Laplace operator with a compactly supported magnetic field is considered. We prove that the spectrum of the magnetic Robin Laplacian is stable under small and local deformations of the boundary.

</details>

### [6] [Constraints on mu-variations from the methanol CH3OH thermal lines in the Galaxy at galactocentric distances of 1.5 < D_GC < 13 kpc](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03431)
*A. I. Shtein,J. S. Vorotyntseva,S. A. Levshakov*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本研究通过分析银河系盘内七个大质量云团中E-甲醇（E-methanol）在216–220 GHz频率范围内的热发射谱线，对基本物理常数——电子与质子质量比（mu = m_e/m_p）的可能变化设定了约束。加权平均得到的Delta mu/mu值为(0.5±0.5)×10^(-7)，未检测到统计显著的mu变化，1σ置信水平为5×10^(-8)，与银河系盘中已有分子云的上限结果高度一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究的动机在于检验基本物理常数是否在宇宙尺度的空间位置上发生微小变化，从而为可能的超越标准模型的新物理学提供观测约束。

Method: 研究基于银河系内七个大质量云团（覆盖1.5 < D_GC < 13 kpc的银心距范围）的E-甲醇热发射谱线，在216–220 GHz频段对mu的潜在变化进行约束分析，并通过加权平均（weighted mean）方法综合作出估计。

Result: 加权平均得到<Delta mu/mu> = (0.5±0.5)×10^(-7)，在1σ置信水平（5×10^(-8)）下未显示mu存在统计显著的变化，这与银河系盘其他分子云中此前建立的上限结果一致。

Conclusion: 该结果为mu在银河系尺度空间范围内不发生变化提供了新的观测证据，进一步收紧了对基本物理常数可变性的约束。

Abstract: In this study, constraints are set on hypothetical variations of the fundamental physical constant mu = m_e/m_p - the electron-to-proton mass ratio - based on thermal emission lines of E-methanol (CH3OH) in the 216-220 GHz frequency range. Molecular spectra toward seven massive clouds located across a wide range of galactocentric distances (1.5 < D_GC < 13 kpc) have been analyzed. The obtained weighted mean value <Delta mu/mu> = (0.5+/-0.5)*10^(-7) does not indicate statistically significant changes in mu at the 1 sigma confidence level of 5*10^(-8), which is in good agreement with previously established upper limits in a number of molecular clouds in the Galactic disk.

</details>

### [7] [Magnetic Couplings in Half-Filled Bipartite Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03548)
*Georges Bouzerar*

Main category: cond-mat.other

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种超越微扰框架的非微扰机制，用于统一描述半填充二部网络中的磁交换规则，可适用于任意二部图，为复杂晶格中的磁性提供了分析预测工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 历史上的磁交换模型严格依赖微扰方法（perturbation approach），且局限于稀薄极限并假设不存在零能量模式。作者旨在突破这些限制，建立普适性的交换相互作用机制。

Method: 放弃传统的微扰展开框架，在非微扰（non-perturbative）层面建立半填充二部网络中的磁交换推广规则，从而绕过传统展开的瓶颈。

Result: 该方法可统一描述任意二部图（bipartite graphs）上的交换相互作用，为复杂晶格中的磁性提供了一个解析预测工具。

Conclusion: 该框架为实现稳定的长程磁有序提供了关键设计原则，可应用于下一代二维自旋电子器件（2D spintronic devices）的工程化设计。

Abstract: We break from perturbative frameworks to establish a non-perturbative generalization of magnetic exchange rules in half-filled bipartite networks. While historical models remain strictly tied to the debatable perturbation approach in the dilute limit and require the absence of zero-energy modes, our universal mechanism governs exchange interactions across arbitrary bipartite graphs. By bypassing traditional expansion bottlenecks, this framework provides an analytical predictive tool for magnetism in complex lattices, offering key design principles to engineer stable, long-range order in next-generation 2D spintronic devices

</details>

### [8] [Nodal obstruction to conditioned-diffusion representations of the weak momentum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03242)
*Chon-Fai Kam,Kai-Wen Wong*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究量子薛定谔桥（Schrödinger bridge）构造在双缝实验中的失效条件，证明当被调理对象为前选择和后选择对在构型空间上的态振幅乘积 φ*ψ 时，正性假设在节点曲线上必然失败，该失败可通过记录数据检验，并给出了相应可观测的实验特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 薛定谔桥构造在端点数据正性假设下确立条件过程的存在性，有三个独立框架采用该构造。但当条件对象为构型空间上前选择与后选择态的振幅乘积 φ*ψ 时，该假设可能在确定轨迹（节点曲线）上失效，这需要被严格检验。

Method: 作者分析 φ*ψ 具有 k 阶移动零点时恒定扩散系数扩散方程的收敛行为；将弱动量分解为渗流部分（osmotic part）与电流部分（current part），分别考察其渐近发散特性；通过符号因子分解（signed factorization）分析实数包络类（real-envelope class），并用双缝轨迹实验中的条纹可见度约束来验证理论推断。

Result: 每当 φ*ψ 具有 k 阶移动零点且概率流穿越该零点时，恒定扩散系数扩散方程无法同时承载条件密度并避开节点曲线——所需电流速度随距节点曲线距离的 2k 次方倒数发散。正性 Doob h-变换在马修构型（Bernstein conditioning）无节点处成立、在节点曲线上失败。渗流部分动量按距离倒数发散并在零点两侧变号，系数由 k 决定；电流部分保持有界。双缝实验中若对比度受离轴零点限制，重构电流通道将出现约 10⁻³ 量级的洛伦兹型（Lorentzian）特征。

Abstract: In this paper, we do not try to solve the measurement problem, but rather to properly formulate it. Inspired by the methodology of theory-independent results like Bell's theorem, we propose to identify the common root of the puzzle in an interpretation-independent way i.e. as a property of the empirical statistics only, before deriving its philosophical consequences. Conditioning this diffusion on a terminal distribution or a terminal measurement effect produces a Doob/Sinkhorn potential whose directional derivative along a unitary control vector field is the imaginary part of a generalized weak value. Method: 作者采用基于矩阵乘积态（matrix-product state）方法的大规模数值模拟，对受周期性驱动（periodic driving）且具有强无序Heisenberg相互作用的有限尺寸自旋链进行动力学演化模拟，从多体局域化初态出发，通过旋度脉冲诱导旋转并监测自旋观测量的子谐波响应。为考察稳定性，作者引入纠缠熵（entanglement entropy）、量子Fisher信息（quantum Fisher information）、短程自旋关联以及受限控制功（restricted-control ergotropy）等多种诊断量，系统扫描旋转角度误差的强度。

Abstract: A discrete time crystal is an out-of-equilibrium phase of matter characterized by the spontaneous breaking of discrete time-translation symmetry. Abstract: Anisotropies in two-dimensional materials are responsible for a variety of effects that significantly modify their physical properties. In this paper, we present a covariant modification of the Dirac equation that incorporates anisotropies into the effective low-energy description around the Dirac point. The model is constructed by analogy with a Lorentz-violating extension of the Standard Model of elementary particles and yields a (2+1)-dimensional framework describing physical effects such as shifted, tilted, and distorted Dirac cones through its free parameters. The proposed model thus provides a general and unified framework in which distinct anisotropy-induced modifications of the Dirac spectrum and their combinations can be systematically characterized. We further apply the model to strained graphene and show that its effective parameters can be quantitatively extracted from first-principles electronic band structures while retaining a direct geometrical interpretation. These results establish a connection between the microscopic electronic structure and a material-independent effective description of anisotropic two-dimensional Dirac systems.

Abstract: We investigate the Andreev reflection (AR) and the nonlinear conductance in a normal-superconductor junction realized with a proximitized semiconductor nanowire exposed to a magnetic field along its axis. The spin-orbit field direction is revealed to be an efficient knob to control the electron transport through the hybrid junction, realizing an electrically widely tunable device. The implementation in realistic setups is discussed.

Abstract: Flux noise is unanimously recognised as a leading dephasing mechanism for flux-tunable superconducting qubits. We apply the resulting framework to time-varying magnetic dipoles, describing surface or substrate spins, as well as current-carrying flux lines. We show that flux noise not only affects dephasing, but also provides a fundamental limit for the qubit quality factor - notably, even when the qubit contains no loops and is thus nominally not flux-tunable. Assuming surface spins as the origin for universal flux noise, we expect that this quality factor limit might be reached in the near term. For flux lines, we formulate a minimal safety distance to conserve the qubit performance, potentially constraining the scale-up of quantum hardware. Conclusion: 研究结果验证了该转移技术的可重复性和通用性，为实现可规模化的单光子和纠缠光子源提供了关键使能步骤。

Abstract: Semiconductor quantum dots (QDs) are a leading platform for realising bright, wavelength-tunable sources of single and entangled photon pairs for photonic quantum technologies. However, implementing additional structures around the photonic nanowire while maintaining its vertical growth geometry has remained a challenge. In this work, we develop a deterministic pick-and-place technique to conduct a vertical-to-vertical transfer of NWQDs from the growth substrate to arbitrary templates. Using this transfer technique, we enhance the photon extraction efficiency to 75% by implementing a bottom gold mirror and tune the emission wavelength by 3.6 GHz via implementing electrostatic gates around the QD. Importantly, we measure low-multiphoton probability (g^(2)(0) = 0.002) and high indistinguishability (>80% for +/-100 ps) of the QD emission after the transfer process, yielding high-quality devices. These results demonstrate the repeatability and versatility of the developed transfer technique, which is an enabling step towards scalable single and entangled photon sources.

Abstract: The absence of translational symmetry in quasiperiodic systems invalidates conventional band theory, posing the central challenge in the field. Second, we introduce that quasiperiodic eigenstates possess Janus quantum numbers: a single eigenstate, continuously tracked across localization transitions, carries dual labels in momentum and real space, which naturally reduce to the familiar Bloch momentum and band index in the commensurate limit. Abstract: Bilayer graphene (BLG) quantum dots (QDs) are a promising platform for semiconductor qubits. However, the low-frequency charge noise that may ultimately limit coherence has remained largely unexplored. Across variations in charge occupation, confinement, source-drain bias, and charge-sensor operating conditions, neither the noise amplitude nor the spectral dependence shows a reproducible trend in electrostatic tuning, indicating that we extracted the intrinsic semiconductor noise. Abstract: The paper presents a theoretical study of many-body electronic phases in doped and electrically biased Bernal-stacked (AB) bilayer graphene. We develop a variational mean-field theory with no fitted parameters. For the electron-electron interaction, we employ a parameter-free random-phase approximation to model the short-range screened Coulomb repulsion. The remaining long-range Coulomb potential energy is dictated by the geometry of the sample, behaving as a parallel-plate capacitor. We formulate the theory directly in terms of the experimentally controlled displacement field $\frak D$ rather than the interaction-renormalized interlayer potential difference. It makes our theory better suited for direct comparison with experiment. The resulting phase diagram is quite rich. It hosts several fractional-metal states connected by first- and second-order transitions. At higher bias and doping, three distinct fractional metallic states emerge. We classify these phases by the number of doped sectors and the symmetry of the order parameters. Our results - obtained without any parameter fitting - reproduce key qualitative and quantitative features of recent experiments. This includes the energy scale associated with the loss of fractional metallic order. At lower bias and doping, the model stabilizes a broad spectrum of fractional-metal phases with more exotic symmetry-breaking patterns. This suggests that further experimental exploration of the latter regime is warranted.

Abstract: The recent discovery of excitons coupled to the magnetic order, and the consequent strong magneto-optic responses, in some van der Waals magnetic semiconductors has triggered intense activity at the interface of magnetism and semiconductor optics. Investigating CrSBr bilayer as a case study, we benchmark our model and its predictions against recent experimental and ab-initio results finding good agreement as well as new insights enabled by the model's simplicity. Examining the optical selection rules, we find the conservation of a quantum number formed from a combination of spin and layer pseudospin to be a useful guiding principle, even in noncollinear magnetic configurations. The presented framework should be valuable in achieving intuitive understanding of recently discovered excitonic phenomena and guiding the discovery of other excitonic states in layered magnetic semiconductors.

Possible applications of the obtained results in organic electronics and biomedicine are discussed.

While the intrinsic band-geometric mechanism and the role of conventional symmetric impurity scattering in the OEE are well understood, the contribution from disorder-induced asymmetric scattering remains unclear. Unlike the Drude channel, the intrinsic, side-jump, and skew-scattering responses survive only in systems with broken time-reversal symmetry. We find that in a magnetized Rashba two-dimensional electron gas (2DEG), these disorder-induced mechanisms can substantially exceed the intrinsic contribution. Remarkably, we find that for a system with Rashba coupling of $1$ $eV Å$, the orbital magnetization is about one order of magnitude larger than the spin magnetization for the chosen parameters, highlighting the crucial role of orbital degrees of freedom in the Edelstein effect.

Namely, we account for both the memory effects at electron scattering by localized defects in magnetic field and an unconventional viscosity effect due to electron scattering by defects in bulk and by rough sample edges. Solution of the model yields a strong negative magnetoresistance, which exhibits at zero magnetic field a sharp maximum in narrower samples or a blunt maximum in wider samples. The ground states fall into three classes: five are collinear, two are non-collinear at angles commensurate with the lattice symmetry, and eight cant at transcendental angles, which we determine to thirty digits. Across the family, failure of the Luttinger--Tisza strong condition coincides exactly with the appearance of incommensurate canting, with no exceptions in either direction. Truncating the interaction range shows that whether a lattice cants is fixed by the local coordination geometry, whereas the value of the canting angle is set by the long-range tail. The quantum corrections are governed by the magnon spectrum through the size of the magnetic basis and are uncorrelated with frustration, so that the frustration and fluctuation classifications are independent; the corner-sharing lattices host the narrowest low-energy branches. The results constitute a spectral atlas of dipolar Heisenberg systems on the full family of $1$-uniform planar tilings and their duals, and provide predictions for inelastic neutron scattering in materials whose magnetic ions occupy Archimedean or Laves lattices, and for artificial arrays of dipolar-coupled nanomagnets.

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 作者采用统一框架处理所有布拉维晶格：用埃瓦德求和计算相互作用张量，通过在全布里渊区最小化最低Luttinger–Tisza能带确定经典基态，并对其序为共线（collinear）的每种晶格计算含零点修正的线性自旋波谱；此外还对每种晶格族在自由度量参数空间中进行优化，并直接进行超胞最小化验证。

Conclusion: 该统一框架揭示了偶极有序是由维数决定的结构原理而非单晶格细节所支配，且整个布拉维晶格空间在最优化后仅有四个吸引子，表明三维偶极磁性可能存在有限的普适类。作者还成功复现了三个已发表基准，验证了方法的可靠性。

Abstract: Every crystal is a Bravais lattice decorated by a basis, so the dipolar ordering of the fourteen Bravais lattices is the natural starting point for any systematic theory of dipolar magnetism in three dimensions. We determine it here in a single common framework: the interaction tensor is Ewald-summed, the classical ground state is obtained by minimising the lowest Luttinger--Tisza band over the entire Brillouin zone, and the linear spin-wave spectrum with its zero-point corrections is computed for every lattice whose order is collinear. First, two structural principles --- the exact tracelessness of the dipolar tensor in three dimensions, and its identical vanishing at $\mathbf k=0$ for every cubic-symmetric lattice --- explain the ordering type, the absence of first-order cubic anisotropy, and the systematics of the zero-point moment reduction. Second, exactly one lattice defeats the Luttinger--Tisza construction: for face-centred orthorhombic the optimal eigenvector is not circular, the single-$\mathbf k$ state is a spin-density wave of non-constant length, and the tabulated energy is a strict lower bound; direct supercell minimisation gives the true ground state. Third, optimising each family over its free metric parameters collapses the whole of Bravais space onto only \emph{four} attractors, with body-centred tetragonal the global optimum and a single interior optimum at rhombohedral $α=62.42^\circ$ lying below face-centred cubic. Three independent published benchmarks are reproduced.

</details>

Resource negativity further shows that microscopic two-qubit entanglement and collective interaction strength are distinct quantities.   <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: Nested sampling (NS) resolves the thermodynamics of an atomistic system from a single simulation, but its practical reach is limited by the Markov-chain updates needed to decorrelate walkers within each likelihood-constrained ensemble. Flow-based NS has removed this bottleneck for gravitational-wave (GW) inference, yet its transfer to atomistic systems is not merely a change of application. Comparing a GW150914-like binary-black-hole likelihood with an eight-particle two-dimensional Lennard-Jones (LJ) system of comparable dimensionality, we show that the two landscapes differ fundamentally: atomistic multimodality is discrete and combinatorial, generated by particle permutations separated by hard collision walls, and its coordinate coupling is dense and collective, whereas the GW posterior exhibits smooth degeneracies and localized parameter coupling. Conclusion: 设计的协议与理论分析揭示了这些非平衡系统中高效驱动控制的设计原则，为理解生物分子机器的优化运行提供了新的见解。

Abstract: Biological molecular machines are mesoscopic systems that act repeatedly and periodically to perform important cellular tasks while contending with strong fluctuations and operating in an overdamped regime. Optimal control theory is a tool that can be used to understand the design principles behind efficient operation of these machines; however, most studies on optimal control of classical mesoscopic systems have focused on control problems that do not repeat periodically. Here, we automatically differentiate Fokker-Planck simulations to design efficient nonequilibrium control strategies for simple models of periodic molecular machines with and without explicit changes in the machine's chemical state. The designed protocols and theoretical analysis provide insight into the design principles governing efficient driving in these nonequilibrium systems. Designed control protocols should seek to reduce mechanical heat by rotating the entire angular probability distribution at a constant speed without changing its shape, and should reduce chemical heat by reducing the proportion of chemical transitions with large heat.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: We investigate the current response of a boundary-driven symmetric exclusion process with quenched site disorder. We approximate the steady-state density profile using a Galerkin projection at linear order and a mean-field closure at higher orders, and thereby obtain the current as a nonlinear function of the reservoir density difference. At linear order in the reservoir density difference, the current-response coefficient depends on the mean reservoir density $ρ$, in contrast to the homogeneous case. Through the linear-response relation, this dependence leads to an equilibrium current-fluctuation coefficient that is asymmetric under $ρ\rightarrow 1-ρ$. Beyond linear response, nonzero even-order current contributions break the antisymmetry of the current under reversal of the reservoir density difference, producing rectification-like behavior in individual disorder realizations. We further show that spatial-reflection symmetry of the equilibrium density profile rules out such behavior, so broken spatial-reflection symmetry of the profile is a necessary condition for rectification-like behavior. Within the present approximation, we further find that, for continuously distributed site disorder, rectification-like behavior occurs arbitrarily close to equilibrium for almost every disorder realization. At the ensemble level, however, the disorder-averaged current remains antisymmetric because the disorder ensemble is invariant under spatial reflection. These results provide a mechanism for rectification-like transport arising from sample-specific spatial heterogeneity rather than from an explicitly imposed directional asymmetry.

Abstract: We present a theory for the optimal control of stochastic systems in structured environments, represented by penalty terms in the cost functional. We show that such control problems generically feature sharp finite-time transitions associated with a qualitative change in the control strategy at a critical time. Starting from an overdamped Langevin equation and a quadratic cost functional, we show that all resulting problems fall into three canonical equivalence classes (parabolic, hyperbolic, and elliptic), distinguished by the sign of the determinant of the control Hamiltonian. For each class, we obtain the optimal protocol, the cost function, and the critical time in closed form, and show that the transition exhibits features of a continuous phase transition at mean-field level. We then establish a mapping between the optimal control cost and the large-deviation rate function governing non-equilibrium relaxation after a potential quench. The mapping covers the parabolic and hyperbolic classes, while the elliptic class has no simple relaxation counterpart. This correspondence implies that recently discovered finite-time dynamical phase transitions, which are exponentially costly to sample directly, are accessible through ordinary averages over optimally controlled trajectories. To validate our theoretical findings, we report three experiments with optically trapped colloidal particles: a control transition for the mean stochastic work, and the finite-time dynamical phase transitions in free diffusion and in harmonic relaxation.

Abstract: We investigate the structures of effective Hamiltonians governing monitored dynamics of a one-dimensional Majorana chain through the Lyapunov spectral analysis. We focus on a gapless phase characterized by finite-size scalings different from those in conventional critical and/or frustration-free systems; the spectral gap closing faster than $1/L$ but slower than $1/L^2$ and the entanglement entropy growing as $[\ln(L)]^2$ with $L$ being the system size. To elucidate the role of these non-Gaussian correlations, we construct random power-law hopping models that capture the essential features of the effective Hamiltonians. We find that, in the absence of hopping correlations, the ground-state entanglement exhibits $\ln(L)$ scaling. In the presence of correlations, by contrast, the entanglement entropy is enhanced and its system-size dependence is consistent with $[\ln(L)]^2$ scaling over the system sizes studied. These results suggest that correlations among long-range hopping magnitudes are responsible for the entanglement scaling that seldom appears in ground states of conventional isolated quantum systems.

Conclusion: 所提出的非渐近框架为热力学推断中的小数据不确定性提供了严格量化工具，可直接用于短轨迹和小样本情境，且能作为探测非平衡特征（如破坏的细致平衡）的鲁棒判据。

Abstract: We derive nonasymptotic upper bounds on the probability that a generalized current of a geometrically ergodic diffusion observed for any amount of time, or its sample mean over any arbitrary sample size, deviates from the stationary mean by more than any given amount. The concentration-of-measure behavior of generalized currents is universally governed by the relaxation time of the underlying dynamics, the locally observed dissipation rate, and the intrinsic local fluctuations of the observable. We uncover stark qualitative and quantitative differences in fluctuations in and out of thermodynamic equilibrium. We further obtain refined inverse thermodynamic uncertainty relations, bounding the variance of generalized currents from above. We construct nonasymptotic confidence intervals for controlling uncertainty in thermodynamic inference from small data, i.e., from short trajectories and small samples, and provide the first quantitative answer to when a trajectory is sufficiently long and a sample is sufficiently large. As an illustration, we apply our results to currents observed on a two-dimensional Ornstein-Uhlenbeck process in and out of equilibrium and show how they can be used to rigorously detect broken detailed balance.

This holds irrespective of the inflationary energy scale and the subsequent expansion history.

Abstract: Primordial black hole (PBH) calculations usually treat rare curvature peaks as isolated collapsing regions. Using fully nonlinear $3+1$ numerical-relativity simulations in a radiation-dominated Universe, we demonstrate that neighbouring primordial curvature perturbations need not map one-to-one onto PBHs: they may ultimately disperse, collapse collectively into a single PBH, or undergo distinct local collapses and form more than one PBH. In the family of two-component profiles studied here, the latter outcome is a pair of PBHs, identified by the coexistence of two disconnected apparent horizons on at least one time slice. We introduce a nonspherical quasi-local compaction diagnostic based on the Hawking mass and referenced to a round flat-FLRW sphere of equal area. It retains the angular structure of the curvature field and reduces to the standard Misner-Sharp compaction function in spherical symmetry. The global maximum of this diagnostic, $\mathcal K_{\rm form}$, provides an empirical indicator of whether at least one PBH forms, with a transition near $\mathcal K_{\rm form,c}\approx0.56$ and modest profile-dependent scatter. For forming bimodal profiles, we supplement the nonlinear compactness with a signed linear surface strength evaluated on the same probing spheres. The viability of the weaker local branch and the competition between the local and common-enclosing branches improve the empirical discrimination between single- and double-PBH outcomes. Our results therefore show that the maximum curvature amplitude alone does not determine the collapse outcome, which also depends on the spatial extent, characteristic scales, and geometry of the surrounding curvature environment.

Abstract: Recent baryon acoustic oscillation measurements from DESI Data Release 2, when combined with CMB and supernova data, strengthen the motivation for exploring departures from $Λ$CDM in the late-time expansion history. Because an evolving dark-energy equation of state and an interaction within the dark sector can produce partially degenerate effects on the background expansion, their observational signatures should be assessed simultaneously. We first consider an interacting $w_0w_a$CDM model with $Q=βHρ_{\rm de}$, using Planck and ACT CMB data, DESI DR2, and DES-Dovekie supernovae. Allowing the dark-energy equation of state to evolve substantially weakens the preference for a nonzero coupling, while the preference for dynamical dark energy persists. The interaction provides essentially no additional improvement in the best fit, suggesting that part of the coupling preference found in more restricted interacting models may reflect a degeneracy with dark-energy dynamics. We then examine the same interaction for three one-parameter dynamical dark-energy trajectories: thawing, mirage, and generalized emergent dark energy (GEDE). The role of the interaction depends strongly on the assumed trajectory. The thawing and GEDE models favor sizable couplings of opposite signs, whereas the mirage trajectory already closely follows the dark-energy evolution preferred by the data and provides little support for an additional interaction. These models also predict markedly different signatures in structure growth, ranging from enhanced matter clustering in the interacting thawing model to strong suppression in interacting GEDE. These contrasting growth signatures, despite substantial degeneracies at the background level, highlight late-time large-scale-structure observations as a promising avenue for distinguishing dark-energy dynamics from dark-sector interactions.

TL;DR: 本文研究了一个与引力非最小耦合（NMC）的标量场驱动宇宙加速膨胀的模型，预测了引力波摩擦函数α_M(z)及引力波/电磁光度距离比，并与GWTC-5数据和宇宙学参数化模型进行了交叉检验，发现当前引力波数据的不确定度过大，无法区分该模型与ΛCDM，但宇宙学数据约束与模型预测存在2.2σ至3.6σ的张力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证非最小耦合标量-张量模型能否同时解释暗能量（与DESI观测一致）及引力波观测数据，并评估常用参数化模型在暗能量动力学背景下是否与物理驱动的标量-张量模型相容。

Method: 采用一个特定的非最小耦合标量-张量（scalar-tensor）模型，该模型可解释与DESI数据吻合的动态暗能量；据此预测引力波摩擦函数α_M(z)及引力波/电磁光度距离比D_L^{GW}/D_L^{EM}，并将其映射到c_M和(Ξ_0,n)两种常用参数化形式；同时采用α_M(z)=c_MΩ_Λ(z)/Ω_Λ0与CPL参数化态方程w_0w_a的常用宇宙学分析模型进行对比。

Result: 映射得到的参数值为c_M=-0.5±0.2，Ξ_0=0.88±0.05，n=3.2±0.3，均与GWTC-5约束在≲1σ水平一致；但该一致性主要源于引力波数据的较大不确定度，使得测量结果同时与ΛCDM和NMC模型相容。宇宙学数据在c_M（w_0w_a）参数上给出的约束与NMC模型预测存在2.2σ（3.6σ）的张力。此外确认，为避免宇宙学不稳定性，该参数化模型引入了与物理驱动的标量-张量模型不相容的强隐含先验。

Conclusion: 当前引力波观测数据不足以区分NMC动态暗能量模型与ΛCDM模型；常用参数化标量-张量模型在动态暗能量情形下施加了与物理动机模型不符的隐含先验，暗示需要更精确的数据或更物理的参数化框架。

Abstract: A scalar field non-minimally coupled to gravity can be the driver of cosmic acceleration. Such a non-minimal coupling (NMC) can produce a non-zero gravitational-wave (GW) friction function $α_M(z)$, which modifies the luminosity distance inferred from GW sources relative to its electromagnetic counterpart. We use a particular NMC scalar-tensor model, that explains time-varying dark energy in good alignment with DESI, to predict $α_M(z)$ and the expected GW/EM luminosity-distance ratio $D_L^{\rm GW}/D_L^{\rm EM}$, and map it onto two common parametrizations---the $c_M$ and the $(Ξ_0,n)$ models. We find $c_M = -0.5\pm 0.2$ and $Ξ_0 = 0.88\pm 0.05$, $n=3.2\pm 0.3$, both consistent with GWTC-5 constraints at the $\lesssim 1σ$ level. This consistency is mostly driven by current large uncertainties in GW data, which lead to measurements consistent with both $Λ$CDM and the NMC model. By contrast, the dark energy constraints derived from analyzing cosmological data, under the common parametric scalar-tensor model using $α_M(z)=c_MΩ_Λ(z)/Ω_{Λ0}$ and the CPL parametrized equation of state $w_0w_a$, are in $2.2σ$ ($3.6σ$) tension with the NMC predictions for the $c_M$ ($w_0w_a$) parameter. We confirm that, in order to avoid cosmological instabilities, this parametrized model imposes strong implicit priors that are incompatible with physically-motivated scalar-tensor models when dark energy is dynamical.

</details>

TL;DR: 本研究利用DUSTGRAIN-PF和DEMNUni宇宙学N体模拟数据库，系统比较了包含有质量中微子、f(R)引力和动力学暗能量等非标准宇宙学模型下暗物质晕的三维与投影剖面，发现低质量晕在f(R)引力下偏离标准宇宙学约10%，并评估了这些差异对Euclid弱引力透镜观测可探测性的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为即将开展的Euclid卫星弱引力透镜（weak lensing）簇观测提供对非标准物理在暗物质晕结构上的精确理论预测，从而评估不同宇宙学模型引入的系统性偏差是否可探测，并避免在未来可能性分析中出现不可忽略的系统误差。

Method: 从ΛCDM及非标准宇宙学的N体模拟数据库（DUSTGRAIN-PF与DEMNUni）中提取三维和投影暗物质晕剖面，比较至5r500c范围内的密度、质量、速度弥散和超额表面密度剖面；在z=1.1进行主分析（并额外分析z=0.5与z=0.3的DUSTGRAIN-PF快照），计算不同机制（f(R)引力、有质量中微子、动力学暗能量）相对于标准模型的偏差百分比，并结合简化欧几里得弱引力透镜假设估计所需晕堆叠数量。

Result: 在低质量晕（M200c<7×10^13 M⊙）中，f(R)引力在剖面外围产生约10%的偏差，而有质量中微子会部分抑制该信号；动力学暗能量和中微子宇宙学通常仅导致数个百分点的较小偏差。在z=1.1检测此类剖面差异需堆叠约10^5个晕，而在z≲0.5时数千个晕即可。浓度-质量关系的宇宙学依赖较弱，约为5%水平。

Conclusion: 非标准宇宙学效应在低质量晕和大半径处对晕剖面产生可观测的偏差，若在欧几里得弱引力透镜质量估计的可能性分析中忽略此类精确建模，将引入不可忽视的系统性偏差，因此建议将非标准物理效应与其他观测效应一并纳入模型。

Abstract: We study the shape of three-dimensional and projected dark-matter halo profiles extracted from cosmological $N$-body simulations in $Λ$CDM and non-standard cosmologies, using the \texttt{DUSTGRAIN-PF} and \texttt{DEMNUni} suites. The models considered include massive neutrinos, $f(\mathcal{R})$ gravity, and dynamical dark energy. By comparing density, mass, velocity-dispersion, and excess-surface-density profiles up to $5\,r_{500{\rm c}}$, we quantify the differential imprint of non-standard physics on halo structure in view of \textit{Euclid} cluster WL studies. Our main analysis is performed at $z=1.1$, a high-redshift regime where the weak-lensing signal-to-noise starts to degrade, providing a conservative stress test for detectability; for \texttt{DUSTGRAIN-PF} we additionally analyse $z=0.5$ and $z=0.3$ snapshots. In low-mass haloes ($M_{\rm 200c}<7\times10^{13}\,M_\odot$), $f(\mathcal{R})$ gravity produces deviations of order $10\,\%$ in projected and three-dimensional profiles, especially in the outskirts where screening is less efficient. Massive neutrinos partially reduce this signal, reflecting the competition between free streaming and fifth-force-enhanced growth. Dynamical dark energy and massive-neutrino cosmologies generally induce smaller, few-percent deviations, with the largest effects again found in low-mass haloes and at large radii. Under simplified assumptions for \Euclid WL, detecting such profile differences at $z=1.1$ requires stacks of $\sim10^5$ haloes, while a few thousands haloes may be sufficient at $z\lesssim0.5$. This further calls for the need of integrating such precise modelling of non-standard effects -- along with other observational effects -- in any likelihood involving \textit{Euclid} WL masses to avoid non-negligible systematic biases. Concentration--mass relations show weaker cosmology dependence, typically at the $\sim5\,\%$ level. [...]

### [68] [Internal geometries regulate the symmetry of defect configurations in cell populations confined to domains with a negative Euler characteristic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03328)
*Mina Kamao,Hiroyuki Miyoshi,Takaaki Nara,Hiroki Miyazako*

Main category: physics.bio-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过实验观测和理论分析揭示了受限细胞群体在三连通域中的向列有序（nematic order）行为，表明障碍物尺寸可调控两个-1/2拓扑缺陷的稳定构型，从而控制细胞排列的对称性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受限细胞群体的向列有序对于决定细胞排列和拓扑缺陷（topological defects）的稳定构型至关重要，但已有研究多聚焦于无内部障碍的区域，其拓扑荷（topological charge）非负。然而生物组织常包围两个或多个内部障碍或孔洞，天然产生带负电荷的缺陷，因此需要系统地阐明障碍物几何效应对细胞排列和负电荷缺陷的影响。

Method: 研究包含两部分：实验上，对受限在两个不同直径圆形障碍物之间的C2C12成肌细胞进行观测，统计缺陷构型的出现频率；理论上，利用细胞排列的显式表达式和Frank弹性能的数值最小化进行系统稳定性分析。

Result: 实验显示当障碍物足够大时，两个-1/2缺陷是最常见构型。数值计算表明，随着障碍物尺寸增大，最稳定构型从水平依次连续转变为离轴再变为垂直构型，且实验观测的缺陷位置与理论预测偏差在60 μm以内。

Conclusion: 障碍物尺寸控制细胞排列的对称性，揭示了几何与拓扑约束如何在形态发生（morphogenesis）或器官运动过程中产生复杂的力模式。

Abstract: Nematic order of confined cell populations plays an important role in determining cell alignment and stable configurations of topological defects, which are related to various biomechanical phenomena. Topological charges (or winding numbers) of topological defects strictly depend on the Euler characteristic of the confining domain, which has typically been non-negative in studies focused on domains without internal obstacles. However, biological tissues often surround two or more internal obstacles or holes, which inherently generate defects with negative charges. To understand the mechanical interaction between cellular tissue and obstacles, it is necessary to elucidate the geometrical effects of obstacles on cell alignment and defects with negative charges. Here, we investigate how cell populations achieve stable defect configurations of two -1/2 defects in a triply connected domain. First, we present experimental observations of C2C12 myoblasts confined by two circular obstacles of varying diameter, demonstrating that two $-1/2$ defects are the most frequent configuration when the obstacles are sufficiently large. Second, to theoretically validate these experimental observations, we perform systematic stability analyses of defect configurations using an explicit expression of cell alignment and numerical minimization of the Frank elastic energy. Our numerical calculations reveal that the most stable configuration shifts continuously from a horizontal, through off-axis, to a vertical configuration as the obstacle size increases. In addition, the experimentally observed defect positions agreed with these theoretical predictions to within 60 $μ$m. These findings suggest that obstacle sizes control the symmetry of cell alignment, providing insights into how geometric and topological constraints can generate complex force patterns during morphogenesis or organ movements.

</details>

### [69] [LHAASO Galactic Plane $γ$-rays Strongly Constrain Heavy Dark Matter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.07982)
*Celine Boehm,Ranjan Laha,Tarak Nath Maity*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本研究首次利用LHAASO观测到的银道面TeV-PeV高能弥散γ射线数据，搜索质量范围在10⁵–10¹¹ GeV的重型暗物质（dark matter）衰变与湮灭信号，通过综合银河系及河外贡献（包括正反光子、逆康普顿散射及级联光子），得到了目前对多种标准模型末态最强的暗物质参数空间约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LHAASO作为地面观测站，最近观测到了银道面在TeV–PeV能段的高能弥散γ射线，这为研究质量高达10⁵–10¹¹ GeV的重型暗物质衰变和湮灭信号提供了前所未有的数据窗口，填补了此前对这一质量区间暗物质搜索的空白。

Method: 论文分别计算了来自银河系和河外的暗物质衰变与湮灭所产生的预期光子通量，并纳入光子对产生（pair production）引起的衰减效应。银河系贡献同时包含正反光子（prompt photons）与由逆康普顿散射（inverse Compton scattering）产生的次级光子，并考虑电子/正电子的传播；河外贡献则额外包含由产生电子-正电子对经逆康普顿散射形成的级联光子（cascade photons）。通过整合以上所有贡献推导暗物质参数空间的约束。

Result: 对于多种双体标准模型（Standard Model）末态，论文导出的暗物质参数空间约束是目前最强的，展示了LHAASO在探测重型暗物质本质方面的能力。

Conclusion: LHAASO的高能γ射线数据能够对重型暗物质的衰变和湮灭场景提供迄今最强的限制，凸显了LHAASO在揭示重型暗物质性质方面的潜力与价值。

Abstract: LHAASO, a ground-based observatory, is unveiling new frontiers in our understanding of high-energy $γ-$rays and cosmic rays. It has recently observed high energy diffuse $γ-$rays from the Galactic plane in the TeV-PeV range. For the first time, we analyze this data to search for signatures of heavy decaying and annihilating dark matter in the mass range $10^{5}-10^{11}$ GeV. We compute the expected photon flux from both Galactic and extragalactic dark matter, incorporating attenuation due to photon pair production. For the Galactic contribution, we include both prompt photons and secondary photons produced via inverse Compton scattering, accounting for electron/positron propagation. For the extragalactic component, in addition to the prompt and inverse Compton contributions, we also include cascade photons arising from inverse Compton scattering of pair-produced electrons and positrons. By combining all these contributions, we derive constraints on the dark matter parameter space. Our bounds for various two body Standard Model final states are strongest to date. This underscore LHAASO's capability to discover the nature of heavy dark matter.

</details>


### [70] [A Task Force on Strong Coupling Determinations from Event Shapes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03002)
*Anthony Badea,Luca Buonocore,Gherardo Vita,Simone Alioli,Zeno Capatti,Yi Chen,David d'Enterria,Thomas Gehrmann,Andre H. Hoang,Alexander Huss,Sebastian Jaskiewicz,Alexander Karlberg,Yen-Jie Lee,Michelangelo L. Mangano,Vicent Mateu,Jiahao Miao,Pier Francesco Monni,Paolo Nason,Luca Rottoli,Iain W. Stewart,Paolo Torrielli,Giulia Zanderighi*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文报告了2025年11月在CERN召开的关于强耦合常数α_s测定的专题工作组首次会议的成果，总结了科学讨论内容，并明确了实验分析和理论任务清单。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: α_s是标准模型（Standard Model）的基本参数，其精确测定对LHC及未来对撞机实验至关重要。尽管事件形状（event shape）观测量是提取α_s的干净途径，但不同测定之间在中心值和不确定性上仍存在显著分歧，因此需要协调社区努力解决这些开放问题。

Method: 提议建立专项工作组（Task Force），通过社区范围内的协调合作处理α_s测定中的分歧。工作组已在2025年11月于CERN召开首次为期两天的会议，综合实验与理论双方的讨论来规划优先事项。

Result: 会议已确定若干优先的实验分析方向，并制定了理论界需在未来会议前完成的具体任务清单。总结了正反电子对撞机（electron-positron collider）上事件形状和相关器（correlator）观测量在α_s提取方面的当前状况。

Conclusion: 为推进α_s精确测定并解决现有分歧，需要持续协调的社区努力；该专题工作组及其定期会议是推动这一目标的核心机制。

Abstract: The strong coupling constant $α_s$ is a fundamental parameter of the Standard Model. Its precise determination is essential for accurately predicting, studying, and understanding processes at the Large Hadron Collider and future experiments such as the Future Circular Collider. Event shape and correlator observables measured at electron-positron colliders provide one of the cleanest environments for extracting $α_s$, thanks to their sensitivity to $α_s$ and the availability of high-precision data from the Large Electron-Positron Collider. More broadly, such observables provide an ideal setting to develop and test our understanding of the perturbative and non-perturbative elements of Quantum Chromodynamics, which will underpin the field's precision and discovery frontiers for decades to come. Despite these advances, significant discrepancies persist between different determinations of $α_s$ from event shapes, both in the extracted central values and estimated uncertainties. This document motivates the establishment of a dedicated Task Force to coordinate a community-wide effort addressing these open questions. We report on the first two-day meeting held at CERN in November 2025, summarizing the scientific discussion and documenting the experimental analyses identified as priorities during the meeting, as well as the concrete list of tasks to be carried out by the theory community in preparation for future meetings.

</details>


### [71] [Energy-Energy Correlators in $Z$-Tagged Jets in $pp$ Collisions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03072)
*João Barata,Zhong-Bo Kang,Xoán Mayo López,Jani Penttala,Yiyu Zhou*

Most of the existing literature hitherto considers a galactic halo dark matter with a heavier partner. In this work, we traverse the alternate route of atmospheric neutrino ($ν$) up-scattering, thus producing a massive beyond standard model (BSM) particle $χ$. The kinematic requirement of such a scattering poses a cut off in the lower recoil energies providing an explanation of the unique isolated event at such a higher recoil energy. Such up-scattering with the nucleons ($\nuc$), $ν\nuc\to χ\nuc$ can be naturally realized in sterile neutrino models, though we keep our analysis generic without specifying $χ$. We identify the region of parameter space that can produce such an isolated event assuming a scalar mediator with mass $m_φ$ and coupling $y_{χ,q}$. For example, with $m_χ\sim1$ GeV, and $\sqrt{y_χy_q}/m_φ=2 \times 10^{-2}$ GeV$^{-1}$ can satisfy such an excess of events while remaining allowed by other existing constraints as well.

*Hitesh Rasineni,Bhavishya Chebrolu*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文基于CMS Run 2015D HTMHT开放数据（积分亮度2.256 fb⁻¹），对强子单Z（hadronic mono-Z）暗物质产生过程进行预期灵敏度研究。采用条件流匹配连续归一化流（conditional flow-matching continuous normalizing flow）对背景进行建模，在三个简化模型基准下分别获得2.89σ、7.62σ和7.41σ的预期显著性，消融实验表明额外喷注运动学信息贡献了53–71%的判别力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 暗物质在强子对撞机中的间接探测信号微弱且背景复杂，需要开发高效的方法从开放数据中提取暗物质产生信号。强子单Z道提供了有别于传统末态（如单喷注）的新探测途径，但其背景建模和特征处理面临独特挑战，亟需系统性研究其灵敏度潜力。

Method: 本研究使用CMS Run 2015D HTMHT开放数据（2.256382381 fb⁻¹），从中选出1,439,523个满足强子单Z选择条件的事件。背景采用条件流匹配连续归一化流模型在选定的HTMHT事件上训练，并在保留的验证集上进行评估后加权至全部分选择事件总体。为缓解缺失对象特征伪影并避免样本内评分偏差，采用了哨兵插补（sentinel imputation）处理不确定角度特征，持久化训练/验证分裂索引，并在选取工作点时强制最低背景产额为20事件。对模拟信号在评分前施加信号侧离线触发代理。此外通过消除精细额外喷注运动学信息的消融实验来评估喷注拓扑的判别贡献。

Result: 在三个简化模型基准下，基线分析分别给出2.89σ、7.62σ和7.41σ的预期显著性。消融研究表明，移除详细额外喷注运动学信息后预期显著性下降53–71%，证明额外喷注拓扑在强子单Z道具备实质性的判别能力。本研究为预期灵敏度（projected sensitivity）研究，未执行解盲（unblinding）。

Conclusion: 条件归一化流模型结合哨兵插补和稳健选择策略，能够在强子单Z道实现有效的背景建模与暗物质信号灵敏度估计；额外喷注结构是该通道的主要判别信息来源。研究的局限性与可重复性已在原文相应章节讨论。

Abstract: We present a projected sensitivity study for hadronic mono-$Z$ dark-matter production using CMS Run~2015D HTMHT open data corresponding to 2.256382381~\invfb, from which 1{,}439{,}523 events satisfy the hadronic mono-$Z$ selection. Backgrounds are modelled with a conditional flow-matching continuous normalizing flow trained on the selected HTMHT events and evaluated on a held-out validation split reweighted to the full selected population. To mitigate artifacts from missing-object features and avoid in-sample scoring bias we apply sentinel imputation for undefined angular features, persist the train/validation split indices, and enforce a minimum reported background yield of 20 events when selecting the working point. A signal-side offline trigger proxy is applied to the simulated signal before scoring. Under this procedure the baseline analysis yields expected significances of 2.89$σ$, 7.62$σ$, and 7.41$σ$ for three simplified-model benchmarks. An ablation study that removes the detailed extra-jet kinematics reduces the expected significance by 53--71\%, indicating that extra-jet topology carries substantial discriminating power in the hadronic mono-$Z$ channel. These results are projected sensitivities (no unblinding performed); the limitations and reproducibility of the study are discussed in Sections limitations and reproducibility.

</details>


### [79] [Comment on arXiv:2608.29450: "First Measurement of Solar Neutrinos through Elastic Neutrino-Electron Scattering at the keV Scale"](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02950)
*Ke Han,Xiangdong Ji,Jianglai Liu,for the PandaX Collaboration*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文针对XENONnT合作组近期发布的太阳pp中微子通量测量结果提出评论，指出其汇总图（summary plot）中存在一个重要遗漏。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: XENONnT合作组通过中微子-电子弹性散射（neutrino-electron elastic scattering）在数十吨级液氩探测器上首次测量了太阳pp中微子通量，但作者注意到其汇总图中缺少一项关键内容。

Method: 作者以评论（comment）形式，对XENONnT合作组发表的摘要图中被忽略的要素进行指正与讨论。

Result: 指出XENONnT汇总图中存在一项重要省略，但摘要未说明该遗漏的具体内容。

Conclusion: 作者认为该遗漏应当被补充或澄清，以确保测量结果的展示完整性和可比性。

Abstract: The XENONnT Collaboration recently reported a new measurement of the solar \(pp\) neutrino flux via neutrino-electron elastic scattering using a multi-ton-scale liquid xenon detector. We comment here on an important omission in their summary plot.

</details>

Zhukov,J. Zhuo,Q. Zou,D. Zuliani,G. Zunica*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文利用LHCb实验采集的质心能量为13 TeV、积分亮度为5.4 fb⁻¹的质子-质子（pp）对撞数据，测量了η和η'(958)介子的反常衰变（anomalous decay）分支比，其中首次搜索了η'(958)的相关衰变，并给出了迄今最精确的B(η→μ⁺μ⁻)测量值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: η和η'(958)介子的反常衰变涉及轻子味和量子反常机制，对其分支比的精确测量可检验标准模型（Standard Model）在低能区的预言，并可能对新物理信号敏感。

Method: 使用LHCb实验采集的pp对撞数据（质心能量√s=13 TeV，积分亮度5.4 fb⁻¹），通过φ(1020)→μ⁺μ⁻衰变作为归一化参考，测量η和η'(958)衰变为μ⁺μ⁻及π⁺π⁻μ⁺μ⁻四个过程的分支比或上限。

Result: 测得B(η→μ⁺μ⁻)=(5.30±0.14±0.18±0.26)×10⁻⁶，B(η'(958)→π⁺π⁻μ⁺μ⁻)=(2.73±0.14±0.13±0.14)×10⁻⁵；在90%置信水平下给出B(η'(958)→μ⁺μ⁻)<1.9×10⁻⁷和B(η→π⁺π⁻μ⁺μ⁻)<5.4×10⁻⁷。各项不确定度分别来自统计、系统及外部归一化分支比。

Conclusion: 该研究首次对η'(958)的相关衰变进行了实验搜索，并提供了迄今最精确的B(η→μ⁺μ⁻)测量，为η/η'反常衰变的理论研究提供了关键实验约束。

Abstract: Branching fractions of $η$ and $η'(958)$ anomalous decays are measured using $pp$ collision data at a center-of-mass energy of $\sqrt{s}=13\,\text{TeV}$ collected by the LHCb experiment and corresponding to an integrated luminosity of $5.4\,\text{fb}^{-1}$. The measured values and upper limits are \begin{align*} \cal{B}(η\toμ^+μ^-) &= (5.30\pm0.14\pm0.18\pm0.26)\times10^{-6} , \\ \cal{B}(η'(958)\toμ^+μ^-) &<1.9\times10^{-7}\textrm{ at 90% CL} , \\ \cal{B}(η\toπ^+π^-μ^+μ^-) &<5.4\times10^{-7}\textrm{ at 90% CL}, \\ \cal{B}(η'(958)\toπ^+π^-μ^+μ^-) &= (2.73\pm0.14\pm0.13\pm0.14)\times10^{-5} , \end{align*} where the uncertainties are, respectively, statistical, systematic, and due to the external branching fraction of the $φ(1020)\toμ^+μ^-$ decay, which is used for normalization. This marks the first experimental search for the $η'(958)$ decay, and the most precise measurement of the branching fraction $\cal{B}(η\toμ^+μ^-)$ to date.

</details>


### [81] [Measurement of Differential Cross Sections and Integrated Luminosity using Proton-Proton Elastic Scattering with HADES at T = 4.53 GeV and T = 1.60 GeV](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02989)

Selyuzhenkov,J. Smyrski,M. Sobiella,S. Spies,A. Sreejith,A. Strach,H. Ströbele,J. Stroth,P. Subramani,K. Sumara,O. Svoboda,K. Swientek,J. Taylor,P. E. Tegner,P. Tlusty,M. Traxler,S. Treliński,I. C. Udrea,F. Ulrich-Pur,V. Wagner,A. A. Weber,C. Wendisch,D. Wielanek,P. Wintz,A. Władyszewska,H. P. Zbroszczyk,M. Zieliński,P. Zumbruch*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文报道了基于升级后的HADES探测器在动能T=4.53 GeV和T=1.60 GeV质子束轰击液氢靶上的弹性质子-质子散射（elastic proton-proton scattering）研究，利用两体运动学约束校准了对准和束流能量，并通过归一化确定了亮度，提取了斜率参数B。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对升级后的HADES探测器进行的首次测量，旨在为弹性质子-质子散射的世界数据库提供贡献，同时为理论建模和未来同能量区域实验的亮度标定提供参考。

Method: 利用弹性散射中约束良好的两体运动学关系，研究探测器对准、束流对准并确定束流能量的最终值。实验亮度通过在四动量转移平方t的重叠范围内归一化到世界其他实验的数据来确定。微分截面在宽范围t上测定，并从中提取斜率参数B。

Result: 在T=4.53 GeV和T=1.60 GeV下分别提取斜率参数B = 8.98 ± 0.3 (GeV/c)^-2和7.3 ± 0.1 (GeV/c)^-2，微分截面在大范围t上被测定。

Conclusion: 该测量是HADES探测器升级后使用前向探测器（Forward Detector）和气体电子倍增器（straw-tube）跟踪平面（为PANDA实验设计）的首个结果，为弹性质子-质子散射世界数据库增添了新的贡献。

Abstract: This work presents an investigation of elastic proton-proton scattering based on data collected with the recently upgraded HADES detector using beams of protons with kinetic energies of $T = 4.53$ GeV and $T = 1.60$ GeV impinging on a liquid hydrogen target. This is the first measurement emerging from the upgraded detector setup, including a Forward Detector with straw-tube tracking planes designed for the PANDA experiment. The well-constrained two-body kinematic relations of elastic scattering have been exploited to study the detector alignment, the beam alignment, and to provide the final measurement of the beam energy. In addition, the recorded luminosity of the experiment has been determined by normalizing the HADES data in a range of the squared four-momentum transfer $t$ that overlaps with measurements by previous experiments worldwide. Furthermore, the differential cross sections have been determined over a large range of $t$ from which the slope parameter $B$ is extracted, yielding $B = 8.98 \pm 0.3$ (GeV/$c$)$^{-2}$ and $7.3 \pm 0.1$ (GeV/$c$)$^{-2}$ at $T = 4.53$ GeV and $1.60$ GeV, respectively. This contribution to the world database on elastic proton-proton scattering provides input to theoretical modeling and serves as a reference for luminosity determinations by future experiments in the same energy range.

</details>

Zhuge,J. H. Zou,J. Zu*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 基于BESIII探测器在BEPCII对撞机上采集的质心能量3.773 GeV、积分亮度7.93 fb⁻¹的e⁺e⁻湮灭数据，测量了D⁰ → Kˢ₀ω和D⁰ → Kᴸ₀ω两个衰变道的绝对分支比（branching fraction），并计算了Kˢ₀–Kᴸ₀分支比不对称度（asymmetry）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确测量D介子（D meson）衰变的绝对分支比及Kˢ₀与Kᴸ₀之间的不对称度，可为理解粲介子弱衰变机制和CPT对称性等基本物理特性提供实验依据。

Method: 利用BESIII探测器在BEPCII对撞机上于质心能量3.773 GeV处采集的e⁺e⁻湮灭数据（对应积分亮度7.93 fb⁻¹）进行测量；基于D⁰ → Kₛ⁀ω与D⁰ → Kᴸ₀ω两个衰变道，将实验不确定度区分为统计误差（statistical）、系统误差（systematic）以及来自D⁰ → π⁺π⁻π⁰Kₛ,ᴸ₀非共振过程（non-resonant process）与目标衰变道之间干涉（interference）引起的不确定度。

Result: D⁰ → Kˢ₀ω的绝对分支比测得为(11.79 ± 0.19 ± 0.26 ± 0.47)×10⁻³，D⁰ → Kᴸ₀ω的分支比为(10.84 ± 0.14 ± 0.23 ± 0.44)×10⁻³；Kˢ₀–Kᴸ₀分支比不对称度R(D⁰, Kˢ,ᴸ₀ω) = (4.2 ± 1.0 ± 0.9 ± 2.8)%。

Conclusion: 两个衰变道的绝对分支比与不对称度均以较高精度确定，且不对称度结果与零相比处于统计误差范围内，未发现显著的Kˢ₀–Kᴸ₀不对称信号。

Abstract: Based on $e^+ e^-$ annihilation data corresponding to an integrated luminosity of 7.93~$fb^{-1}$ collected at a center-of-mass energy of 3.773 GeV with the BESIII detector at the BEPCII collider, the absolute branching fractions of the decays $D^0 \to K_{S}^{0} ω$ and $D^0 \to K_{L}^{0} ω$ are measured to be $(11.79 \pm 0.19 \pm 0.26 \pm 0.47) \times 10^{-3}$ and ($10.84 \pm 0.14 \pm 0.23 \pm 0.44) \times 10^{-3}$, respectively.
  The $K_{S}^{0}- K_{L}^{0}$ branching-fraction asymmetry of these two decays is $R(D^0,K_{S,L}^{0} ω) = \frac{\mathcal{B}(D^0 \to K_{S}^{0} ω) - \mathcal{B}(D^0 \to K_{L}^{0}ω)}{\mathcal{B}(D^0 \to K_{S}^{0} ω) + \mathcal{B}(D^0 \to K_{L}^{0} ω)} =(4.2 \pm 1.0 \pm 0.9 \pm 2.8)\%$.
  Here, the first uncertainties are statistical, the second systematic, and the third arise from the interference between $D^0 \to K_{S,L}^{0} ω$ and the non-resonant $D^0 \to π^+ π^- π^0 K_{S,L}^{0}$ processes.

</details>


### [83] [Multi-tesla operation of high-temperature superconducting cavities for accelerated axion dark matter searches](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03879)
*Danho Ahn,Jinsu Kim,Seongtae Park,Jiwon Lee,Ohjoon Kwon,Woohyun Chung,HeeSu Byun,Sergey Uchaikin,Arajan Ferdinand van Loo,Yasunobu Nakamura,Dojun Youm,SungWoo Youn,Yannis K. Semertzidis*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于应变控制机械剥离稀土钡铜氧（REBCO）超导带材的极对极（pole-to-pole）三维腔体架构，用于轴子暗物质（axion dark matter）搜索。该方案通过将损耗性金属衬底选择性剥离并利用铜稳定层作为导电背衬，将纵向装配间隙转化为截止波导，有效抑制了跨缝隙射频泄漏，在8 T磁场下实现无载品质因数（$Q_0$）达1.4×10⁷的5.4 GHz谐振腔，比铜基线高两个数量级；同时工程化了一款2.3 GHz可调谐探路者腔体，在CAPP-PACE实验中实现180 mK噪声温度和5倍品质因数提升，累计获得约8.4倍扫描速率加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 轴子（axion）作为暗物质候选粒子能够同时解释量子色动力学中的强电荷宇称问题以及暗物质本质两大基本物理疑难，但现有探测手段面临双重瓶颈：标准超导体在多特斯拉磁场中遭受严重涡旋耗散，而铜腔体则受异常趋肤效应（anomalous skin effect）限制，品质因数仅约为10⁵。因此，亟需一种能在强磁场下维持超高品质因数又能实用化集成的谐振腔架构。

Method: 采用从应变控制、机械剥离的REBCO带材构建极对极三维腔体架构：通过选择性剥离损耗性金属衬底，同时利用铜稳定层作为"导电背衬"，将纵向装配间隙转化为截止波导（cutoff waveguide），从而有效抑制跨缝隙射频泄漏。研究采取双轨策略：其一，用5.4 GHz谐振腔揭示材料在高场下的固有潜力；其二，以实用化轴子光腔（haloscope）集成为优先目标，设计可调谐、体积最大化的2.3 GHz探路者腔体。

Result: 5.4 GHz腔体在8 T磁场中获得无载品质因数为1.4×10⁷，超越传统铜基线两个数量级；2.3 GHz可调谐探路者腔体部署于CAPP-PACE的前驱轴子腔实验（Pilot Axion Cavity Experiment），实现180 mK噪声温度与较铜提升5倍的品质因数，累计约8.4倍的扫描速率（scan rate）加速。

Conclusion: 该REBCO三维腔体架构同时克服了标准超导体在强场下的涡旋耗散与铜腔的异常趋肤效应限制，兼具超高无载品质因数与实用化集成能力，显著提升轴子光腔的探测扫描速率，为高场轴子搜索提供了可行且高效的技术路径。

Abstract: Axion haloscopes use radio-frequency cavities immersed in a magnetic field to search for dark-matter axions, which could resolve two central puzzles in fundamental physics: the strong charge-parity problem in quantum chromodynamics and the nature of dark matter. Multi-tesla fields trigger axion-to-photon conversion but induce severe vortex dissipation in standard superconductors, whereas copper cavities are limited by the anomalous skin effect ($Q \lesssim 10^5$). Here, we overcome these barriers by introducing a pole-to-pole 3-dimensional cavity architecture constructed from strain-controlled, mechanically delaminated rare-earth barium copper oxide (REBCO) tapes. By selectively stripping the lossy metallic substrate while utilizing the copper stabilizer as a "conductive backing" we convert the longitudinal assembly gaps into waveguides below cutoff, effectively suppressing cross-seam RF leakage. Employing a two-track strategy, we first unveiled the intrinsic high-field potential of the material with a 5.4 GHz resonant cavity, achieving an unloaded quality-factor ($Q_0$) of $1.4 \times 10^7$ in an 8 T magnetic field, exceeding conventional copper baselines by two orders of magnitude. Second, prioritizing practical haloscope integration, we engineered a tunable, volume-maximized 2.3 GHz pathfinder cavity. Deployed in the Pilot Axion Cavity Experiment at the Center for Axion and Precision Physics Research (CAPP-PACE), this system achieved a 180 mK noise temperature and a 5-fold $Q$ enhancement over copper, cumulatively delivering a $\sim$8.4-fold scan-rate acceleration.

</details>

### [84] [Exact Scaling Laws and Non-Hermitian Topological Phase Transitions of Active Continuum on Hyperbolic Manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03500)
*Yu-Xin Xie*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本文在双曲空间（hyperbolic space）$\mathbb{H}^2$上建立了精确代数框架，重写活性连续介质的相变理论，导出临界阈值 $\alpha_c = \frac{5}{4}D\kappa^2$，揭示了负曲率在活性相变中作为非微扰红外正则化机制的几何根源，并进一步预言了由无相互作用矩阵驱动的非厄米奇异点（exceptional point）及其幂律动力学标度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的曲流形上活性连续介质集体运动研究依赖于微扰动态重整化或有限元数值模拟，难以揭示运动背后的几何机制。作者旨在建立一个精确的代数框架，从而以解析方式理解几何曲率对活性相变与非厄米拓扑行为的深层作用。

Method: 作者对协变的类Navier-Stokes方程进行严格展开，利用Weitzenböck恒等式（Weitzenböck identity）将其归约为Hodge–de Rham拉普拉斯算子的几何质量隙问题；随后定义了拓扑自由能达到Bogomolny-Prasad-Sommerfield（BPS）极限的参数子空间，并通过Möbius规范对称性将速度场投影为Blaschke乘积；还将非变分活性对流模映射到缺陷平移零模，构造出本质非互易的相互作用矩阵。

Result: 精确推导出宏观偏振的临界阈值 $\alpha_c = \frac{5}{4}D\kappa^2$；证明平直极限（$\kappa\to 0$）精确退化到经典O(2)模型的拓扑相；识别出动态凝聚缺陷环的奇异积分算子所对应的宏观二阶奇异点（EP2），其动态标度满足严格的代数律 $\tau \sim |\Delta\nu|^{-1/2}$。

Conclusion: 恒定负曲率充当了非线性振幅饱和的非微扰红外正则化，避免了微扰方法的系统性局限。该封闭式理论框架为软力学（soft mechanics）中的几何阻挫与非厄米拓扑提供了精确解析解。

Abstract: The macroscopic collective motion of active continuum on curved manifolds is conventionally addressed through perturbative dynamic renormalization or finite-element simulations, often obscuring the underlying geometric mechanisms. Here, an exact algebraic framework is established to reformulate the active phase transition on hyperbolic spaces $\mathbb{H}^2$. By rigorously expanding the covariant Navier-Stokes-like equations and applying the Weitzenböck identity, we derive the exact critical threshold $α_c = \frac{5}{4}Dκ^2$ for macroscopic polarization, which is dictated by the geometric mass gap of the Hodge-de Rham Laplacian. We strictly define the parameter subspace $α= 2Dκ^2$ where the topological free energy reaches the Bogomolny-Prasad-Sommerfield (BPS) limit. This enables the reduction of the complex velocity field to Blaschke products via Möbius gauge symmetry. The flat-space limit ($κ\to 0$) exactly degenerates to the topological phase of the classical O(2) model, demonstrating that the constant negative curvature acts as an un-perturbative infrared regularization for non-linear amplitude saturation. Furthermore, mapping the non-variational active convective modes onto the defect translational zero-modes yields an intrinsically non-reciprocal interaction matrix. By analytically extending the singular integral operator of the dynamically condensed defect ring, we identify a macroscopic second-order exceptional point (EP2) characterized by a strictly algebraic dynamic scaling law $τ\sim |Δν|^{-1/2}$. This closed-form theoretical paradigm provides exact solutions for geometric frustration and non-Hermitian topology in soft mechanics.

</details>


### [85] [Critical scaling and soft granular rheology of frictionless non-Brownian suspensions across jamming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03110)
*Rohan Vernekar,Romain Mari,Bruno Chareyre,Hugues Bodiguel*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本文通过三维计算机模拟研究了可变形、非布朗、无摩擦颗粒在堵塞转变（jamming transition）附近的悬浮液流变学（suspension rheology），确认剪切驱动堵塞为需要强剪切修正的二阶临界现象，并利用软颗粒流变学标度关系在宽广密度范围内实现了数据坍缩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解悬浮液在堵塞转变附近的流变行为是流变学和软物质物理学中的核心问题，作者希望验证剪切驱动堵塞是否为二阶临界现象，并检验现有流变学标度框架在多大密度范围内有效。

Method: 采用恒定剪切速率和悬浮密度的三维计算机模拟（3D computer simulations），研究对象为多分散球形粒子，粒子具有可变形、非布朗、无摩擦特性。模拟覆盖堵塞点φ_c附近的密度以及更宽的密度范围，对接近堵塞数据开展临界标度分析，构建局部应变速率指数图以对流动机理进行分类，并在更宽密度范围内应用软颗粒流变学标度（soft granular rheological scaling）。

Result: 临界标度分析确认剪切驱动堵塞为二阶临界现象（second-order critical phenomenon），且需要强剪切修正；基于临界标度堵塞密度，软颗粒流变学标度关系在宽广的动力学和密度范围内对数据实现了优秀的数据坍缩。

Conclusion: 软颗粒流变学关系能够在几乎整个动力学与密度范围内预测悬浮液流变行为，表明该框架对可变形非布朗摩擦无颗粒系统的普适适用性。

Abstract: We carry out 3D computer simulations to study suspension rheology across the jamming transition for deformable non-Brownian frictionless particles. The simulations are carried out at constant shear rates and suspension densities ($\dotγ , φ$), for polydisperse spherical particles. The suspension is probed at densities in the neighbourhood of the jamming point $φ_c$ , as well as across much wider density ranges. We carry out critical scaling analysis of our close-to-jamming data and confirm that shear-driven jamming is a second-order critical phenomenon requiring strong corrections on shear. We build maps of the local strain rate exponent to classify flow regimes and examine the effect of the corrections on the suspension rheology. Further, using the critical scaling jamming density, we carry out soft granular rheological scaling over the wider density range, demonstrating an excellent data collapse. The soft granular rheology relations are able to predict our data over almost the entire dynamical and density range.

</details>


### [86] [Electromechanical Domain Wall Propagation in Dielectric Elastomers: An Exact Geometric Resolution via Conformal Mapping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03913)
*Yu-Xin Xie*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本文提出一种渐近精确的几何框架，通过共形映射和拓扑质量项将介电弹性体中的局域机电相变解析为非线性σ模型，严格推导出畴壁作为不对称异宿轨道连接两个鞍点，并给出界面能量、畴壁厚度和渐近衰减长度的精确标度律，消除所有唯象参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统唯象模型完全忽略面内电场分量和几何奇异性，从根本上低估了介电弹性体中由高曲率界面驱动的构型力，无法准确解析畴壁传播中的运动边界和静电边缘场行为。

Method: 通过共形映射将高度变形电流构型映射为正则参数条带以代数消除场奇异性；利用精确分部积分将高阶几何度量转化为严格的拓扑质量项，将全局共形静电学投影为非线性σ模型拉格朗日密度；结合Gent应变硬化模型，利用连续平移对称性获得哈密顿首次积分；通过本征值分析和BPS界计算证明畴壁作为不对称异宿轨道的存在性。

Result: 严格证明了畴壁以不对称异宿轨道形式连接两个鞍点，获得了局域界面能量、畴壁厚度和渐近衰减长度的精确解析标度关系。

Conclusion: 该几何解析框架消除了所有唯象参数，为活性软物质中的几何不稳定性提供了确定性的解析范式。

Abstract: The localized electromechanical phase transition in dielectric elastomers involves complex moving boundaries and severe electrostatic fringe fields driven by high-curvature interfaces. Traditional phenomenological models fundamentally underestimate the configurational forces by completely ignoring the in-plane electric field components and geometric singularities. Here, we present a asymptotically exact geometric framework to resolve the domain wall propagation. By mapping the highly deformed current configuration to a regular parametric strip via conformal mapping, the field singularities are algebraically eliminated. An exact integration by parts directly translates the higher-order geometric metric into a rigorous topological mass term, projecting the global conformal electrostatics into a non-linear $σ$-model Lagrangian density. Incorporating the Gent strain-stiffening model, the continuous translation symmetry yields a Hamiltonian first integral. Eigenvalue analysis and Bogomol'nyi-Prasad-Sommerfield (BPS) bound calculations prove that the domain wall emerges strictly as an asymmetric heteroclinic orbit connecting two saddle points. This geometric resolution provides exact analytical scalings for the localized interface energy, domain wall thickness, and asymptotic decay lengths, eliminating all phenomenological parameters and offering a deterministic paradigm for geometric instabilities in active soft matter.

</details>


### [87] [Computing stable configurations of confined smectic liquid crystals with a deep variational framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03389)
*Yuchen Xie,Baoming Shi,Yucen Han,Lei Zhang*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种深度变分框架（deep variational framework, DVF），用于在修正的Landau–de Gennes模型中计算近晶（smectic）液晶在复杂几何约束下的稳定构型。该方法通过坐标映射将取向序参量和位置序参量定义在规则参考域上，并引入预热惩罚（warmup penalty）来缓解神经网络的频谱偏置（spectral bias），从而稳定地恢复振荡性的近晶层状态。实验表明，该框架能够复现实验上已确立的近晶A缺陷结构和层形态，并在切向锚定球体中预测出类似人字形（chevron-like）的近晶C态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近晶液晶具有取向序与周期性密度调制双重特征，尽管其结构可用连续介质理论建模，但在复杂几何中计算稳定构型仍具挑战性——特别是需要精确解析与近晶层相关的低频和高频密度调制（high-frequency density modulations）耦合问题时，传统方法面临数值困难。

Method: 提出深度变分框架（DVF），在修正的Landau–de Gennes模型下将耦合的取向序参量和位置序参量表示在规则参考域上，物理约束通过坐标映射（coordinate mappings）施加；此外引入预热惩罚（warmup penalty）抑制神经网络倾向于生成光滑、无分层场的频谱偏置，从而恢复振荡性近晶态。

Result: 与神经网络基线和有限差分松弛方法比较，验证了预热惩罚的关键作用及所得层状态的数值稳定性。DVF在不同约束几何下复现了实验中已确立的近晶A（smectic-A）缺陷结构与层形态，并在切向锚定球体中预测出类似人字形的近晶C（smectic-C）态。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，DVF能够可靠地计算多种实验相关约束几何与锚定条件下的稳定近晶构型，为该类液晶系统的数值模拟提供了通用且稳定的新工具。

Abstract: Smectic liquid crystals are layered liquid-crystalline phases characterized by orientational order and periodic density modulation. Although their structures can be modeled using continuum theories, computing stable configurations remains challenging in complex geometries, particularly when the high-frequency density modulations associated with smectic layering should be resolved. We propose a deep variational framework (DVF) for computing these configurations within the modified Landau--de Gennes model, in which the coupled orientational and positional order parameters are represented on a regular reference domain while physical confinement is incorporated through coordinate mappings. A warmup penalty mitigates the spectral bias of neural networks toward smooth, nonlayered fields, enabling robust recovery of oscillatory smectic states. Comparisons with a neural-network baseline and finite-difference relaxation demonstrate the essential role of this penalty and the numerical stability of the resulting layered states. The DVF reproduces experimentally established smectic-A defect structures and layer morphologies across diverse confinement geometries and further predicts a chevron-like smectic-C state in a tangent-anchored sphere. Together, these results demonstrate the applicability of the DVF to computing stable smectic configurations across experimentally relevant confinement geometries and anchoring conditions.

</details>


### [88] [Granular Rods Fall Faster in Denser Obstacle Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03473)
*Fumiaki Nakai,Hiroaki Katsuragi*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本研究通过模拟耗散性杆状粒子在重力驱动下穿越随机固定障碍物场的运动，发现随着障碍物密度增加，杆的平均下落速度呈现先减、后增、再减的非单调变化，最终导致粒子被阻陷。这种由粒子各向异性引发的非单调驱动输运（driven transport）行为，可通过碰撞率与杆几何形状的标度论证得以完整解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 粒子形状如何影响外力驱动下穿越障碍物场的输运行为，是非平衡物理（nonequilibrium physics）中的基本问题，但此前缺乏系统性的理解。

Method: 采用计算机模拟方法，在重力作用下模拟耗散性杆状粒子（dissipative rod）穿过随机分布的固定障碍物的下落过程，并系统变化障碍物密度以观测输运行为的变化。

Result: 发现平均下落速度随障碍物密度增加呈现四阶段响应：先下降、再上升、随后再次下降直至粒子被阻陷（trapping）。即杆在更密的障碍物场中反而可能下落得更快。基于碰撞率和杆几何形状的标度论证（scaling arguments）能够解释全部三个输运区间及其交叉点，并可预测阻陷前的平均下落距离。

Conclusion: 研究表明粒子各向异性（particle anisotropy）是产生非单调驱动输运现象的关键机制，为理解形状效应对无序介质中主动或外部驱动粒子运动的影响提供了新的理论视角。

Abstract: How particle shape affects transport through obstacle fields under external driving is a fundamental question in nonequilibrium physics. We simulate a dissipative rod falling under gravity through randomly placed fixed obstacles. As the obstacle density increases, the mean descent speed decreases, increases, and then decreases again before trapping. The rod can therefore fall faster in a denser obstacle field. Scaling arguments based on collision rates and rod geometry explain all three regimes, their crossovers, and the mean fall distance before trapping. These results reveal nonmonotonic driven transport arising from particle anisotropy.

</details>


### [89] [Near-surface colloidal dynamics in jammed and slipping microgel suspensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03540)

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 致密的软微凝胶粒子悬浮液在光滑边界处可能发生滑移，但其所依赖的近表面层的动力学行为因典型亚微米尺度而难以被直接观测，因此需要一种能够在纳米尺度探测壁面附近粒子动力学的方法。
