Result: 悬浮液的速度剖面呈非线性，表明近壁存在动力学异质性；速度剖面在距壁面亚微米距离处趋于恒定滑移速度，与宏观壁面滑移测量结果一致；纳米尺度粒子海拔分布强烈依赖于滑移速度，揭示出由动力学介导的纳米尺度粒子组织效应。

Conclusion: 实验结果支持近壁异质性（near-wall heterogeneity）是导致微凝胶壁面滑移的主导机制，并为复杂界面环境中粒子动力学与组织行为的研究开辟了新途径。

Abstract: Jammed suspensions of soft microgel particles may exhibit slippage along smooth boundaries. Owing to their expected sub-micrometric dimensions, direct observations of dynamics within the near-surface layers supposed to be responsible for this slippage have been difficult to achieve. Here, we use total internal reflection fluorescence microscopy (TIRFM) to observe nanoparticle dynamics near glass/microgel-suspension interfaces. Indicating near-wall dynamic heterogeneity, velocity profiles for suspensions are nonlinear. These profiles tend to a constant slippage velocity at submicrometric distances from the wall, consistent with macroscopic wall slip measurements. Furthermore, nanoscale particle altitude distributions are strongly dependent on the slip velocity, revealing a dynamically-mediated and nanoscale particle-organisation effect. The collected observations give support for the existence of near-wall heterogeneity as a dominant mechanism contributing to microgel wall slip. Our work also opens new perspectives for the study of particle dynamics and organisation in complex interfacial environments.

</details>


### [90] [Heterogeneous collapse in thermoresponsive copolymer microgels varying molar composition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03571)
*Jacopo Vialetto,Francesco Brasili,Letizia Tavagnacco,Gavino Bassu,Elena Buratti,Stephen King,Emanuela Zaccarelli,Marco Laurati*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本文通过小角中子散射（SANS）与同位素标记及单体分辨模拟相结合，揭示了热响应型P(NIPAM-co-NIPMAM)共聚物微凝胶的内部异质性由其嵌段状单体分布所控制。研究发现网络网格相关长度在相转变附近呈现普适最大值，且该最大值随NIPMAM含量增加而增大，在75 mol% NIPMAM时达到最大，并与平衡溶胀率相关联，为设计功能性软材料提供了指导原则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解共聚物微凝胶的内部架构对于建立纳米尺度聚合物组织如何控制其刺激响应行为至关重要。本文旨在阐明不同摩尔组分比例的P(NIPAM-co-NIPMAM)微凝胶中，内部异质性如何决定其体积相转变与平衡溶胀行为。

Method: 通过自由基沉淀聚合法（radical precipitation polymerization）合成不同摩尔分数的P(NIPAM-co-NIPMAM)微凝胶；采用小角中子散射（small-angle neutron scattering, SANS）结合同位素标记（isotopic labeling）进行实验表征，并与单体分辨模拟（monomer-resolved simulations）结果进行对比分析，研究聚合物网格网格相关长度（mesh correlation length）及内部结构。

Result: SANS与模拟结果一致表明两种组分呈嵌段状（block-like）单体分布；聚合物网格相关长度在相转变附近呈现普适最大值，证实了塌缩的NIPAM富集域与溶胀域共存。相关长度随NIPMAM含量增加而增大，在75 mol% NIPMAM时达到最大值，表明网络内稀疏塌缩区域导致高度异质性。最大异质性与平衡溶胀率相关联，表明塌缩微凝胶保留了相转变的结构记忆。

Conclusion: 嵌段状单体分布对共聚物微凝胶的响应特性具有复杂影响，这一发现为通过组分调控设计响应性胶体（responsive colloids）提供了可操作的规则，有助于功能性软材料的开发。

Abstract: Understanding the internal architecture of copolymer microgels is crucial for establishing how nanoscale polymer organization controls their stimuli-responsive behavior. Here we focus on thermoresponsive P(N-isopropylacrylamide-co-N-isopropyl-methacrylamide), P(NIPAM-co-NIPMAM), microgels with varying mole fraction of the components, synthesized via radical precipitation polymerization, and we demonstrate that changes in their volume phase transition and equilibrium swelling are governed by composition-dependent internal heterogeneity. Comparison between small-angle neutron scattering (SANS) with isotopic labeling and monomer-resolved simulations show a block-like monomer distribution of the two components. SANS analysis reveals a universal maximum in the polymer mesh correlation length near the transition, evidencing coexistence of collapsed NIPAM-rich and swollen domains. The correlation length increases with increasing NIPMAM content, with a maximum for 75 mol \% NIPMAM, implying sparse collapsed regions within the network and thus a large degree of heterogeneity induced by the presence of an increasingly large fraction of intercalated, non-collapsing PNIPMAM. The maximum heterogeneity correlates with the equilibrium swelling ratio, indicating that collapsed microgels retain a structural memory of the transition and present a less-compliant structure in response to temperature variations. Overall, these insights highlight a complex effect of the block-like monomer distribution on the responsive properties of copolymer microgels with different compositions, thus providing a design rule for tailoring responsive colloids for functional soft materials.

</details>


### [91] [Charge Regulated conformational properties of polyelectrolyte near an oppositely charged nanoparticle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03587)
*Kawaljeet Kaur,Rashmi Kandari,Sunita Kumari,Subhajit Paul*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本文通过混合CR蒙特卡洛/分子动力学模拟，研究了电荷调控（charge regulation, CR）对聚电解质（polyelectrolyte, PE）在带相反电荷纳米颗粒表面的吸附动力学的影响，发现高盐浓度下CR增强了吸附并使PE回转半径迅速下降，吸附过程呈弹道运动；而恒定电荷条件下吸附呈次扩散行为且出现延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 聚电解质修饰纳米颗粒的表面特性及刺激响应行为在先进诊断等实际应用中具有重要意义，但电荷调节机制在PE-NP吸附动力学中的关键作用尚未被充分阐明。

Method: 采用混合CR蒙特卡洛/分子动力学（hybrid CR Monte Carlo/molecular dynamics）模拟方法，系统研究盐浓度、聚合物链长对聚电解质与非同种电荷纳米颗粒相互作用的影响，并与恒定电荷（constant charge, CC）条件下的分子模拟进行对照。

Result: 高盐浓度下CR增强了PE在NP表面的吸附并使PE回转半径迅速减小，而CC条件下PE呈伸展构象；低/高盐浓度下PE链长均无明显效应，但CC模拟中短链PE吸附弛豫更快；CR条件下PE在吸附中的均方位移呈弹道运动，而CC条件下呈次扩散行为且吸附延迟。

Conclusion: 可调电荷调控（tunable CR）是控制纳米颗粒稳定性及其在复杂生化环境相互作用的稳健策略。

Abstract: Customizing the surface characteristics and stimuli-responsive behavior of nanoparticles with polyelectrolytes ushers in a new era across many aspects of our lives, ranging from advanced diagnostics to practical applications. Here, using hybrid CR Monte Carlo/ molecular dynamics simulations, we investigate how charge regulation can play a crucial role in shaping the adsorption dynamics of polyelectrolyte (PE) on oppositely charged nanoparticle (NP). We systematically investigate the influence of salt density, and polymer chain length on the PE-NP interaction. To complement CR results, we also perform molecular simulations under constant charge conditions. At high salt concentrations, CR enhances the adsorption of PE onto the NP surface, leading to a rapid decrease in the radius of gyration of PE; conversely, CC promotes the extended conformation of PE. No clear effect of PE length is observed at either low or high salt concentrations, whereas in CC simulations, the PE relaxes faster on the NP in the case of short chains. Furthermore, By comparing these results, we demonstrate that the MSD of PE follows a more direct path during adsorption implying a ballistic motion, whereas in the CC case, it exhibits subdiffusive behavior and delayed adsorption in both low and high salt density. Our findings indicates that 'tunable CR' is a robust strategy for controlling nanoparticle stability and interaction within a complex biochemical cues.

</details>


### [92] [Patterned Substrates Unlock Self-Electrophoretic Phenomenon in Active Janus Microswimmers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03605)
*Jyoti Sharma,Yashpal Singh Brar,Omar Tricinci,Paola Parlanti,Mauro Gemmi,Stefano Palagi*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本文提出一种图案化基底（patterned substrate）方法，通过球形凹槽控制硅胶微球上铂（Pt）镀层的厚度分布，从而制造表面推进性能可调、缺陷（Pt桥）被抑制的Janus活性粒子。在2.5%过氧化氢（H2O2）中，该方法显著提升推进效率，并为自电泳（self-electrophoresis）为主导推动机制提供了间接证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 惰性胶体半球镀铂是化学驱动活性粒子的标准模型，但其在过氧化氢中的推进微观起源难以通过实验剖析；先前占主导的自扩散泳（self-diffusiophoresis）理论正被自电泳假说挑战。现有标准镀膜方法（如蒸发或溅射）无法实现对铂厚度分布的精确控制，从而限制了推进性能的优化。

Method: 将硅胶微球组装在含球形凹槽的图案化基底上，通过凹槽深度与间距调节粒子镀层程度，同时抑制邻近效应引发的铂桥形缺陷；利用FIB截面验证镀层厚度对比度。

Result: 所得Janus活性粒子展现出可调的铂厚度对比，在2.5% H2O2中推进效率显著提升。结果间接支持自电泳为粒子推进主导机制而非传统自扩散泳假说。

Conclusion: 所提出的图案化基底路线可实现对催化剂沉积的更好控制，支持新型Janus形貌的制造，从而为活性胶体的精确工程化提供更有效的途径。

Abstract: Inert colloids half-coated with platinum (Pt) are a standard model of chemically powered active particles, yet the microscopic origins of their propulsion in hydrogen peroxide (H2O2) remain difficult to dissect experimentally. Whereas self-diffusiophoresis was the prevailing theory, self-electrophoresis has been more recently suggested as the main mechanism of propulsion. According to the latter mechanism, the pole-to-equator Pt-thickness gradient produced by directional metal deposition is sufficient to create anodic and cathodic regions on the metal cap and thereby generate an electric field sustained by H2O2 decomposition. Enhancing self-propulsion performance of such particles thus requires precise control over the Pt thickness distribution, which is currently not achievable with standard methods (e.g. evaporation or sputtering). Here, we propose a method to fabricate Janus active particles by assembling silica microspheres on patterned substrates containing spherical grooves whose depth and spacing set the degree of particle coating while simultaneously suppressing proximity-led defects (Pt bridges). The resulting particles exhibit a tunable platinum-thickness contrast, as verified by Focused-Ion-Beam cross-sections. In 2.5% H2O2, our results suggest that this control can significantly increase propulsion efficiency, while providing evidence indirectly supporting the hypothesis that self-electrophoresis is the dominant mechanism. These results demonstrate that our patterned-substrate route can enhance control over the catalyst deposition and enable novel Janus morphologies, allowing for more precise engineering of active colloids.

</details>


### [93] [Pseudospectral Methods and Critical Phenomena](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03627)
*Jake Skelton,Joseph Brader,Salomée Tschopp,Benjamin Goddard*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本文利用伪谱方法（pseudospectral methods）在临界点附近求解均匀Ornstein-Zernike（OZ）方程，并采用平均球近似（Mean-Spherical Approximation, MSA）作为闭合关系，获得了硬核Yukawa粒子系统在二维和三维中的临界指数η、δ和γ。三维结果与已知解析解一致，二维临界指数为该工作的新贡献。文中还改进了Picard迭代方案，提高了收敛稳定性与速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临界点附近，流体关联长度发散，使得基于液相积分方程理论（liquid-state integral equation theories）的数值求解在有限网格上极度困难；本文旨在发展一种能够在无限域上快速高精度求解OZ方程的方法，以数值获得临界指数。

Method: 采用伪谱方法在均匀OZ方程上结合MSA闭合关系进行数值求解；针对处于高度关联态的临界点附近系统，实现真正无限域上的计算。此外，分析标准Picard迭代方案并提出改进版本，以增强数值稳定性并加快收敛。

Result: 对于硬核Yukawa粒子，在三维情形下数值得到的MSA临界指数与已知解析解一致，验证了方法的正确性；在二维情形下获得了新的临界指数估计。改进的Picard迭代方案在稳定性与收敛速度上均有提升。

Conclusion: 伪谱方法可快速且高精度地求解液相积分方程理论，尤其适用于临界点附近高度关联状态下的无限域计算；该方法有望推广至其他闭合关系及其他流体模型的临界行为研究。

Abstract: We employ pseudospectral methods to solve the homogeneous Ornstein-Zernike (OZ) equation for a model fluid in the vicinity of the critical point. Focusing on the Mean-Spherical Approximation (MSA) as a closure to the OZ equation, we obtain numerical estimates for the critical exponents $η$, $δ$ and $γ$ for a system of hard-core Yukawa particles both in two and three dimensions. The three-dimensional MSA exponents are already well-known from an analytic solution and are recovered by our numerical methods. The two-dimensional exponents are a new output of this work. The pseudospectral method allows for rapid and highly accurate solution of liquid-state integral equation theories, and enables calculations on truely infinite domains, as needed for highly correlated states. In addition, we analyse the standard Picard iteration scheme and propose a variation of it which provides increased stability and speed of convergence.

</details>


### [94] [Simultaneous measurements of enzyme propagation and biopolymer mobility elucidate the directional degradation of a dense biopolymer matrix](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03792)
*Vincenzo Ruzzi,Antoine Bouchoux,Carole Antoine-Assor,Donna-Joe Bigot,Salma Menzeh,Maike Petermann,Laurent Leclercq,Hervé Cottet,Cédric Montanier,Claire Dumon,Luca Cipelletti,Laurence Ramos*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本文通过一个模型系统，以时空分辨率同时追踪单方向酶扩散与生物聚合物降解过程，揭示了酶扩散与催化活性解耦、降解前沿推进由酶扩散、反应动力学与缓慢酶失活共同决定的规律，建立了可指导生物医学、生物质利用和纳米技术中酶学过程优化的定量框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物聚合物的酶降解在生物技术应用及消化、癌症发展等自然过程中至关重要，但致密底物中酶传播与底物降解之间的相互作用尚未被阐明，尤其不清楚致密基质降解究竟促进还是阻碍酶传播。

Method: 在模型系统中，以时空分辨率同时追踪单方向（unidirectional）酶扩散与生物聚合物降解过程，并对酶扩散、催化活性及降解前沿推进进行同步观测与分析。

Result: 实验表明，酶扩散与催化活性是解耦（decoupled）的；降解前沿（degradation front）的推进由酶扩散、反应动力学（reaction kinetics）以及缓慢的酶失活（enzyme deactivation）共同决定。

Conclusion: 这些发现建立了一个定量框架，可用于优化生物医学、生物质增值（biomass valorization）和纳米技术等领域中的酶学过程。

Abstract: Enzymatic degradation of biopolymers underpins critical processes in biotechnological applications and natural processes, such as food digestion and cancer development, yet the interplay between enzyme propagation in a dense substrate and substrate degradation remains unresolved. It remains especially unclear whether degradation of a dense matrix facilitates or impedes enzyme propagation. We simultaneously track with spatiotemporal resolution unidirectional enzyme diffusion and biopolymer degradation in a model system. We demonstrate that enzyme diffusion is decoupled from catalytic activity, while the degradation front progression is dictated by enzyme diffusion, reaction kinetics and slow enzyme deactivation. These findings establish a quantitative framework to optimize enzymatic processes for applications in biomedicine, biomass valorization, and nanotechnology.

</details>


### [95] [Peeling threshold for removal of an adhered elastic sheet by a shear flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03798)
*Hugo Perrin,Lorenzo Botto*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本文通过宏观尺度的剪切流剥离实验，研究了薄弹性片从基底剥离的临界剪切速率，发现其量级约为 $\dot\gamma \sim B/(ηL^3)$ 且意外地不依赖于粘附能（adhesion energy）$Γ$；借助格里菲斯断裂理论（Griffith's fracture theory）对实验数据进行分析，解释了这一与粘附无关的剥离阈值，并为二维材料（如石墨烯）的大规模生产提供了定量公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流体剪切诱导薄弹性片从基底剥离的过程在众多环境与技术系统中具有重要意义，然而支配该剥离过程的定量规律尚不明确，研究者希望揭示剥离临界条件与剪切率、流体黏度、片长、弯曲刚度和粘附能等参数之间的关系。

Method: 在中等粘附（intermediate adhesion）条件下，采用宏观弹性片粘附在基底上并置于含黏性流体的剪切流池（shear cell）中进行实验；利用光学测量获取薄片形状数据，在自由移动剥离前沿或夹持边界条件下，将格里菲斯断裂理论应用于这些数据以计算应变能释放率（strain energy release rate）。

Result: 实验给出的剥离临界剪切速率约为 $\dot\gamma \sim B/(ηL^3)$，该阈值意外地与粘附能 $Γ$ 无关；在临界条件下薄片曲率很大，导致应变能释放率在该阈值处几乎发散，这一近似发散使剥离阈值对 $Γ$ 的依赖至多非常微弱。

Conclusion: 实验结果证实了近期由Salussolia等人提出的理论（J. Mech. Phys. Solids, 2020, 134），表明剥离阈值在关键参数范围内本质上由弹性弯曲主导而非粘附决定；该研究提供的定量公式可用于二维材料（如石墨烯）的规模化生产。

Abstract: Fluid shear can induce detachment of a thin elastic sheet adhered to a flat substrate. This peeling process is important in a variety of environmental and technological systems. The condition for peeling depends on: the shear rate $\dotγ$, the fluid viscosity $η$, the length of the detached portion of the sheet $L$, the bending rigidity $B$ and the adhesion energy $Γ$. What are the laws governing the detachment? We address this question experimentally in the regime of intermediate adhesion, using macroscopic sheets bonded to a substrate and immersed in a shear cell containing a viscous fluid. The experiments indicate a critical shear rate for peeling of the order of $\dotγ \sim B/(ηL^3)$. This threshold is, unexpectedly, independent of adhesion. We rationalise this result by applying Griffith's fracture theory to optical measurement data of the shape of the sheet, under conditions of freely moving peeling front or clamped boundary. The results indicate that the large curvature of the sheet for $\dotγ \sim B/(ηL^3)$ yields a nearly diverging strain energy release rate at this threshold. This approximate divergence in turn yields a peeling threshold that depends at most weakly on $Γ$, confirming a theory that was proposed recently (Salussolia et al., J. Mech. Phys. Solids, 2020, 134). Among other applications, our work provides a quantitative formula that can aid the production at scale of 2D materials such as graphene.

</details>


### [96] [Tailoring Mechanical and Acoustic Properties of Liquid-Filled Elastomers as Reusable Ultrasound Couplants](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03835)
*Surojit Ranoo,Romain Fayolle,Jean Baudry,Nicolas Bremond*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于硅酮弹性体基质的复合材料设计，通过二醇和三醇的微胶囊化实现力学与声学性能的独立调控，开发出含高达75体积分数液体的弹性体复合耦合剂，兼具皮肤级柔软度和软组织级声学匹配性能，可用于可穿戴健康监测等生物医学及工业场合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有水/油基凝胶耦合剂声学性能优异但为一次性使用，不适合可穿戴健康监测、超声生物识别和长时间声动力疗法等新兴应用；而聚合物基固体耦合剂虽可重复使用，但难以同时满足有效力学共形性与高效声学传输的需求。

Method: 基于硅酮弹性体基质，通过二醇和三醇的微胶囊化实现力学与声学性能的独立调控；采用乳液材料配方策略，制备液体液滴含量高达75体积分数的弹性体复合材料，并建立可对力学和声学性能进行独立参数控制的稳健配方方法。

Result: 鉴定出含70%甘油的特定组分，其力学柔软度与皮肤相当、声学性能与软组织匹配；该材料已集成到功能性超声表征设备中，展现出结构均匀性；复合物对具有表面不规则性的刚性基底表现出良好的力学共形性，有望用于工业领域。

Conclusion: 所提出的高液体含量弹性体复合材料作为一种可重复使用的超声声学耦合剂，在生物医学与工业应用中均具有广阔前景。

Abstract: Couplants are indispensable for ultrasound-based applications to ensure seamless propagation of the acoustic waves between the transducer and targets. Existing hydro/oil-based gel couplants provide excellent acoustic coupling but are single-use and therefore not suitable for emerging applications such as wearable health monitoring, ultrasound-based biometric devices, and prolonged sonodynamic therapy. Alternatively, polymer-based solid couplants have been explored as reusable solutions but generally fail to simultaneously provide effective mechanical conformability and efficient acoustic transmission. Here, we propose an innovative composite material design based on a silicone elastomer matrix, in which mechanical and acoustic properties are tailored independently through the microencapsulation of diols and triols. An emulsion-based material formulation strategy is employed to produce a family of high-liquid droplet content elastomeric composites containing up to 75 volume fraction (%) of liquid. A robust material formulation strategy is established that enables independent parametric control over the resulting mechanical and acoustic properties. A specific composition containing 70 % glycerol has been identified and explored for biomedical applications, exhibiting mechanical softness comparable to that of skin and acoustic properties matching those of soft tissues. The material has been integrated into a functional ultrasound characterization device, demonstrating structural homogeneity and applicability of the proposed couplant. Furthermore, the composites exhibit mechanical conformability to stiff substrates with surface irregularities, making them also promising candidates for industrial applications.

</details>


### [97] [Boundary- and Screening-Induced Bubbly Phases in Autophoretic Active Matter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03991)
*Kingshuk Panja,Günther Turk,Rajesh Singh*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了自推动（self-propelled）活性粒子在空间约束与化学屏蔽作用下的非平衡相行为，发现新型边界诱导的气泡相（分为沸腾和爆裂气泡），并绘制了相图以揭示从宏观坍缩到气泡态、动态微团簇、再到均质气体的序列相变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空间约束与化学筛选能够从根本上重塑自泳（autophoretic）活性粒子的非平衡相行为，但此前对这些调控因素如何共同影响有序组装的理解有限，亟需系统性的映射研究。

Method: 研究覆盖三个控制参数——屏蔽参数κ、力矩强度μ_r及边界条件系数Λ^c，并采用稳态团簇分数⟨S⟩、时间波动幅度σ_S和配位数⟨Q⟩构成的三元度量组绘制相图，对比无通量边界（Λ^c=1）与化学可渗透界面（Λ^c=0）两种情况。

Result: 发现除标准趋化性宏观相分离与动态团簇之外，存在新型边界及屏蔽诱导的沸腾与爆裂两类气泡相；增大化学屏蔽会系统性抑制长程吸引，驱动从宏观坍缩经气泡态、动态微团簇到均质气体的序列相变，同时诱导聚集体形状各向异性。

Conclusion: 研究为微流体环境中活性组装与输运的可预测性设计提供了调控规则。

Abstract: Spatial confinement and chemical screening fundamentally reshape the non-equilibrium phase behavior of autophoretic active particles. Here, we present a systematic study mapping the collective dynamics of self-propelled particles governed by chemo-attractive translational forces ($μ_t < 0$) and chemo-repulsive rotational torques ($μ_r > 0$) across varying screening parameters $κ$, torque magnitudes $μ_r$, and boundary condition coefficients $Λ^c$. Beyond standard chemotactic macro-phase separation and dynamic clustering, we report the emergence of novel boundary- and screening-induced bubbly phases, classified into boiling and bursting bubbles. Using a metric triad of steady-state cluster fraction $\langle S \rangle$, temporal fluctuation magnitude $σ_S$, and coordination number $\langle Q \rangle$, we draw phase diagrams to demarcate phases for no-flux boundaries ($Λ^c = 1$) and chemically permeable interfaces ($Λ^c = 0$) . Increasing chemical screening ($κ$) systematically suppresses long-range attraction, driving sequential phase transitions from macro-scale collapse toward bubbly states, dynamic micro-clusters, and homogeneous gas phases, while simultaneously inducing aggregate shape anisotropy. These findings provide predictive design rules for controlling active assembly and transport in microfluidic environments.

</details>


### [98] [Designing corrugated surfaces to guide colloidal self-assembly](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04001)
*Dinesh Kumar Sahu,Jude Ann Vishnu,Lisa Shafroth,Martin Lenz,Olivia du Roure,Julien Heuvingh*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本文研究了三维打印平面多边形胶体粒子（flat polygonal colloids）在耗散力诱导下的选择性自组装，通过二维光子聚合精确设计纳米尺度表面形貌，实现从二聚体、链状、锯齿状到蜂窝状的可控聚集体结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 胶体粒子自组装是构建结构化软材料的重要途径，但如何以可逆且可扩展的方式精确控制相互作用特异性和聚集体形态仍是一个重大挑战。

Method: 通过双光子聚合（two-photon polymerization）进行精确建模，在三维打印的扁平多边形胶体上工程化各向异性侧面，操控表面形貌的长度尺度、耗散剂浓度（depletant concentration）及溶液离子强度（ionic strength）以调节相互作用的特异性与选择性。

Result: 不同表面构型分别形成二聚体、链状、锯齿状和蜂窝状结构；系统参数的变化可使体系从选择性聚集转向非选择性聚集；相互作用强度的调节可稳定特定结构基元，且实验、理论与模拟（以六边形胶体为例）三方结果一致。

Conclusion: 该研究建立了通过微结构设计编程胶体相互作用的通用框架，为制备可重构和功能性软材料提供了新途径。

Abstract: The self-assembly of colloidal particles enables the creation of structured materials with programmable functionalities; however, controlling interaction specificity and aggregate morphology in a reversible and scalable manner remains a major challenge. Here, we investigate the selective depletion-induced self-assembly of 3D-printed flat polygonal colloids, where nanoscale surface topography is engineered through precise modeling in two-photon polymerization. By designing anisotropic lateral surfaces, we direct specific interactions that govern aggregate morphology, yielding dimers, chains, zigzag, and honeycomb structures depending on the surface configuration. The specificity of interaction is tuned by varying the length scale of the topographic surfaces, the depletant concentration and the ionic strength of the solution, revealing a transition from selective to non-selective aggregation regimes. The relative placement of lateral interacting surfaces on the colloids enables assembly into aggregates spanning a broad range of sizes, while tuning the interaction strength selectively stabilizes distinct structural motifs. We demonstrate this interplay between geometric arrangement and interaction energy experimentally and corroborate through both theory and simulations for specifically hexagonal shaped colloids. This study establishes a versatile framework for programming colloidal interactions via micro-architectural design, offering new routes for fabricating reconfigurable and functional soft materials.

</details>


### [99] [Mechanistic Framework for Multicomponent Nanoparticle Assembly: Predicting RNA-lipid and PEI-DNA nanoparticle assembly](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04029)
*Turash Haque Pial,Tine Curk*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于预测多组分纳米颗粒性质分布的计算建模框架，通过将加工条件、早期自组装物理和分子化学细节与动力学蒙特卡洛（kinetic Monte Carlo, kMC）模拟耦合，可在单颗粒水平上解析尺寸-负载相关性、组成异质性及颗粒谱系历史。该框架已应用于mRNA脂质纳米颗粒和PEI-DNA聚电解质复合体系，并能解释对数正态体积与载荷分布的涌现机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多组分纳米颗粒的组装通常受动力学控制并表现出强烈的路径依赖，运输、溶剂交换、成核/生长及碰撞驱动的聚并共同决定了颗粒间的组成异质性；然而现有方法难以在单颗粒水平上预测这些多因素耦合导致的颗粒性质分布。因此有必要建立一种能够关联加工条件、自组装物理与分子化学细节的计算框架，以预测并调控单颗粒性质分布。

Method: 该框架包含两个主要部分：(i) 将混合条件与溶剂交换介导的颗粒初始化和生长相耦合；(ii) 采用动力学蒙特卡洛（kMC）模拟解析随机碰撞历史、静电控制的聚并以及单个颗粒水平的组成信息。框架以开源软件形式实现，命名为FormLNP。

Result: 在mRNA脂质纳米颗粒体系中，模型成功预测了尺寸-载荷相关性，并揭示加工依赖性组装路径如何导致异质载荷分布；kMC模拟提供的合并谱系历史解释了通过对数乘法颗粒生长路径涌现的对数正态体积和载荷分布。该框架同样应用于PEI-DNA聚电解质复合体系，获得了单颗粒分辨的DNA-PEI化学计量分布。

Conclusion: 该框架（及其开源实现FormLNP）提供了一种具备过程意识（process-aware）的途径，可用于在广泛的多组分纳米颗粒体系中预测和控制单颗粒性质分布。

Abstract: The assembly of multicomponent nanoparticles is often kinetically controlled and exhibits strong pathway dependence. Transport, solvent exchange, nucleation/growth, and collision-driven coalescence together determine not only ensemble-averaged properties but also particle-to-particle compositional heterogeneity. Here, we present a computational modeling framework for predicting nanoparticle property distributions by coupling processing conditions, early-stage self-assembly physics, and molecular chemical details with kinetic Monte Carlo (kMC) simulations. The framework combines (i) mixing conditions with solvent-exchange-mediated particle initialization and growth, and (ii) kMC simulations that resolve stochastic collision histories, electrostatics-controlled coalescence, and composition at the level of individual particles. Applied to mRNA lipid nanoparticles, the model predicts size-loading correlations and provides insight into how processing-dependent assembly pathways lead to heterogeneous payload distributions. The kMC simulations further provide merging lineage histories, which explain the emergence of log-normal volume and payload distributions through multiplicative particle-growth pathways. The same framework is also applied to PEI-DNA polyelectrolytic complexation, yielding single-particle-resolved DNA-PEI stoichiometry distributions. The framework and its open-source implementation, FormLNP, provide a process-aware route to predicting and controlling single-particle property distributions across a broad range of multicomponent nanoparticle systems.

</details>


### [100] [Topological Mixing and Braiding Universality in Polar Active Matter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04062)
*Wei Feng,Tianyu Ren,Zhihan Ye,Jonas Berx,Guangyin Jing*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本文通过将受限细菌悬浮液中稀疏的拉格朗日轨迹映射为(2+1)维几何辫子，引入有限时间辫子指数(FTBE)作为拓扑熵和混沌混合的代理量，揭示了从脉动气体到相干涡旋再到主动湍流的三种密度依赖的拓扑混合状态，并建立了FTBE与有效扩散系数之间的平方根标度关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将活性物质(active matter)的自组织运动学与其涌现的宏观输运联系起来，需要高分辨率的欧拉速度场，这构成了实际求解的约束条件。作者试图寻找无需直接测量全局速度场即可量化混沌混合的替代途径。

Method: 以受限细菌悬浮液为模型活性流体，将荧光示踪细胞的稀疏拉格朗日轨迹映射为(2+1)维几何辫子(geometric braids)，以编码流动的时空纠缠；采用有限时间辫子指数(FTBE)量化拓扑熵(topological entropy)与混沌混合。

Result: 发现三种密度依赖的拓扑混合态：稀薄活性气体、相干涡旋、主动湍流。在极端受限条件下系统趋近干活性物质极限，高密度下平面内立体碰撞抑制不可约纠缠，显著降低FTBE。并揭示了从离散几何碰撞区到面积逃逸机制的转变，建立了FTBE与有效扩散系数间的平方根标度关系。

Conclusion: 活性流体的自维持混合属于路径线辫子普适类(pathline braiding universality class)，为无需高分辨率速度场即可量化活性流体混合提供了拓扑学框架。

Abstract: Connecting the autonomous kinematics of active matter to its emergent macroscopic transport is constrained by the requirement for high-resolution Eulerian velocity fields. Here, we use confined bacterial suspensions as a model active fluid, mapping the sparse Lagrangian trajectories of fluorescent spy cells into (2+1)-dimensional geometric braids that directly encode the spatiotemporal entanglement of the flow. We use the finite-time braiding exponent (FTBE) as a proxy to quantify the topological entropy and chaotic mixing of confined bacterial suspensions. In moderately confined wet systems, we find that hydrodynamic coupling drives a structural transition from a dilute active gas to coherent vortices, and ultimately to active turbulence, revealing three distinct density-dependent regimes of topological mixing. Conversely, truncating the hydrodynamic screening length via extreme confinement drives the system toward a dry active matter limit. In this limit, dense in-plane steric collisions suppress irreducible entanglement and substantially reduce the FTBE at high particle densities. By evaluating the topological complexity generated per encounter, we reveal a transition from a discrete geometric encounter regime to an areal escape mechanism. Finally, we establish a square-root scaling between the FTBE and the effective diffusivity, placing the self-sustained mixing of active fluids into the pathline braiding universality class.

</details>


### [101] [Reciprocity can halve what a mechanical network can learn](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04169)
*Thai-Son Vu,Hoang-Giang Nguyen,Quoc-Bao Nguyen,Sengaloun Keoalounxay,Bao-Viet Tran*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本文揭示可调力学网络（tunable mechanical networks）中，Maxwell-Betti互易性（reciprocity）对被动物理网络的学习能力施加了根本限制：当p个自由度同时被驱动和读出时，所有可达响应块位于一个余维数为p(p-1)/2的子空间中，任何学习规则的最小误差均可预先计算，且非奇偶耦合的引入会因外部扭矩源需求使网络变为主动系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者指出已有研究通过计数可调参数与目标约束来衡量力学网络的学习容量，却忽略了被动的、线弹性网络天然遵守的Maxwell-Betti互易性对称性，导致容量估计存在系统性偏差。该对称性对给定图上有限可调刚度在部分重叠情形下的后果此前未被揭示。

Method: 论文从理论上证明当一个线性弹性无源网络中p个自由度同时被驱动和读出时，所有可达响应块必然位于余维数为p(p-1)/2的子空间内，与网络大小、拓扑及刚度均无关；推导出训练误差下界为共享自由度上目标反对称部分的范数。作者通过数值实验验证了该理论界限：采用精确雅可比（Jacobian）的二阶优化器在143/144次运行中达到1%以内误差，键局域对比学习规则在22/24次运行中达到0.1%以内误差。论文还对规定位移驱动给出伴随定律的证明，并将理论应用于一个已发表的机器人超材料（metamaterial）数据。

Result: 实验结果表明，全重叠时几乎一半的目标空间不可达；任意学习规则的最小误差为目标反对称部分在共享自由度上的范数，正确性（positive definiteness）约束还会附加一项正交项。对已发表的机器人超材料分析显示，其沉积数据所积分的线性模型中，不存在对称正定刚度矩阵能同时满足两个目标。

Conclusion: 该对称性是经典结果，但其对固定图上有限个可调刚度在部分重叠情形下的具体影响是新的理论贡献。非奇偶耦合可以恢复丢失的方向，但代价是需要外部扭矩源——即网络从被动转变为主动。这为被动可调力学网络的学习能力提供了本质上界，并对主动网络的必要性给出了理论依据。

Abstract: Tunable mechanical networks are being developed as materials that learn in place. Capacity is estimated by counting tunable parameters against target constraints, ignoring Maxwell-Betti reciprocity, the symmetry every passive, linear elastic network obeys by construction. When p degrees of freedom are both driven and read out, every reachable response block lies in a subspace of codimension p(p-1)/2, whatever the size, topology and stiffnesses; at full overlap nearly half the target space is unreachable. The consequence for training is a number: any learning rule leaves an error at least the norm of the target's antisymmetric part on the shared degrees of freedom, computable before training, and positive definiteness adds an orthogonal second term. A second-order optimiser with the exact Jacobian reaches that floor within 1% in 143 of 144 runs, a bond-local contrastive rule within 0.1% in 22 of 24, and odd couplings restore the lost directions at the price of an external torque source: the network becomes active. Prescribed-displacement drives obey a companion law we prove. The symmetry is classical; its consequence on a fixed graph with finitely many tunable stiffnesses at partial overlap is new. For a published robotic metamaterial it shows that no symmetric positive-definite stiffness matrix meets both targets in the linear model their deposited data integrate.

</details>

### [102] [Optimisation of importance sampling implementation in rare-event shielding simulations using Geant4](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03822)
*P. Knights,L. J. Milligan,K. Nikolopoulos*

Main category: physics.ins-det

TL;DR: 本文针对稀有事件搜寻实验中背景模拟计算开销高的问题，提出了一种优化重要性分裂与俄罗斯轮盘赌（importance splitting and Russian roulette）技术中层厚度设置的方法。通过平衡相对不确定性与执行时间，研究发现最优层厚度可表示为初级粒子平均自由程（mean free path）的倍数，该倍数对给定粒子类型可跨能量通用，并能带来约20倍的计算效率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀有事件搜寻实验对极低本底有严格要求，由此产生的超纯材料和抑制性屏蔽体使得相关背景模拟在缺乏事件偏置（event biasing）时计算成本极高，亟需高效的方差缩减方法。

Method: 提出一种针对广泛使用的"重要性分裂与俄罗斯轮赌"技术的优化方法，采用整数个等宽度重要性层填充屏蔽几何结构，通过平衡相对不确定性与执行时间来确定层厚，并针对γ射线和中子以及不同屏蔽材料与厚度进行优化。

Result: 最优层厚度可表达为初级粒子平均自由程的倍数，该表达对给定粒子类型可跨能量泛化，从而为任意屏蔽设计提供基于物理学的预测。研究同时揭示过度偏置（over-biasing）的可能性：在固定执行时间下，高层数会导致相对不确定性恶化。最优配置相较无偏置情形可带来约O(20)倍的计算效率增益。

Conclusion: 该优化方法提供了一种基于物理机制的、可推广的层厚选择准则，能够有效提升屏蔽体背景模拟的计算效率，且其关于过度偏置的发现为后续设计提供了重要警示。

Abstract: Rare-event search experiments demand ever-lower backgrounds, and the resulting radio-pure materials and suppressive shielding make related background simulations computationally expensive without event biasing. We present a method for optimising the widely used importance splitting and Russian roulette technique for rare-event searches, in which a whole number of equal-width importance layers fill the shielding geometry. Balancing relative uncertainty against execution time, we optimise the layer thickness for both $γ$-rays and neutrons, and across different shielding materials and thicknesses. Expressed as a multiple of the primary particle's mean free path, the optimal thickness generalises across energies for a given particle type and yields a physics-based prediction applicable to any shielding design. The same trend reveals that over-biasing is possible, with relative uncertainty worsening at high layer counts for fixed execution time. The optimal configuration gives an $\mathcal{O}$(20) gain in computational efficiency over the unbiased case.

</details>


### [103] [Testbeam characterization of a 3D silicon sensor read out by Timepix4](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04042)
*E. Chatzianagnostou,M. J. Madurai,K. Akiba,D. Bacher,R. Bates,M. van Beuzekom,T. Bischoff,V. Coco,R. Dumps,T. Evans,K. Heijhoff,D. Johnson,U. Krämer,E. Lemos Cid,D. Oppenhuis,T. Pajero,E. Rodríguez Rodríguez,D. Rolf*

Main category: physics.ins-det

TL;DR: 本文报告了300 μm厚3D硅传感器与Timepix4 ASIC键合后的测试束（testbeam）结果，系统研究了不同入射角度、偏压和电荷阈值下的探测效率、空间分辨率和时间性能。垂直入射时时间分辨率为245 ps、效率97%；在8°最优入射角下，时间分辨率提升6%，效率超过99%，空间分辨率约7 μm。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在评估3D硅传感器在粒子探测器中作为精确时间测量元件的性能，特别是其时间分辨率、空间分辨率与探测效率随实验条件的综合表现，以确定最优工作构型。

Method: 采用300 μm厚3D硅传感器与Timepix4读出芯片进行测试束实验；对时间测量进行了时钟频率变化与时间游走（timewalk）校正，对空间研究确定了非线性电荷共享（charge-sharing）校正，并在不同入射角度、偏压和电荷阈值下设多种工作条件展开测量。

Result: 垂直入射下时间分辨率为245 ps、探测效率为97%；在相对于束流方向8°的最优入射角下，时间分辨率改善6%，探测效率超过99%，空间分辨率约7 μm。像素内研究表明，在垂直入射时电极间命中点的时间分辨率可达153 ps（对应信号电荷最概然值），但在传感器旋转时该最优区域的时间性能退化；掠射角研究表明时间分辨率沿传感器深度存在依赖性。

Conclusion: 3D硅传感器在掠射角度下可同时实现亚纳秒时间分辨率与亚10 μm空间分辨率，其时间性能与电极位置和入射角度密切相关，最优工作角度约8°。

Abstract: Testbeam results from a $300\,μ\mathrm{m}$-thick 3D silicon sensor bump-bonded to a Timepix4 ASIC, are presented. The hit detection efficiency, spatial resolution, and timing performance of the 3D sensor are studied for several track angles, bias voltages and charge thresholds. The time measurements are corrected for Timepix4 clock-frequency variations and timewalk, while for the spatial studies, nonlinear charge-sharing corrections are determined. At perpendicular incidence, the time resolution of the 3D detector is equal to $245\,\mathrm{ps}$ with a $97\%$ hit detection efficiency. An optimal angle of $8^\circ$ with respect to the beam direction of the 3D detector was found, in which the time resolution improves by $6\%$ compared to normal incidence, while the hit detection efficiency reaches above $99\%$ and the spatial resolution is approximately $7\,μ\mathrm{m}$. Intrapixel studies show that a time resolution of $153\,\mathrm{ps}$ at the most probable value of the signal charge can be achieved for hits between the electrodes at normal incidence, while the timing performance of these best-performing regions deteriorate upon sensor rotation. The timing properties at different depths of the 3D sensor have been investigated with tracks at grazing-angle incidence, revealing a dependence of the time resolution along the sensor depth.

</details>


### [104] [Monitoring antiproton numbers with a CMOS detector in a dense-track environment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04078)
*C. Regenfus,P. Adrich,I. Belosevic,F. Benkel,M. Chung,P. Cladé,P. Comini,P. Crivelli,P. Debu,A. Douillet,S. Geffroy,S. Guellati-Khelifa,P. Guichard,P. -A. Hervieux,L. Hilico,P. Indelicato,S. Jonsell,J. -P. Karr,B. Kim,S. Kim,E. -S. Kim,N. Kuroda,B. Lee,L. Liszkay,D. Lunney,G. Manfredi,B. Mansoulié,V. Martimort,M. Matusiak,V. Nesvizhevsky,F. Nez,K. Park,N. Paul,E. Perez,P. Pérez,C. Roumegou,J. -Y. Roussé,F. Schmidt-Kaler,K. Szymczyk,T. A. Tanaka,B. Tuchming,D. -P. van der Werf,D. Won,S. Wronka,P. Yzombard*

Main category: physics.ins-det

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一种利用商用CMOS数字相机确定反质子数量的方法，该方法基于微通道板（microchannel plate）探测器表面上反质子与核子湮灭产生的电离粒子检测。通过簇（cluster）重建和蒙特卡洛模拟校正材料效应，绝对反质子数重建的系统误差约为10%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GBAR实验在AD/ELENA装置上生产反氢（antihydrogen），需要准确知道入射keV能量反质子的数量，但精确计数存在困难。因此需要一种可靠且具有较小系统不确定性的方法来确定反质子数目。

Method: 在实验真空腔周围安装商用CMOS数字相机，利用微通道板探测器表面的反质子-核子湮灭事件中产生的电离粒子进行探测。高粒度、小像素尺寸的CMOS像素探测器对最小电离粒子（minimal ionising particles）具有接近100%的探测效率，同时对背景伽马（gamma）不敏感。通过簇长度（cluster length）研究非垂直径迹来估算耗尽层厚度和有效探测面积，并结合对已知束流强度的标定测量覆盖立体角，利用Geant4蒙特卡洛计算估计材料效应对湮灭产物的影响。

Result: 湮灭产生的带电粒子多重性符合反质子与静止核子单个湮灭的预期，考虑周围材料预算后大部分粒子处于最小电离能区。该方法可在大动态范围内高密度的径迹环境中重建数以百万计的反质子湮灭事件，簇长度还可用于监测径迹角度。材料效应对湮灭产物的影响已通过Geant4蒙特卡洛计算估算，微通道板复杂表面上的湮灭伪影在该方法中被抵消，最终绝对反质子数重建的系统误差约为10%。

Conclusion: 该方法最大程度地减少了系统不确定性，使GBAR实验中绝对反质子数的重建误差控制在约10%范围内，为反氢生产提供了可靠的束流强度监测手段。

Abstract: The production of antihydrogen by the GBAR experiment at AD/ELENA requires good knowledge of the number of incident keV antiprotons, which can be problematic. We have used a commercial CMOS digital camera mounted around the experimental vacuum chamber to determine antiproton numbers from ionising particles created in the annihilation process on the surface of microchannel plate detectors which are used for beam imaging. We show that the multiplicity of emerging charged particles is as expected for individual annihilations of antiprotons with nucleons at rest, taking into account the surrounding material budget. Most of those particles are in the minimal ionising regime, but can be detected with nearly 100% efficiency in the CMOS pixel detector, while due to the thin depletion layer the device is insensitive to background gammas. Thanks to the high granularity and small pixel size millions of antiproton annihilations can be reconstructed in a dense tracking environment over a large dynamic range with good resolution. From cluster length studies of non perpendicular tracks the thickness of the depletion zone and effective detection area was estimated. The cluster length also allows for a monitoring of track angles. Antiproton numbers are determined from the number of reconstructed clusters in the CMOS sensor by means of the covered solid angle relative to a calibration measurements with well known beam intensities at the most upstream location of the GBAR apparatus. Material effects on the emerging annihilation products were estimated by Monte Carlo (Geant4) calculations, while annihilation artefacts on the complex surface of a microchannel plate are cancelled out in this approach. This method minimises largely systematic uncertainties, leading to a final error of roughly 10% for the reconstruction of absolute antiproton numbers.

</details>

### [105] [Buckling Prediction for Nonlinear Elastic Beams with Soft Inclusions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02956)
*Thien Tran-Duc,J. E. Bunder,A. J. Roberts*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出一种高效且精确的多尺度（multiscale）计算框架，用于预测含周期性软夹杂弹性梁的屈曲（buckling）及后屈曲（post-buckling）行为。该方法将此前针对线弹性问题的多尺度补片计算均匀化（patch computational homogenisation）方法扩展至非线性领域，仅需在少量稀疏子域（补片）中进行微尺度计算，并通过补片耦合算法恢复宏观行为。与全域仿真对比表明，该框架在显著降低计算成本的同时精确预测屈曲阈值与后屈曲响应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 含周期性软夹杂的弹性梁在压缩载荷下容易发生屈曲失稳，而精确预测其屈曲起始点和后屈曲行为对工程结构设计至关重要。然而，全域细观仿真成本高昂，已有的多尺度线弹性框架无法处理非线性屈曲问题，因此需要发展一种兼顾精度与效率的非线性多尺度计算方法。

Method: 研究在既有线性弹性多尺度补片计算均匀化框架的基础上引入非线性，形成扩展的计算框架；微观尺度计算仅在一组稀疏分布的补片子域内执行，通过经过验证的补片耦合算法恢复宏观响应。采用四分之一和半域补片计算进行评估，覆盖夹杂与基体杨氏模量比0.001至1的范围，并对雅可比矩阵（Jacobian matrix）进行特征值分析以预测失稳起始点及临界应变和临界应力。

Result: 结果表明，降低夹杂刚度会同时降低临界屈曲应变和临界屈曲应力，表明结构对失稳的敏感性增加，同时产生更温和的后屈曲响应，表现为更小的横向挠度和应力跌落。雅可比矩阵的特征值分析能准确预测失稳起始点及对应的临界应变与应力。研究还给出了压缩载荷下非线性屈曲构型的分岔图（bifurcation diagram），并定量分析了插值阶数对预测结果的影响；与全域仿真对比验证了框架的精度与计算成本优势。

Conclusion: 该多尺度计算框架能精确预测弹性梁的屈曲阈值与后屈曲行为，同时大幅降低计算成本；方法可方便地推广至非均匀梁、板、壳及其他工程结构。

Abstract: We develop an efficient and accurate multiscale computational framework for predicting the buckling and post-buckling behaviour of elastic beams containing periodically distributed soft inclusions. The framework extends our previous multiscale, patch, computational homogenisation for linear elasticity by incorporating nonlinearity, and thus enables accurate prediction of both the buckling onset and the subsequent post-buckling response. Microscale computations are performed only within a sparse set of small subdomains (patches), while the macroscale behaviour is recovered through a proven patch-coupling algorithm. The scheme is assessed through quarter- and half-domain patch computations for beams with inclusion-to-matrix Young's modulus ratios ranging from 0.001 to 1. The results show that reducing the inclusion stiffness lowers both the critical buckling strain and the critical buckling stress, indicating an increased susceptibility to instability, while producing a milder post-buckling response with smaller transverse deflections and stress drops. Eigenvalue analysis of the Jacobian matrix accurately predicts the onset of instability and the corresponding critical strain and stress. Bifurcation diagrams of the nonlinear buckled configurations under compressive loading, and a quantitative analysis of the effect of the interpolation order on the predicted buckling and post-buckling responses, are also presented. Comparisons with full-domain simulations demonstrate that the proposed framework accurately predicts both the buckling threshold and the post-buckling behaviour while substantially reducing the computational cost. The methodology is readily extendable to heterogeneous beams, plates, shells, and other engineering structures.

</details>

### [106] [Direct Validation of Superconductivity through Contact-Free Detection of Persistent Supercurrents Using Room-Temperature Quantum Magnetometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02995)
*Xinyi Zeng,Chengzhen Qin,Bowen Fan,Hammad Ahmed,Hui Fang,Stuart Long,Xiaonan Shan,Jiefu Chen,Shoujun Xu,Liangzi Deng,Ching W. Chu,Jiming Bao*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文提出一种室温、无接触的超导验证技术，利用无制冷剂的光泵原子磁力计检测残余超电流产生的温度依赖磁场，直接识别超导转变，实现对毫米级REBCO圆盘和亚100微米YBCO微晶体的超导性一步验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 新材料的加速涌现推动了对高温超导体的探索，但快速实验验证仍是关键瓶颈，尤其对于高压下微观样品，需要一种可扩展的高通量超导验证方法。

Method: 该技术利用无制冷剂的光泵原子磁力计（atomic magnetometer）检测超导体超电流产生的温度依赖磁场。超导转变通过转变温度以上残余超电流磁场的骤然消失以及磁场反向时超电流方向反转来直接识别；采用铁氧体磁通导引实现厘米级距离的灵敏检测。

Result: 该方法在YBCO微晶体和REBCO带材上得到验证，可检测毫米级REBCO方盘中由环境地磁场感应的超电流产生的皮特斯拉级磁场，以及适用于高压金刚石压砧装置的亚100微米YBCO微晶体。

Conclusion: 该技术无需电接触、磁线圈或集成磁传感器，作为无侵入式室温平台，为超导性的高通量筛选和验证提供了可扩展途径。

Abstract: The accelerated emergence of new materials is driving the search for high temperature superconductors, but rapid experimental validation remains a critical bottleneck, particularly for microscopic samples under high pressure. Here, we demonstrate one-step direct superconductivity validation through room-temperature, contact-free detection of remnant supercurrents. The technique utilizes a cryogen-free optically pumped atomic magnetometer to detect the temperature-dependent magnetic field produced by supercurrents of a superconductor. The superconducting transition is directly identified by the abrupt disappearance of magnetic field from the remnant supercurrent above the transition temperature and the reversal of the supercurrent direction upon reversing the applied magnetic field. Validated on YBCO microcrystals and REBCO tape, this technique detects pico-Tesla magnetic fields from supercurrents induced by the ambient Earth's magnetic field in a millimeter-sized REBCO square disk, as well as from sub-100 micrometer YBCO microcrystals compatible with high-pressure diamond anvil cells. The use of a ferrite flux guide enables sensitive detection from centimeter-scale distances. Requiring no electrical contacts, magnetic coils, or integrated magnetic sensors, this non-invasive, room-temperature platform offers a scalable approach for high-throughput screening and validation of superconductivity.

</details>


### [107] [Doping dependence of local moments in infinite layer nickelates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03388)
*Martin Gonzalez,Andreas Suter,Michal Kiaba,Thomas Prokscha,Zaher Salman,Marc Gabay,Harold Y. Hwang,Jennifer Fowlie*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本研究利用μ子自旋转动（muon spin rotation, μSR）技术系统测量了（La,Sr）NiO₂掺杂系列（Sr替代0%至25%）的磁性基态与温度依赖的静/动态行为，发现局域磁矩在所有掺杂水平下均在数十开尔文温度尺度冻结为玻璃态，且空穴掺杂增加会轻微削弱该玻璃态，表明磁性与超导大体上相互解耦，仅通过多轨道框架中的间接相互作用关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无限层镍酸盐（infinite layer nickelates）缺乏长程反铁磁有序（antiferromagnetic ordering），与超导铜氧化物母体形成鲜明对比，但此前尚未完全解析其随掺杂系统演化的磁性行为；表征该系统掺杂依赖的磁性变化可能揭示局域磁矩与超导穹顶（superconducting dome）之间的关系。

Method: 对（La,Sr）NiO₂掺杂系列（Sr替代浓度0% ≤ x ≤ 25%）实施μ子自旋转动（μSR）测量，覆盖从未掺杂母体、穿过超导穹顶直至过掺杂正常态的全掺杂窗口，以探测磁基态以及随温度变化的静态与动态行为。

Result: 研究发现，无论掺杂水平如何，局域磁矩均在数倍十开尔文的温度量级发生自旋冻结（spin freezing）并进入玻璃态（glassy state）；同时观察到随着空穴掺杂（hole doping）增加，玻璃态出现轻微的失稳效应。

Conclusion: 这些观察结果表明磁性与超导在很大程度上是解耦的（decoupled）现象，二者之间仅存在可描述于多轨道框架（multi-orbital framework）中的间接相互作用。

Abstract: The infinite layer nickelates are notable for their lack of long-range antiferromagnetic ordering, in contrast to the parent compounds of the superconducting cuprates. Instead, the nickelates show evidence of short-range glassy behavior in both the undoped and optimally-doped regimes, implying that local electronic moments exist independent of superconductivity. However, the systematic doping-dependent magnetic behavior is not yet fully resolved, and characterizing it could uncover the relationship between local moments and the superconducting dome. In this work, we use muon spin rotation ($μ$SR) on a (La,Sr)NiO$_2$ doping series from the undoped parent compound, through the superconducting dome, to the over-doped normal state (Sr substitution 0% $\leq$ x $\leq$ 25%) to probe the magnetic ground state and the temperature-dependent static and dynamic behavior. We find that local moments experience spin freezing into a glassy state at temperatures on the order of a few tens of kelvin regardless of the doping level. We also observe a subtle destabilization of the glassy state with increased hole doping. These observations suggest that magnetism and superconductivity are largely decoupled phenomena with indirect interactions described in a multi-orbital framework.

</details>


### [108] [Tunable Chern superconductivity of PtBi$_2$ in slab geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03867)
*Luca Ketmaier,Jeroen van den Brink,Ion Cosma Fulga*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 该论文研究拓扑超导体PtBi₂表面上i波能隙（i-wave gap）下的Majorana锥体（Majorana cones），在有限厚度平板几何中通过简化模型分析这些锥体的能隙化机制，并绘制出准二维极限下的相图，发现平凡（trivial）与非平凡（nontrivial）能隙区域共存。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期实验表明PtBi₂是一种Weyl半金属（Weyl semimetal），其表面在低温下成为具有i波能隙对称性的拓扑超导体，体态拥有12个Weyl锥（Weyl cones），每个表面额外承载6个Majorana锥。作者希望理解这些表面Majorana锥在有限厚度下的能隙化行为及其拓扑性质。

Method: 作者在平板几何（slab geometry）中构建简化模型，利用有限厚度使体态Weyl锥产生能隙，从而将表面Majorana锥保留为低能自由度。通过分析两种竞争机制——锥间杂化（inter-cone hybridization）打开平凡能隙与Zeeman场（Zeeman field）打开非平凡能隙——并结合手性Majorana边缘模（chiral Majorana edge modes）的波函数图像，对系统进行相图映射。

Result: 研究发现两种能隙化机制相互竞争：锥间杂化导致平凡能隙，而Zeeman场导致非平凡能隙。在非平凡区域中，手性Majorana边缘模连接被能隙化的锥体，其波函数分布与锥体的动量空间分布相关联。准二维极限下的相图同时包含平凡和非平凡能隙区域。

Conclusion: 该研究揭示了PtBi₂表面Majorana锥在有限厚度系统中受两种竞争机制调控的能隙化行为，展示了准二维极限下从拓扑非平凡到平凡的相变结构，为理解此类Weyl半金属表面拓扑超导电性的物理机制提供了理论框架。

Abstract: Recent experiments indicate that PtBi$_2$ is a Weyl semimetal whose surfaces become topological superconductors with i-wave gap symmetry at low temperature. The bulk hosts 12 Weyl cones while in the superconducting state each of the two surfaces hosts in addition 6 Majorana cones. We study a simplified model in a slab geometry, where the finite thickness gaps out the bulk Weyl cones and leaves the surface Majorana cones as the low-energy degrees of freedom. We identify two competing mechanisms that gap these cones: inter-cone hybridization opens a trivial gap, whereas a Zeeman field opens a nontrivial one. In the nontrivial regime, chiral Majorana edge modes connect the gapped cones. We determine their wavefunction profile and relate it to the momentum-space distribution of the cones. Mapping out the phase diagram, we find both trivial and nontrivial regions in the quasi-two-dimensional limit.

</details>


### [109] [A Superconducting Peierls Instability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04164)
*Pramodh Senarath Yapa,Joseph Maciejko,Frank Marsiglio,Annica M. Black-Schaffer*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文在二维超导体的边缘发现了一种与Peierls不稳定性类似的"超导Peierls不稳定性"机制：色散的Andreev束缚态（Andreev bound state, ABS）在±k_c处具有Bogoliubov费米点，连接波矢Q=2k_c处的增强准粒子响应耦合到配对涨落，产生超导Kohn反常（superconducting Kohn anomaly），配对模式软化并凝聚后生成边缘配对密度波（pair-density wave, PDW），使ABS打开能隙。该机制无需零能平带或拓扑保护边缘态，可广泛适用于非常规超导体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Peierls不稳定性是凝聚态物理中电子-声子相互作用将一维金属转变为具有新晶格周期性的绝缘态的基础机制。作者希望在超导体中寻找类似的类比机制，探讨自发平移对称性破缺能否在超导边界以全新方式实现。

Method: 作者以正方晶格扩展Hubbard模型为具体实例，其混合对称性s+d+ip态在有限边缘动量处具有零能穿越的色散ABS。通过自洽Bogoliubov–de Gennes（BdG）计算，验证了边缘PDW的形成及波矢由超导Kohn反常决定。

Result: 计算表明超导体的序参量在边缘发展出PDW，其波矢由超导Kohn反常设定，从而使ABS的零能穿越打开能隙。这一机制不依赖于广延的零能平带或拓扑保护的边缘态。

Conclusion: 作者提出了一种超导体中自发平移对称性破缺的新机制，即超导Peierls不稳定性。由于该机制要求条件较为宽松，可能广泛适用于非常规超导体。

Abstract: The Peierls instability is a foundational mechanism in condensed matter physics, showing how the electron--phonon interaction can transform a simple metal into an insulating state with a new lattice periodicity. In a one-dimensional (1D) metal the Peierls instability follows from the enhanced electronic response at wavevector $Q=2k_F$ (connecting the Fermi points) producing a Kohn anomaly: a softening of the phonon mode at the same wavevector. Condensation of this mode then generates the tell-tale Peierls periodic lattice distortion and charge-density wave (CDW) that gaps the electronic spectrum. Here we show an analogous instability at the edge of a two-dimensional (2D) superconductor, where a dispersing Andreev bound state (ABS) hosts Bogoliubov Fermi points at $\pm k_c$. The enhanced quasiparticle response at the connecting wavevector $Q=2k_c$ couples directly to pairing-fluctuations and produces a superconducting Kohn anomaly: a softening of a pairing mode at the same wavevector. Condensation of this mode then generates an edge pair-density wave (PDW) that gaps the ABS. We refer to this as a superconducting Peierls instability and identify the boundary quasiparticle structure that enables it. As a concrete realization, we consider a square-lattice extended Hubbard model whose mixed-symmetry $s+d+ip$ state hosts a dispersing ABS with zero-energy crossings at finite edge momenta. Using self-consistent Bogoliubov--de Gennes (BdG) calculations we show that the order parameter develops an edge PDW, with the wavevector set by the superconducting Kohn anomaly, that gaps the ABS crossings. Our results identify a novel mechanism for spontaneous translation-symmetry breaking in a superconductor. As this superconducting Peierls mechanism does not require an extensive zero-energy flat band or topologically protected edge states, it may apply broadly to unconventional superconductors.

</details>
