### [146] [Real-Time String Dynamics in $3+1$D Lattice Quantum Electrodynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04114)
*Helen Zwölfer,Giovanni Cataldi,Umberto Borla,Jad C. Halimeh*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文通过树张量网络（tree tensor networks）方法，首次实现了3+1维格点量子电动力学中弦断裂（string breaking）过程的实时量子模拟，揭示了强耦合下弦在同一性共振条件下断裂的动力学机制，并对非共振情况下的竞争通道进行了系统分类与定量刻画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解三维空间中的实时弦动力学，对将格点规范理论（lattice gauge theories, LGTs）的量子模拟与QED和QCD的实际物理维度相连接至关重要，因为在该维度下横向涨落和竞争性局域过程大量存在。

Method: 采用树张量网络（tree tensor networks）模拟带动力学物质的3+1维U(1)格点规范理论中电通量弦（electric flux strings）的猝灭动力学（quench dynamics）；并引入通道分辨的微扰论（channel-resolved perturbation theory）对动力学及其傅里叶谱进行定量复现。

Result: 在强耦合下，弦在质量与规范耦合的尖锐共振条件下共振断裂，将电能转化为屏蔽静态电荷的物质-反物质对；在非共振情况下，系统存在多种竞争通道——粒子对产生、弦形变与延伸以及通量环——其中粒子对产生与通量环具有广延性的多重性，即使在远离共振处也会消耗弦扇区。

Conclusion: 结果为即将到来的高维格点规范理论量子模拟器确立了诊断方法（diagnostics）与基准测试（benchmarks），促进了量子模拟与真实物理维度间的桥接。

Abstract: Understanding real-time string dynamics in three spatial dimensions is essential for connecting quantum simulations of lattice gauge theories (LGTs) to the physical dimensionality of QED and QCD, where transverse fluctuations and competing local processes proliferate. We present the first real-time simulations of string breaking in $3+1$D lattice quantum electrodynamics. Using tree tensor networks, we simulate the quench dynamics of electric flux strings in a $3\!+\!1$D U(1) LGT with dynamical matter. At strong coupling, the string breaks resonantly at a sharp resonance condition of mass and gauge couplings, converting electric energy into matter--antimatter pairs that screen the static charges. Off resonance, we classify all competing channels---pair production, string deformations and extensions, and flux loops---whose multiplicity, extensive for pair production and flux loops, depletes the string sector even far from resonance. A channel-resolved perturbation theory quantitatively reproduces these dynamics and their Fourier spectrum. Our results establish diagnostics and benchmarks for upcoming quantum simulators of higher-dimensional LGTs.

</details>


### [147] [Non-Abelian string melting and thermalization in an open lattice gauge theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04117)
*Erick Parra Verde,Giovanni Cataldi,Stefan Kühn,Giuseppe Magnifico,Jad C. Halimeh*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文通过张量网络求解1+1维SU(2) Yang-Mills格点规范理论（lattice gauge theory, LGT）在规范保持的Lindblad演化下与热标量环境耦合的耗散实时动力学，发现热介质会使色电荷退定域化并屏蔽通量从而熔化色通量弦，且在强退相干极限下可通过Schrieffer-Wolff展开将动力学映射为经典排斥过程并解析求得Liouvillian热化时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放系统的格点规范理论此前主要在阿贝尔（Abelian）框架下发展，尚不清楚非阿贝尔（non-Abelian）规范结构如何重塑耗散实时动力学，这一空白构成了本文的研究动机。

Method: 作者研究1+1维SU(2) Yang-Mills格点规范理论，将动态物质场与热标量环境通过规范保持的Lindblad演化耦合，并利用张量网络（tensor networks）对系统进行求解；在强退相干极限下采用Schrieffer-Wolff展开将动力学映射为经典排斥过程并解析求热化时间。

Result: 发现热介质通过退定域化色电荷和屏蔽通量来熔化弦；在共振条件下，这种耗散熔化与相干弦断裂竞争并延迟其发生。热化时间随环境耦合强度呈现非单调行为，弱耗散区域因环境辅助输运而下降，随后在量子Zeno（quantum Zeno）区域上升。非阿贝尔物质结构产生稳态中系统的介子偏置，且热化时间随温度降低而减少，这与阿贝尔Schwinger模型的趋势相反。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了开放非阿贝尔格点规范理论中热化与弦动力学的规范保持（gauge-preserving）研究框架，相关温度依赖趋势与夸克-胶子等离子体（quark-gluon plasma）的pNRQCD研究定性一致。

Abstract: Open-system lattice gauge theory (LGT) has so far been developed predominantly in Abelian settings, leaving open how genuinely non-Abelian gauge structure reshapes dissipative real-time dynamics. Here, we study a $1+1$D SU(2) Yang--Mills LGT with dynamical matter coupled to a thermal scalar environment through a gauge-preserving Lindblad evolution, which we solve using tensor networks. Starting from a quark--antiquark pair connected by a chromoelectric flux string, we find that the thermal medium melts the string by delocalizing the color charges and screening the flux; on resonance, this dissipative melting competes with and delays coherent string breaking. The thermalization time is non-monotonic in the environment coupling, decreasing through environment-assisted transport at weak dissipation before increasing in a quantum-Zeno regime. In the strong-dephasing limit, a Schrieffer--Wolff expansion maps the dynamics to a classical exclusion process and yields the Liouvillian thermalization time analytically. Beyond these generic open-system effects, the non-Abelian matter structure produces a systematic mesonic bias in the steady state, while the thermalization time decreases with temperature, in contrast to the Abelian Schwinger model trend and in qualitative agreement with pNRQCD studies of the quark--gluon plasma. These results establish a gauge-preserving framework for thermalization and string dynamics in open non-Abelian lattice gauge theories.

</details>

### [148] [On the length conjecture for twist knots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02706)
*Samuel Panitch,Mauricio Romo*

Main category: math.GT

TL;DR: 本文利用新发展的三维量子迹映射（3d quantum trace map），为长度猜想（length conjecture）提供了更多证据——该猜想是文献中“全阶体积猜想”的精细化版本。作者特别证明了对于无穷族扭结（twist knots），长度猜想在一阶修正下成立，并进一步提出了三维量子迹映射在Dehn填充（Dehn filling）下具有自然行为的猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机是验证并深化长度猜想。该猜想是全阶体积猜想（all-order volume conjecture）的细化，涉及在结补空间（knot complement）的 skein 模（skein module）中插入额外链环的操作。作者希望通过新的三维量子迹映射工具获取更多实证支持，并探索该映射在拓扑操作下的行为。

Method: 采用新发展的三维量子迹映射作为核心工具，对结补空间中的 skein 模及其不变量进行计算与比较。作者通过验证无穷族扭结满足长度猜想的首阶条件来收集证据，并在此过程中提出了关于三维量子迹映射在Dehn填充下保持自然性的新猜想。

Result: 论文的主要结果是证明了长度猜想对于无穷家族扭结在首阶（first order）意义上成立，为这一猜想的整体可靠性提供了新的具体证据。此外，在论证过程中形成了关于三维量子迹映射与Dehn填充之间相容性的新猜想。

Conclusion: 本文的结果进一步支持了长度猜想的正确性，尤其是将其验证范围扩展到了一个无穷族。同时提出的新猜想指出了三维量子迹映射与Dehn填充之间值得探索的深层联系，为后续研究提供了新的方向。

Abstract: Using the newly developed $3$d quantum trace map, we compile more evidence for the length conjecture, a refinement of the all-order volume conjecture that incorporates insertions of additional links in the skein module of the knot complement. In particular, we prove that the length conjecture holds up to first order for an infinite family of twist knots. Along the way, we form a conjecture that the $3$d quantum trace map behaves naturally with respect to Dehn filling.

</details>

### [149] [Estimating the asymptotics of integer partitions in intermediate dimensions ($d = 3,4,5,6$)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03034)
*Avinandan Mondal*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本研究通过马尔可夫链蒙特卡洛方法（Markov chain Monte Carlo, MCMC）模拟至N=15000，数值估计了中间维度（d=3,4,5,6）整分拆的渐近行为，结果表明这些维度下整分拆的增长速度均严格大于MacMahon数对应的渐近系数，并给出了各维度的领先阶系数及部分次领先阶项的估计值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前Yeliussizov已证明维度d≥7的整分拆渐近增长速度严格快于MacMahon数，而d=1,2时MacMahon数与整分拆吻合。中间维度d=3,4,5,6下两者渐近行为的比较仍为开放问题，本文旨在填补该空白。

Method: 采用自适应权重学习（adaptive weight learning）配合常规马尔可夫链蒙特卡洛步骤进行数值模拟，模拟规模直至N=15000，以此估计中间维度整分拆的渐近增长常数。

Result: 数值结果表明，在中间维度d=3,4,5,6下，整分拆渐近增长速度均快于MacMahon数。具体估计值为：d=3时极限系数为1.8196±0.0019，d=4时为1.7215±0.0045，d=5时为1.6521±0.0059，d=6时为1.652±0.021（在假设极限存在的前提下）。这些数值均大于MacMahon数对应维度下的领先阶渐近系数1.7898、1.6614、1.5737与1.509。此外还获得了部分次领先阶渐近项估计。

Conclusion: 论文在数值上确立了中间维度d=3,4,5,6下整分拆的渐近增长均严格快于MacMahon数的结论，为将Yeliussizov在d≥7维度的结果推广至全部d≥3维度提供了数值证据；同时给出了次领先阶项的估计，为进一步的解析工作提供了参考。

Abstract: It was recently shown by Yeliussizov \cite{Yeliussizov} that integer partitions in dimensions $d \geq 7$ asymptotically grow strictly faster than MacMahon numbers. As MacMahon numbers match with integer partitions in dimensions $d = 1,2$, the comparison of asymptotics of integer partitions with MacMahon numbers in intermediate dimensions ($d = 3,4,5,6$) is an open question. In this work, we perform Markov chain Monte Carlo (MCMC) simulations till $N=15000$ by using adaptive weight learning followed by conventional MCMC steps to numerically estimate the asymptotics of integer partitions in these intermediate dimensions. We numerically establish that in these intermediate dimensions, partitions asymptotically grow faster than MacMahon numbers. More specifically, assuming that the limits exist, we show: $\lim_{n\to\infty}n^{-3/4}\log p_3(n) = 1.8196 \pm 0.0019$, $\lim_{n\to\infty}n^{-4/5}\log p_4(n) = 1.7215 \pm 0.0045$, $\lim_{n\to\infty}n^{-5/6}\log p_5(n) = 1.6521 \pm 0.0059$, and $\lim_{n\to\infty}\log n^{-6/7}p_6(n) = 1.652 \pm 0.021$ for partitions in dimensions $d=3,4,5,$ and $6$ respectively. These numbers are all larger than MacMahon leading order asymptotic coefficients of $1.7898, 1.6614, 1.5737,$ and $1.509$ respectively. Additionally, we also find estimates for some of the sub-leading asymptotic terms in $\log p_d(n)$ in each of the dimensions.

</details>


### [150] [Computational Algorithms for Invariant Reduction of Variational Forms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03197)
*Kostya Druzhkov,Alexey Shevyakov*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文开发了将偏微分方程（PDE）的对称约简显式计算的算法，利用变分p-形式作为增广系统——即（度平移的）切系统的守恒律这一解释，将守恒律、变分1-形式与前辛结构的约简统一为单一算法流程，并在多个示例方程上进行了验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 偏微分方程的对称约简相比其他类型的约简继承了更多的几何结构，如不变守恒律、变分结构以及在适当条件下的哈密顿型结构。然而，如何显式地执行此类约简计算缺乏系统的算法支持，因此本文旨在发展一套能够显式完成这类约简的计算算法。

Method: 本文将变分p-形式解释为增广系统（即度平移的切系统）的守恒律，其中微扰变量被视为反对易的，从而将守恒律、变分1-形式和前辛结构的约简统一为单一算法程序。文中给出了三种具体算法：(i) 基于同伦（homotopy）的约简算法，适用于演化方程组系统，已在Maple中实现；(ii) 适用于一般ℓ-正规系统中p>0情形的下降约简算法；(iii) 在适当条件下适用于点对称的简单约简算法。

Result: 算法示例涵盖一维和二维空间中的非线性演化方程、拉普拉斯方程（Laplace equation）、不可压缩欧拉方程（incompressible Euler equations）以及巴甫洛夫方程（Pavlov's equation）的切系统。

Conclusion: 通过将约简问题嵌入到分级交换（graded-commutative）框架中，本文使守恒律的约简、变分1-形式以及前辛结构的约简成为统一的算法流程，为PDE对称约简的显式计算提供了系统化工具。

Abstract: Symmetry reductions of partial differential equations (PDEs) inherit more geometric structures than other types of reductions: invariant conservation laws, variational structures, and, under suitable conditions, Hamiltonian-type structures of the original model descend to the reduced model through the mechanism of invariant reduction. This paper develops computational algorithms that carry out such reductions explicitly. We use the interpretation of variational $p$-forms as conservation laws of an enlarged system --- the (degree-shifted) tangent system --- consisting of the original equations together with their linearizations, in which the perturbation variables are treated as anticommuting. This allows us to formulate the algorithms using well-known concepts from the theory of conservation laws, naturally adapted to the graded-commutative setting. The reduction of conservation laws, variational $1$-forms, and presymplectic structures thereby becomes a single algorithmic procedure. We present (i) a homotopy-based reduction algorithm for systems of evolution equations, implemented in Maple; (ii) a descent reduction algorithm applicable to general $\ell$-normal systems for $p>0$; and (iii) a simple reduction algorithm available for point symmetries under suitable conditions, including the description of reductions in terms of systems involving fewer independent variables. Examples include nonlinear evolution equations in one and two spatial dimensions, the Laplace equation, the incompressible Euler equations, and the cotangent system of Pavlov's equation.

</details>


### [151] [Optimal bounds for embedded eigenvalues of one-dimensional discrete Schrödinger operators with decaying potentials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03224)
*Jifeng Chu,Wencai Liu,Kang Lyu*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究一维离散薛定谔算子（discrete Schrödinger operator）在具有自伴边界条件（self-adjoint boundary condition）的 ℓ²(ℕ) 空间上的嵌入特征值（embedded eigenvalue）问题，并确定扰动势 V(n) 的渐近系数所对应的存在与不存在之间的尖锐转变（sharp transition）条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究一维离散薛定谔算子中嵌入特征值的精确门槛（critical threshold）具有重要意义，但此前缺乏对渐近势系数控制嵌入特征值存在性的尖锐刻画，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 考虑形如 H = Δ + V 的一维离散薛定谔算子，定义在 ℓ²(ℕ) 上，并在 n=0 处施加自伴边界条件；势能 V(n) 为满足 V(n) = O(1)/(1+n) 的实值扰动，通过分析其渐近系数来研究嵌入特征值的判定阈值。

Result: 确定了势 V 的渐近系数在控制嵌入特征值存在与不存在之间的尖锐转变（sharp transition）条件。

Conclusion: 获得了嵌入特征值存在性与不存在性由扰动势渐近系数决定的精确临界界标，为离散薛定谔算子的谱性质提供了明确的判别依据。

Abstract: In this paper, we consider one-dimensional discrete Schrödinger operators \begin{align}
  Hu(n)=(Δ+V)u(n)\nonumber \end{align}
  on $\ell^2(\mathbb{N})$ with a self-adjoint boundary condition at $n=0$, where $Δ$ denotes the discrete Laplacian and $V(n)$ is a real-valued perturbation satisfying
  $$V(n)=\frac{O(1)}{1+n}.$$ We determine the sharp transition for the asymptotic coefficient of \(V\) governing the existence and nonexistence of embedded eigenvalues.

</details>


### [152] [Controllability, returning waves and scattering without reverberation in 3D acoustic dynamic system](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03536)
*Mikhail I. Belishev,Aleksei F. Vakulenko*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究三维空间中由波动方程 $u_{tt}-\Delta u+qu=0$ 控制的动态声学散射系统。系统在无限远源（controls）产生有限能量波，若哈密顿量 $H=-\Delta+q$ 存在束缚态（bound states），则系统在重新聚焦于某些点 $a$ 时丧失可控性（controllability），从而出现同时在过去与未来光锥中消失、且无混响地离开非均匀区域的有限能量波，该效应与波前反转（Time Reversing Mirror）有相似性但数学上更具意义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究具有紧支撑势函数 $q$ 的动态声学散射系统的可控性，并探讨哈密顿量存在束缚态时系统的物理与数学后果。

Method: 从波动方程和无限远源产生的入射球面波出发，通过分析与控制系统在时刻 $t=0$ 覆盖空间 $L_2(\mathbb{R}^3)$ 的条件，考察哈密顿量 $H=-\Delta+q$ 束缚态存在时的可控性丧失现象。

Result: 当哈密顿量存在束缚态时，存在某些聚焦点 $a$，系统在这些点上丧失可控性；由此产生有限能量的波，它们在过去与未来的光锥 $|x|<\pm t$ 中同时消失，并留下非均匀区域时不发生混响（reverberation）。

Conclusion: 该效应与波前反转（wavefront reversal）在物理上具有相似性，但从数学角度更具深层意义，为散射系统中束缚态与控制理论的交叉提供了新的理解。

Abstract: The dynamic acoustic scattering system is governed by the wave equation $u_{tt}-Δu+qu=0$ in $\Bbb R^3,\,\,\,-\infty<t<\infty$, with a compactly supported potential $q$ and infinitely distant sources (controls) $f$, which initiate incoming spherical waves $u=u^f(x,t)$ provided $u^f\big|_{|x|<-t,\,\,\,t<0}=0$. These waves are focused at $x=0$ and fill up the whole space at the moment $t=0$. The system is {\it controllable} if the set of waves $u^f(\cdot,0)$ produced by all finite energy controls $f$, covers the space $L_2(\Bbb R^3)$.
  As we show, if the Hamiltonian $H=-Δ+q$ has the bound states, then in the space the points $a$ appear such that the system, being refocused at $x=a$, loses controllability. The latter leads to a physical effect: the waves $u^f$ of finite energy appear, which vanish simultaneously in the past and future cones $|x|<\pm\, t$ and leave the region of inhomogeneity of $q$ without reverberation. This effect has some similarities with the wavefront reversal (Time Reversing Mirror), but is more meaningful from a mathematical point of view.

</details>


### [153] [The conductivity matrix at the topological phase transition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03832)
*Simone Fabbri,Giovanna Marcelli,Alessandro Giuliani*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文针对格点上一大类二维无相互作用半金属电子系统，利用欧几里得多体形式（Euclidean many-body formalism）推导出完整电导率矩阵的显式公式，揭示了纵向与横向电导率的不同决定因素及在涌现旋转对称下恢复的普适性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 半金属相（semi-metallic phase）出现在两个布洛赫带（Bloch bands）在费米能量处以圆锥交叉形式接触时，通常发生于不同整数量子霍尔相之间的转变点。理解该状态下电导率矩阵的完整行为及其普适性或非普适性特征，是物理上重要且未完全解决的问题。

Method: 采用欧几里得多体形式推导覆盖一大类不可相互作用半金属系统的完整电导率矩阵显式公式；随后建立费米点处线性化哈密顿量的涌现旋转对称（emergent rotational symmetry）假设，并在此条件下检验电导率矩阵的普适性恢复。

Result: 纵向电导率仅依赖于费米能级处圆锥的形状，而横向电导率同时由带的圆锥结构和附近两个拓扑相的性质决定。在半金属态中，一般情形下纵、横向电导率均不量子化为普适量子电导的整数倍；然而在涌现旋转对称假设下，电导率矩阵恢复普适性。利用该公式计算了量子霍尔流体在拓扑相变处多个物理模型的电导率矩阵，展示了横向电导率量子化为e²/h半整数倍的情形，以及其连续依赖于外部应变参数的情形。

Conclusion: 该研究揭示了半金属相中电导率矩阵普适性与非普适性的深层机制：圆锥结构与邻近拓扑相共同决定横向电导率，而涌现旋转对称是关键条件以恢复电导率矩阵的普适行为。

Abstract: For a general class of two-dimensional non-interacting semi-metallic electron systems on a lattice, we derive an explicit formula for the whole conductivity matrix, by using Euclidean many-body formalism. The semi-metallic phase takes place whenever two Bloch bands touch at the Fermi energy with conical intersections and generally occurs at the transition between distinct integer quantum Hall phases. Unlike the longitudinal conductivity (which depends solely on the shape of the cones at the Fermi level), the transverse conductivity is remarkably determined both by the conical structures of the bands and by the nature of the two nearby topological phases. Generically, in the semi-metallic state, neither the longitudinal nor the transverse conductivities are quantized in integer multiples of a universal conductivity quantum; however, universality of the conductivity matrix is restored under the assumption of emergent rotational symmetry of the linearized Hamiltonian at the Fermi points. Using our formula, we compute the conductivity matrix for several physically relevant models of quantum Hall fluids at the topological phase transition, exhibiting cases where the transverse conductivity is quantized in half-integer multiples of $e^2/h$ and cases where it depends continuously on an external strain parameter, thus shedding light on its universality or non-universality features.

</details>


### [154] [Classification of abstract Bose field models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03863)
*Yasumichi Matsuzawa*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文对量子场论中的一类抽象玻色场模型（abstract Bose field models）进行了幺正等价（unitary equivalence）下的分类，覆盖了抽象自由玻色场模型、抽象van Hove–Miyatake模型以及红外重整化（infrared-renormalized）的van Hove–Miyatake模型；作为应用的推论，进一步对二次相互作用模型进行分类，并解答了A. Arai关于红外重整化van Hove–Miyatake模型分类的公开问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子场论中存在多种结构相似的抽象玻色场模型，但此前缺乏统一的幺正等价分类框架。特别是A. Arai曾提出关于红外重整化van Hove–Miyatake模型分类的悬而未决问题。

Method: 本文方法建立在幺正等价的框架之上，对包含抽象自由玻色场模型、抽象van Hove–Miyatake模型及红外重整化van Hove–Miyatake模型在内的模型类进行系统性分类，并利用该分类结果延伸应用于二次相互作用模型的分类。

Result: 成功完成了上述抽象玻色场模型类在幺正等价意义上的分类，并将分类结果应用于二次相互作用模型（quadratic interaction models）的分类，从而解决了A. Arai提出的相关问题。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了一个统一的幺正等价分类框架，覆盖了包括红外重整化情形在内的多种抽象玻色场模型，并据此回答了量子场论分类中的一个公开问题。

Abstract: We classify a class of abstract Bose field models in quantum field theory up to unitary equivalence. The class includes abstract free Bose field models, abstract van Hove--Miyatake models, and infrared-renormalized van Hove--Miyatake models. Moreover, as an application of our classification, we classify quadratic interaction models. In particular, our results answer a question of A. Arai concerning the classification of infrared-renormalized van Hove--Miyatake models.

</details>


### [155] [Limits of Stochastic Semigroups and Block-Triangular Majorisation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04057)
*Fabio Deelan Cunden,Jakub Czartowski,Giovanni Gramegna,Marilena Ligabò*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究由不变分布参数化的随机矩阵（stochastic matrices）半群的极限行为，证明极限半群通常不同于保极限分布的半群，并针对逆温度β下的吉布斯分布（Gibbs vectors）情形给出完整刻画，引入分块三角优超（Block-Triangular majorisation）概念，证明其可由有限族单调量完整刻画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当概率向量依赖参数β并趋于无穷时，对应保分布的随机矩阵半群是否存在明确定义的极限，以及极限的代数结构如何，此前缺乏系统研究。作者希望厘清“先取极限再取保分布半群”与“先保分布再取极限”这两个操作何时不交换，进而建立一个通用框架。

Method: 引入依赖于参数β的保γ(β)分布随机矩阵半群在β→∞意义下的收敛概念，并在一般框架下研究极限半群结构；特别针对γ(β)为逆温度β下的吉布斯向量情形，将极限半群刻画为具有附加亚随机性（substochasticity）约束的分块上三角随机矩阵类，并对其极值元素进行表征与枚举。

Result: 证明极限半群由满足额外亚随机约束的分块上三角随机矩阵构成；得到其极值元素的完整刻画与计数；确定该半群作用所诱导的概率向量上的预序，即分块三角优超，它在普通优超（majorisation）与上三角优超之间插值；证明该优超关系可由有限族单调量完全刻画，并分析了Rényi α熵在β→∞时的相应行为。

Conclusion: 一般情形下极限半群与保极限分布的半群不同，表明两个操作不交换；通过吉布斯向量特例的系统分析，建立了分块三角优超这一介于普通优超与上三角优超之间的新结构，其可由有限个单调量决定，为相关熵与概率向量排序研究提供了新的理论工具。

Abstract: We investigate limits of semigroups of stochastic matrices defined by their invariant distribution. Given probability vectors $γ(β)$ depending on a parameter $β$, we introduce a notion of convergence as $β\to\infty$ for the corresponding semigroups of $γ(β)$-preserving stochastic matrices and investigate the structure of the resulting limit. In general, the limiting semigroup differs from the semigroup preserving the limiting distribution, showing that these two operations do not commute. We develop a general framework for such limiting semigroups and study in detail the case in which the invariant distributions are Gibbs vectors at the inverse temperature $β$. We show that the limiting semigroup consists of block-upper-triangular stochastic matrices subject to additional substochasticity constraints. We characterise and enumerate their extremal elements and determine the preorder on probability vectors induced by the action of the semigroup. The resulting notion of Block-Triangular majorisation interpolates between ordinary majorisation and upper triangular (aka unordered) majorisation. We show that it is completely characterised by a finite family of monotones and analyse the corresponding behaviour of Rényi $α$-entropies as $β\to\infty$.

</details>


### [156] [A simple derivation of the zero-temperature BCS functional from the reduced BCS Hamiltonian](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04060)
*Christian Hainzl,Riccardo Panza*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本研究考虑一个基于完整费米子福克空间（full fermionic Fock space）的巨正则系综约化BCS哈密顿量（grand-canonical reduced BCS Hamiltonian），其吸引配对相互作用（attractive pairing interaction）支撑在费米面（Fermi surface）周围的能量壳层内。作者证明在固定化学势（chemical potential）下，精确有限体积基态能量（finite-volume ground-state energy）与相应有限体积BCS泛函的最小值之差是一个与体积无关的误差界，从而表明BCS变分原理（variational principle）在热力学极限下对基态能量密度是精确的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BCS理论是描述超导性的核心框架，但其变分原理在有限体积下的精确性缺乏严格的数学证明。作者旨在严格建立BCS泛函变分法在热力学极限下对基态能量密度的精确性，为超导理论的数学基础提供严格论证。

Method: 论文考虑巨正则系综中的约化BCS哈密顿量，该哈密顿量定义于完整的费米子福克空间上，吸引配对相互作用被限制在费米面附近的能量壳层内。作者严格证明有限体积精确基态能量与有限体积BCS泛函极小值之间的差可由一个与体积无关的误差上界控制。

Result: 证明了固定化学势下，精确的有限体积基态能量与对应BCS泛函最小值之间的偏差被一个依赖于体积的误差所界住，从而在热力学极限（thermodynamic limit）下确立了BCS变分原理对基态能量密度的精确性。

Conclusion: 该结果表明BCS变分方法在热力学极限下对基态能量密度是严格精确的，为超导系统的变分研究提供了严格的数学基础。

Abstract: We consider a grand-canonical reduced BCS Hamiltonian on the full fermionic Fock space with an attractive pairing interaction supported in an energy shell around the Fermi surface. For fixed chemical potential, we prove that the exact finite-volume ground-state energy differs from the minimum of the corresponding finite-volume BCS functional by a volume-independent error. As a consequence, the BCS variational principle is exact for the ground-state energy density in the thermodynamic limit.

</details>


<div id='math.PR'></div>

# math.PR [[Back]](#toc)

### [157] [Airy limit for the Jack process and topological expansion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03532)
*Jiaming Xu*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 本文对所有 β>0 证明了 Jack–Plancherel 过程（Dyson 布朗运动的离散 β 类比）的多时间软边矩收敛，极限矩可由非负布朗桥配以等大小反向跳跃对的展开表示；在等时情形下，该极限在 β≥1 时与 Airy_β 过程的联合 Laplace 统计量一致，并由此推导出 β-拓扑展开及 Witten–Kontsevich 交点数公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究 Jack–Plancherel 过程的软边极限行为，旨在建立离散 β 模型中与随机矩阵/Dyson 布朗运动类比的多时间普适极限结构，并探索与 Airy_β 过程、拓扑组合量（如 Witten–Kontsevich 交点数）之间的深层联系。

Method: 作者对 Jack–Plancherel 过程建立多时间软边矩收敛定理，将极限矩展开为绝对收敛的布朗桥（Brownian bridge）项级数，其中每项对应成对等大反向跳跃的装饰；通过解析比较与等时约化，将极限矩与 Airy_β 过程的联合 Laplace 统计量对接，并利用同一展开推导出 β-拓扑展开形式。

Result: 对所有 β>0 证明了多时间软边矩收敛；在 β≥1 的等时情形下，该极限与 Airy_β 过程的联合 Laplace 统计量一致；布朗桥展开进一步导出了关于边缘 b-猜测的双曲渐近 β-拓扑展开，并在 β=2 时给出了 Witten–Kontsevich 交点数（Witten–Kontsevich intersection numbers）的显式非负公式。

Conclusion: Jack–Plancherel 过程的软边极限可统一地由布朗桥跳跃展开描述，且该框架在统计物理与枚举几何之间建立了新的桥梁；所得非负交集数公式提供了 Witten–Kontsevich 交点数的一种新的组合/概率解读。

Abstract: For every $β>0$, we establish multi-time soft-edge moment convergence for the Jack--Plancherel process, a discrete $β$-analogue of Dyson Brownian motion. The limiting moments admit an absolutely convergent expansion in terms of nonnegative Brownian bridges decorated by pairs of equal and opposite jumps. At equal times, we identify this limit with the joint Laplace statistics of the Airy$_β$ process for $β\geq1$. The same Brownian expansion yields an asymptotic $β$-topological expansion of the marginal $b$-conjecture and, at $β=2$, an explicit nonnegative formula for the Witten--Kontsevich intersection numbers.

</details>


### [158] [An Effective SPDE Model for a Schrödinger Equation with a Fluctuating Magnetic Potential](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03556)
*Franco Flandoli,Reika Fukuizumi,Francesco Grotto,Eliseo Luongo*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 本文研究带电粒子在随机涨落外磁场中的薛定谔方程（Schrödinger equation），建立了一个奇异摄动极限（singular perturbation limit），在该极限下解的收敛行为由确定性薛定谔方程及其高斯涨落（Gaussian fluctuations）描述。作者提出一个同时包含确定性平均值与涨落行为的随机薛定谔方程（stochastic Schrödinger equation），证明其与原模型具有相同的极限行为，同时为研究相关可观测量（observables）的分布提供了更简便的途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 带电粒子在随机磁场中的量子动力学在实际物理系统中广泛存在，但直接求解随机外磁场下薛定谔方程往往复杂且难以解析处理。作者的目标是寻找一个更简单的等效方程，既能还原原模型的极限行为，又便于研究相关物理量的统计分布。

Method: 论文针对带电粒子在随机涨落外磁场中的薛定谔方程，建立奇异摄动极限并证明解在该极限下收敛到确定性薛定谔方程加上高斯涨落描述。在此基础上构造一个随机薛定谔方程，使其同时刻画确定性平均效应与涨落特征，并通过数学分析验证其与原模型在极限行为上的一致性。

Result: 研究确定了奇异摄动极限下解到确定性薛定谔方程（伴随高斯涨落）的收敛性；提出的随机薛定谔方程与原模型具有相同的极限行为，且结构更简单，便于研究相关可观测量的分布。

Conclusion: 所提出的随机薛定谔方程可以作为原随机磁场模型中动力学行为的有效简化替代模型，在保持极限意义下等价性的同时，为研究可观测量分布提供了更便捷的理论框架。

Abstract: We consider the Schrödinger equation for a charged particle in a randomly fluctuating external magnetic field and establish a singular perturbation limit in which the solution converges to a deterministic Schrödinger equation with Gaussian fluctuations. We propose a stochastic Schrödinger equation incorporating the behavior of both the deterministic average and the fluctuations and show that it has the same limiting behavior as the original model, while providing a simpler way to study the distribution of relevant observables.

</details>


### [159] [Condensation and metastability in the supercritical two-species zero-range process via resolvent and $H^1$-approximation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03821)
*Seonwoo Kim,Seungchan Lee*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 本文研究双物种零程过程（two-species zero-range process）的凝聚（condensation）机制与动力学亚稳态（metastability）行为。主要结果证明，在加速时间尺度N^{1+α}下，单一凝聚体的位置演化遵循简单马尔可夫链；证明方法结合了近期发展的预解方法（resolvent approach）与H¹近似方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 吸引性粒子系统（attractive particle system）中的凝聚与亚稳态现象在多物种推广中尚缺乏系统性理论分析。本文旨在将经典零程过程（zero-range process）的凝聚结果推广至多物种情形，并为其亚稳态动力学提供严格的数学刻画。

Method: 将双物种零程过程中的凝聚区（condensation regime）与单物种情形直接类比分析；在证明技术上，创新性地整合了近期发展的预解方法和H¹近似方法（H¹-approximation method），以此对亚稳态行为进行严格刻画。

Result: 核心结果确立了单一凝聚体位置的动力学亚稳态行为：其运动在加速时间尺度N^{1+α}上服从一个简单马尔可夫链（Markov chain），其中N为系统总粒子数，参数α>1控制系统的吸引力强度。

Conclusion: 本文建立了双物种零程过程凝聚态的亚稳态动力学结果，并展示了结合预解方法与H¹近似方法的证明路径可行性，为更一般多物种系统亚稳态研究提供了方法论基础。

Abstract: In this article, we investigate the two-species zero-range process, a multi-species generalization of the classical zero-range process. First, we analyze its condensation regime, which directly parallels its single-species counterpart. As our main result, we establish the dynamical metastable behavior of the location of the single condensate, showing that its motion is governed by a simple Markov chain on the accelerated time scale $N^{1+α}$, where $N$ denotes the total number of particles in the system and the parameter $α>1$ governs the attractivity of the system. Another novelty of this work lies in the proof technique, which integrates the recently developed resolvent approach with the $H^{1}$-approximation method to rigorously characterize metastability.

</details>


### [160] [Beyond normal fluctuations in local laws for Wigner matrices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03924)
*László Erdős,Oleksii Kolupaiev*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 本文研究了大规模实对称或复厄米维格纳矩阵经验特征值分布的斯蒂尔切斯变换在中心极限定理中的非高斯修正，发现实情形下收敛速率显著慢于复情形，并开发了可任意阶分析的扩展累积量静态与动态层级方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 关于全局定律和局部定律的中心极限定理通常只考虑高斯极限，但需要对非高斯修正进行刻画，以更精确地理解维格纳矩阵特征值统计行为的收敛性质。

Method: 本文发展了扩展到任意阶的新静态（static）与动态（dynamic）扩展累积量（extended cumulants）层级，并通过之字形（zigzag）策略的精细化对这些层级进行分析，以推导全局定律和局部定律中斯蒂尔切斯变换（Stieltjes transform）的非高斯修正。

Result: 在实对称维格纳矩阵情形下，中心极限定理的收敛速率显著慢于复厄米情形，表明对于大的实对称性类（symmetric symmetry class），非高斯修正在CLT中具有更强的持久性效应。

Conclusion: 本文识别出维格纳矩阵全局和局部定律中心极限定理中的非高斯修正，并揭示了实与复对称性类之间收敛速率的本质性差异，为理解高维随机矩阵特征值统计的精细渐近行为提供了新的理论工具。

Abstract: We identify non-Gaussian corrections to the central limit theorem for the global and local laws, i.e. for Stieltjes transform of the empirical eigenvalue distribution of a large real symmetric or complex Hermitian Wigner matrix. We find that in the real case the rate of convergence in this CLT is substantially slower than in the complex case. For the proofs, we develop new static and dynamic hierarchies of extended cumulants up to any order that can be analyzed by a refinement of the zigzag strategy.

</details>


### [161] [The conformally invariant metric on CLE$_4$ I: subsequential limits of the non-simple CLE graph metric](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04138)
*Emmanuel Kammerer,Konstantinos Kavvadias,Jason Miller,Yi Tian*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 本文研究参数 κ=4 的共形环系综（CLE$_4$），证明其环唯一确定一个共形不变、局部的测地度量，该度量可作为 κ 从上方趋近 4 时 CLE$_κ$ 环上图形度量的重整化极限得到。子序列极限的存在性及非平凡性在第一篇论文中得到证明，极限度量的度量球增长与 Werner 和 Wu 的一致探索过程吻合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CLE$_4$ 处于临界参数值，其环为简单曲线且互不相交。此前 Sheffield、Watson 和 Wu 的未发表工作已构造了该度量，本文采用不同途径，旨在通过重整化极限的方式建立该度量的存在性与性质，并为后续证明真实极限的存在奠定基础。

Method: 本文将 CLE$_κ$ 环上的图形度量视为 κ↑4 时的近似，通过取重整化极限构造 CLE$_4$ 上的度量。文章证明子序列极限的存在性，并验证该极限满足共形不变性、局域性（locality）以及度量为非平凡的（non-trivial），同时证明从边界出发的度量球增长等价于 Werner–Wu 的一致探索过程。

Result: 文章证明了 CLE$_4$ 的环唯一确定一个共形不变、局部的非平凡度量，其度量球从域边界的增长与 Werner–Wu 的一致探索一致；子序列极限存在且定义了该度量，但真实极限的存在性留待后续工作证明。

Conclusion: 本文建立了一个与 Sheffield–Watson–Wu 度量一致但源自不同构造路径的 CLE$_4$ 度量，即通过 κ↓4 时 CLE$_κ$ 图形度量的重整化极限刻画其系统性来源。作为三篇系列论文的第一篇，后续将证明子序列极限即为真实极限，使该度量构造得以最终完成。

Abstract: We consider the conformal loop ensemble (CLE) with the parameter $κ=4$, the critical value at or below which the loops are simple and do not intersect each other or the domain boundary. We show that the loops of a CLE$_4$ uniquely determine a conformally invariant, local, and geodesic metric so that the metric ball growth from the domain boundary coincides with the uniform exploration of Werner and Wu. This metric was previously constructed in unpublished work of Sheffield, Watson, and Wu. Our approach differs in that we show that the metric arises as the renormalized limit of the graph metric on CLE$_κ$ loops as $κ\downarrow 4$. In this first paper in a series of three, we prove that the subsequential limits exist and define a non-trivial conformally invariant metric on CLE$_4$ which is local and such that the metric ball growth from the boundary is given by the uniform exploration of Werner and Wu. In subsequent work, we will show that the subsequential limit exists as a true limit.

</details>


### [162] [The conformally invariant metric on CLE$_4$ II: existence of geodesics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04139)
*Emmanuel Kammerer,Konstantinos Kavvadias,Jason Miller,Yi Tian*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 本文是系列论文的第二篇，研究参数为κ=4的共形环系综（CLE$_4$）的度量几何性质，证明CLE$_4$环之间存在测地线，并建立了包括矩形距离指数尾界和多尺度四臂SLE$_4$非相交界在内的尖锐定量估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该系列论文旨在证明CLE$_4$的环能唯一确定一个共形不变、局部且测地的度量，使得从区域边界出发的度量球增长与Werner和Wu的一致探索（uniform exploration）过程相吻合。本文作为系列第二部分，需要证明测地线的存在性及其支撑结构，为最终建立该度量的唯一性与完整性奠定基础。

Method: 本文采用分析方法证明CLE$_4$环之间的测地线存在性，并通过建立尖锐的定量估计来实现：包括矩形距离的指数尾界（exponential tail bounds）以及用于度量球的多尺度四臂SLE$_4$不相交界（multi-scale four-arm SLE$_4$ non-intersection bounds）。证明的关键在于说明任何测地线由CLE$_4$环支撑（除去一个Hausdorff维数为零的集合），并且不与区域边界相交。

Result: 本文证明了CLE$_4$中两环之间测地线的存在性，并表明该测地线在CLE$_4$环上支撑（除去Hausdorff维数为零的集合）且不与区域边界相交。此外，建立了CLE$_4$度量几何的尖锐定量估计，包括矩形距离的指数尾界和多尺度四臂SLE$_4$不相交界。

Conclusion: 通过证明测地线的存在性及其结构性质，本文为系列论文最终确立CLE$_4$环唯一确定一个满足所需性质的测地度量提供了关键环节。所建立的定量估计为该度量几何的深入研究提供了工具。

Abstract: We continue our study of the conformal loop ensemble (CLE) with parameter $κ=4$, the critical threshold at or below which the loops are simple and disjoint, touching neither each other nor the domain boundary. This paper is the second in a series of three establishing that the loops of a CLE$_4$ uniquely determine a conformally invariant, local, and geodesic metric such that the metric ball growth from the domain boundary coincides with the uniform exploration of Werner and Wu. In this second paper, we prove the existence of geodesics, showing that any geodesic between two loops is supported on the CLE$_4$ loops (off a set of Hausdorff dimension zero) and does not intersect the domain boundary. Along the way, we establish sharp quantitative estimates for the CLE$_4$ metric geometry, including exponential tail bounds for rectangle distances and multi-scale four-arm SLE$_4$ non-intersection bounds for metric balls.

</details>


### [163] [The conformally invariant metric on CLE$_4$ III: uniqueness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04140)
*Emmanuel Kammerer,Konstantinos Kavvadias,Jason Miller,Yi Tian*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 本文是构造共形环系（conformal loop ensemble, CLE）临界参数 κ=4 时共形不变环度量系列论文的第三篇（最终篇），证明该度量由其性质唯一刻画、其测地线亦然，且度量是 CLE₄ 的可测函数，从而无须取子序列即可保证重整化 CLE_κ 图度量在 κ↓4 时收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前两篇文章已通过子序列极限构造出 CLE₄ 环上共形不变的局部度量，但尚未解决唯一性以及是否可免于子序列取极限的问题。本文旨在确立该度量的唯一刻画，验证其测地线的唯一性，并证明度量是 CLE₄ 的可测函数，从而经整个序列收敛（而非子序列收敛）来断定极限度量的存在。

Method: 证明的关键步骤是表明该度量由每条环到域边界的测地线所唯一决定，而这些测地线又由 Werner 与 Wu 的一致探索（uniform exploration）过程决定。基于此测地线表示，作者证明度量的唯一性、测地线的唯一性以及度量对 CLE₄ 的可测性，最终推导出无须取子序列的全序列收敛结果。

Result: 本文证明该 CLE₄ 上的共形不变局部度量由其性质唯一刻画，其测地线亦被唯一确定；同时度量是 CLE₄ 的可测函数。作为结论，重整化 CLE_κ 环图度量在参数 κ 下降到 4 时沿整个序列收敛，无须再依赖子序列极限。

Conclusion: 该文完成了 κ=4 时 CLE 共形不变环度量构造的系列工作，确立了度量的唯一性与测地线刻画；文中提出的“由每条环到域边界的测地线决定度量”的表示将在后续研究中发挥重要作用。

Abstract: This paper is the third and final article in a series of papers constructing the canonical conformally invariant metric on the set of loops of the conformal loop ensemble (CLE) with critical parameter $κ=4$. The previous two articles construct, as a subsequential limit of the renormalized graph metric on the loops of CLE$_κ$ as $κ\downarrow 4$, a conformally invariant, local metric on the loops of a CLE$_4$ whose metric ball growth from the domain boundary coincides with the uniform exploration of Werner and Wu. In this paper, we establish that this metric is uniquely characterized by its properties, as are its geodesics, and that it is a measurable function of the CLE$_4$. In particular, we show that the renormalized CLE$_κ$ graph metric converges as $κ\downarrow 4$ without passing to a subsequence. A key step in the proof is to show that the metric is determined by the geodesics from each loop to the domain boundary, which are in turn determined by the uniform exploration; this representation will have important applications in future work.

</details>

### [164] [Experiments with membranes, maps and sheaves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03152)
*Daniel Holmes,Yannik Schuler*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文围绕M理论（M-theory）的猜想性存在，研究带有环面作用的Calabi-Yau五折叠（Calabi-Yau fivefold）上枚举不变量之间的对应关系网络，建立了五折叠Gromov-Witten不变量与三折叠的K理论Pandharipande-Thomas不变量之间的对应，并证明了特定情形的几何工程化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受M理论猜想性存在的启发，研究者旨在探索Calabi-Yau五折叠与低维流形之间枚举不变量对应的深层数学结构，以推动对M理论数学基础的理解。

Method: 该工作建立了五折叠Gromov-Witten不变量与三折叠X ⊂ Z的K理论Pandharipande-Thomas不变量之间的对应关系，通过推广Gopakumar-Vafa不变量的膜指标（membrane indices）作为中介；对于限制性环面作用下的条带几何（strip geometries）严格证明该对应，并对一般环面作用进行数值验证；还研究了闭顶点（closed vertex）、局部曲面及局部射影空间等几何的数值证据。当五折叠为仿射平面与合适的环面簇之积时，证明了几何工程化（geometric engineering），将相应的实例配分函数与Pandharipande-Thomas不变量生成级数等同。

Result: 对条带几何在限制环面作用下建立了严格对应；对一般环面作用及闭顶点、局部曲面、局部射影空间提供了数值验证；对后者给出了低度膜指标的猜想公式；在五折叠为仿射平面与环面簇之积的情形下证明了Pandharipande-Thomas不变量与实例配分函数的相等性，而Gromov-Witten级数的相等性则通过数值探测支持。

Conclusion: 该研究为M理论背景下五折叠枚举几何与低维不变量之间的联系提供了坚实的数学证据与猜想框架，为进一步严格化一般情形的对应关系奠定了基础。

Abstract: Motivated by the conjectural existence of M-theory, we investigate a web of correspondences between enumerative invariants of a Calabi-Yau fivefold $Z$ with a torus action. This includes a correspondence between the fivefold Gromov-Witten invariants and K-theoretic Pandharipande-Thomas invariants of a threefold $X \subset Z$, mediated by so-called membrane indices, which generalise Gopakumar-Vafa invariants. We establish the correspondence for strip geometries for restricted torus actions and test it numerically for general torus actions. Further numerical evidence is provided for the closed vertex, local surfaces and local projective spaces. For the latter we present conjectural formulae for their low-degree membrane indices. When the fivefold is the product of the affine plane with a suitable toric variety, we prove geometric engineering and equate the generating series of Pandharipande-Thomas invariants with the corresponding instanton partition function while equality with the Gromov-Witten series is probed numerically.

</details>

### [165] [Observational selection effects on radio pulsars are minimal for masses, but significant for orbits and spins](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03157)
*Lisa V. Drummond,Katerina Chatziioannou,Emmanuel Fonseca*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本研究通过模拟射电计时观测过程，构建选择函数（selection function）并将其纳入分层贝叶斯推断（hierarchical inference），从而修正观测选择效应对银河系射电脉冲星（radio pulsar）特性的影响，联合推断其本征质量、伴星质量、轨道偏心率、轨道周期和自转周期等参数的种群分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 河系射电脉冲星的质量分布呈双峰结构（约1.3和1.6倍太阳质量）且上限约2倍太阳质量，这有助于理解致密物质和天体物理形成过程。然而，观测到的射电脉冲星种群同时受到脉冲星内在属性和观测选择效应（如可探测性detectability与可测量性measurability）的影响，必须加以区分以提取本征分布。

Method: 作者模拟射电脉冲星系统及其计时观测过程，追踪特定系统进入观测数据集的全流程，评估双星系统和脉冲星参数的影响；将所得选择函数（selection function）纳入分层种群推断（hierarchical population inference），并联合推断脉冲星质量、伴星质量、轨道偏心率、轨道周期及脉冲星自转周期的本征种群分布。

Result: 选择效应对质量分布影响较小，但对其他参数影响显著：圆轨道与偏心轨道系统的比例由观测的1:1修正为本征的2:1；校正后的真实种群相比观测结果更偏向更长的轨道周期和自转周期。

Conclusion: 通过选择函数校正后，射电脉冲星种群的本征物理特性可被更准确地恢复，这对脉冲星质量-致密物质约束及双星演化研究具有重要意义。

Abstract: The masses of Galactic pulsars in binaries measured through radio timing point to structure in the mass distribution with implications for dense matter and astrophysical formation processes: a bimodal shape with peaks at ${\sim} 1.3\,M_\odot$ and ${\sim} 1.6\,M_\odot$, with a cutoff above ${\sim}2\,M_\odot$. However, this observed population of radio pulsars is shaped not only by intrinsic pulsar properties, such as birth masses, binary evolutionary pathways and dense-matter constraints, but also by observational effects. In existing catalogs of pulsar mass measurements, observational selection effects influence (i) which systems are detected in the first place (detectability) and (ii) which systems yield well-measured masses (measurability). In this work, we revisit the radio pulsar population distribution in the context of observational effects associated with pulsar radio timing. Simulating systems of radio pulsars and timing observations, we track the process by which a given system ends up in an observational dataset and estimate the impact of the binary and pulsar parameters. We fold this selection function into hierarchical population inference and extract the intrinsic astrophysical distribution of pulsars observed with radio timing. We jointly infer the populations of pulsar mass, companion mass, orbital eccentricity, orbital period and pulsar spin period. Selection effects have minimal impact on the mass distributions, but more significantly affect other parameters. The ratio of circular-to-eccentric systems shifts from an observed $1\!:\!1$ to an astrophysical, selection-corrected $2\!:\!1$ ratio, while the true population is shifted toward longer orbital and spin periods than observed.

</details>


### [166] [A catalogue of insights from the fourth LIGO-Virgo-KAGRA observing run](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03859)
*Christopher P L Berry,Daniel Williams*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文综述了LIGO-Virgo-KAGRA（LVK）合作组第四次观测运行（O4）期间引力波科学的重大进展，包括约100个新探测事件、多个显著单源发现以及基于群体分析（population-level analyses）对致密双星天体物理分布、广义相对论偏离和宇宙膨胀速率的重要约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 过去十年引力波科学已从单次探测即获诺贝尔奖的时代发展为几乎日常化的观测活动，LVK合作组旗舰科学成果在其探测目录中呈现。作者旨在系统梳理O4运行以来的新发现，展示该领域当前状态与未来发展方向，特别是强调群体级分析相对于单源发现对基础物理和宇宙学带来的更深远影响。

Method: 本文采用综述（review）方式，回顾LVK网络第四次观测运行（fourth observing run, O4）期间的探测结果，结合最新发布的探测目录，综合描述新发现的单个引力波信号（包括迄今探测到的最强信号及此前未见过的源类型），并以日益增长的样本量为基础进行群体统计分析，推断合并致密双星的天体物理分布、对广义相对论的可能偏离以及宇宙膨胀速率。

Result: 最近各版探测目录各包含约100个新探测事件；其中包含迄今探测到的最强信号和此前未见过的源类型等显著单源发现。群体层面分析已揭示多种源亚群体的存在证据，并对致密双星星体物理分布、引力理论检验和宇宙膨胀率测量提供了新约束。

Conclusion: 这些观测为理解大质量恒星演化和并合双星的形成提供了独特探针。随着更多观测的开展，对引力波信号及其源的认识将持续深化。O4最终完整目录预计于2026年12月发布，未来灵敏度更高的观测运行将带来更大的目录和更多强信号，继续推动引力波科学向前发展。

Abstract: Over the last decade, gravitational-wave science has developed from an era where a single detection was a Nobel-winning achievement to an (almost) everyday occurrence. The LIGO-Virgo-KAGRA (LVK) Collaboration's flagship scientific results are now their catalogues of detections, which include a comprehensive set of analysis results characterising the astrophysical properties of sources, and their implications for fundamental physics and cosmology. We review discoveries from the fourth observing run (O4) of the LVK network. The most recent catalogues each include $\sim100$ new detections. Among these new detections are remarkable single discoveries: the loudest signals detected so far, and sources unlike those seen before. However, significant advances in the field are coming from population-level analyses, leveraging the increasing sample size to infer constraints on the astrophysical distribution of merging compact binaries, potential deviations to general relativity, and the expansion rate of the Universe. Analyses of the latest data have revealed evidence for multiple subpopulations of sources. These observations provide a unique probe into the evolution of massive stars and the formation of coalescing binaries. Our understanding of gravitational-wave signals and their sources will continue to deepen with more observations. The final catalogue from O4}is due for release in December 2026, and future observing runs with even greater detector sensitivity are planned. These will bring even larger catalogues, and additional loud signals, continuing to advance gravitational-wave science.

</details>


### [167] [Sgr A* as a Galactic PeVatron: Multimessenger Signatures of the Magnetic Penrose Process](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04051)
*Marina Cermeño,Pedro De la Torre Luque,Cristina Fernández-Suárez,Viviana Gammaldi,Enrique Mier-Alonso,María J. Rodríguez,Miguel Á. Sánchez-Conde,Jaume Zuriaga-Puig*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文研究了磁化克尔黑洞（magnetized Kerr black hole）的磁彭罗斯过程（magnetic Penrose process, MPP）在人马座A*（Sgr A*）中的具体表现，通过直接从核反应运动学计算吸积流中的中子产生谱，并结合中子轨迹追踪，展示Sgr A*可将质子加速至PeV能量。研究进一步计算了逃逸质子与中央分子云的强子相互作用产生的伽马射线和中微子辐射，指出其可观测信号位于SWGO灵敏度之内，并接近CTAO灵敏度，从而确立MPP作为可观测的黑洞转动能量提取机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 磁彭罗斯过程是从磁化克尔黑洞提取转动能的高效机制，但其观测可行性尚未被系统验证。作者选择银河系中心的超大质量黑洞Sgr A*作为测试对象，旨在建立MPP与多信使（multimessenger）观测之间的直接联系。

Method: 方法分为三个层次：(1) 从核反应运动学直接计算吸积流中子的产生谱；(2) 在克尔时空（Kerr spacetime）中追踪中子轨迹，确定能进入能层（ergosphere）并经历β衰变进而发生MPP的中子数量；(3) 计算逃逸质子与中央分子云（Central Molecular Zone）发生强子相互作用所产生的高能伽马射线和中微子辐射通量。

Result: 结果表明Sgr A*可将质子加速至PeV能标，强化其作为银河系PeVatron候选体的解释。预测的伽马射线通量具有独特的光谱特征，可为MPP提供观测信号，并对H.E.S.S.和HAWC探测到的甚高能辐射有不可忽略的贡献。中微子通量低于IceCube探测的弥散银河成分，但预测信号全部位于SWGO计划灵敏度之内，部分模型下仅比名义CTAO灵敏度低数倍。

Conclusion: MPP被确立为一种可观测的黑洞转动能量提取机制，其预测信号可被未来多信使观测台（SWGO、CTAO、KM3NeT/ARCA、IceCube-Gen2）检验。该工作建立了视界尺度（horizon-scale）物理与多信使观测之间的直接联系，且方法论可推广至其他磁化黑洞系统。

Abstract: The magnetic Penrose process (MPP) is one of the most efficient mechanisms for extracting rotational energy from a magnetized Kerr black hole (BH), enabling charged particles to reach very-high energies. We investigate this process in Sagittarius A* (Sgr A*), the supermassive BH at the Galactic center (GC), focusing on its ultra-high-efficiency regime, in which neutrons undergo beta decay inside the BH ergosphere. As a key novelty, we compute the neutron production spectrum in the accretion flow directly from the nuclear reaction kinematics and follow neutron trajectories in the Kerr spacetime to determine the population that reaches the ergosphere and undergoes the MPP. From this population, we derive the spectrum of accelerated protons and show that Sgr A* can accelerate them up to PeV energies, strengthening its interpretation as a candidate Galactic PeVatron. We further compute the gamma-ray and neutrino emission produced through hadronic interactions of the escaping protons in the Central Molecular Zone. The predicted gamma-ray fluxes exhibit distinctive spectral features that could provide an observational signature of the MPP and may constitute a non-negligible contribution to the very-high-energy emission detected by H.E.S.S. and HAWC. The associated neutrino fluxes remain below the diffuse Galactic component inferred by IceCube, but may still contribute to the high-energy emission from the GC. Remarkably, the predicted signals lie within the projected sensitivity of SWGO for all scenarios considered here and, for some models, only a factor of a few below the nominal CTAO sensitivity, while KM3NeT/ARCA and IceCube-Gen2 will provide complementary tests. Our results establish the MPP as an observable mechanism for extracting BH rotational energy, providing a direct connection between horizon-scale physics and multimessenger observations that can be extended to other magnetized BHs.

</details>
