### [168] [Valley polarization dependence of candidate even-denominator fractional quantum Hall states in monolayer graphene](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02976)
*Saswata Sahu,Amarendra Pr. Indra,Moumita Indra*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本研究利用Chern-Simons复合费米子（composite fermion）框架，在SU(4)对称性下构建了单层石墨烯中偶数分母分数量子霍尔态（甚至分数量子霍尔态，EDFQH）ν=1/2和ν=1/4的多分量变分波函数，并通过球面几何中的库仑相互作用数值比较各极化构型基态能量，识别出能量上有利的候选态为多分量关联量子霍尔液体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示单层石墨烯中偶数分母分数量子霍尔态（EDFQH）的微观机制，特别关注自旋（spin）和谷（valley）自由度导致的近似SU(4)对称性如何影响这些态的形成与稳定。

Method: 采用SU(4)系统的Chern-Simons复合费米子（Chern-Simons composite fermion）框架，为ν=1/2和ν=1/4构建对应不同自旋、谷以及混合极化构型的候选变分波函数（variational wave function），并在球面几何（spherical geometry）中利用库仑相互作用（Coulomb interaction）数值评估各态的基态能量。

Result: 通过比较不同极化扇区间的能量，识别出ν=1/2和ν=1/4处能量上优先的构型。能量比较表明，有利的候选态对应于多分量关联量子霍尔液体，且在所考虑的trial态族内，这些态不同于简单的复合费米子费米海（composite-fermion Fermi-sea）描述。

Conclusion: 研究结果阐明了谷极化和SU(4)对称性在稳定石墨烯中偶数分母分数量子霍尔态（EDFQH）方面的作用。

Abstract: We investigate even-denominator fractional quantum Hall (EDFQH) states in monolayer graphene, focusing on the experimentally observed filling fractions $ν= 1/2$ and $ν= 1/4$. Owing to the approximate SU(4) symmetry arising from spin and valley degrees of freedom, graphene hosts a rich variety of multi-component fractional quantum Hall states with different polarization configurations. Using the Chern-Simons composite fermion framework for SU(4) systems, we construct candidate variational wave functions corresponding to distinct spin, valley, and mixed polarization states. The ground-state energies of these states are evaluated numerically using the Coulomb interaction in spherical geometry. By comparing energies across different polarization sectors, we identify the energetically favored configurations at $ν= 1/2$ and $ν= 1/4$. Our variational energy comparison suggests that the energetically favored candidate states correspond to multi-component correlated quantum Hall liquids. Within the restricted family of trial states considered here, these states differ from a simple composite-fermion Fermi-sea description. These findings provide insight into the role of valley polarization and SU(4) symmetry in stabilizing even-denominator fractional quantum Hall states in graphene.

</details>


### [169] [Orbital current rectifier and linear magnon Edelstein effect in $p$-wave antialtermagnets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03075)
*Kristian Mæland,Björn Trauzettel*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文通过对称性论证和具体模型计算，证明p-波磁体（p-wave magnet）中的磁子（magnon）携带轨道磁矩（orbital magnetic moment），由此产生线性热Edelstein效应驱动的磁化生成以及纯偶数阶非互易轨道电流（nonreciprocal orbital current），从而将磁振子学（magnonics）与轨道电子学（orbitronics）联系起来，并提出磁子的反交磁（antialtermagnetism）轨道对应物。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索磁子携带的轨道磁矩能否产生新的输运现象，并建立磁振子学与轨道电子学之间的物理联系。该研究也试图理解奇宇称p-波类磁体中磁子的磁矩结构，特别是其轨道磁矩在非互易输运中的角色。

Method: 采用对称性论证（symmetry arguments）建立一般性结论，并以最小子晶格（sublattice）数目的共面（coplanar）基态为例进行显式演示，该模型受CeNiAsO基态的启发。分析涵盖线性热Edelstein效应和纯偶数阶非线性响应，分别在有无自旋-轨道耦合（spin-orbit coupling）的情况下均成立。

Result: p-波磁体中的磁子具有非零轨道磁矩，其线性热Edelstein效应可高效产生磁化；通过纯偶数阶非线性响应可生成完全非互易（perfectly nonreciprocal）的轨道电流，适合轨道电流整流应用。此外，奇宇称p-波类磁体的共面基态中，磁子自旋磁矩为零，而携带共线面外轨道磁矩，构成轨道版本的反交磁。

Conclusion: 磁子的轨道磁矩是连接磁振子学与轨道电子学的桥梁，p-波磁体是轨道电流整流的有力候选材料；上述结论在有无自旋-轨道耦合情形下均成立，表明相关物理具有鲁棒性。

Abstract: We show that $p$-wave magnets efficiently generate magnetization via a linear thermal Edelstein effect arising from the orbital magnetic moments of their magnons. Furthermore, they can generate perfectly nonreciprocal orbital currents through a purely even-order nonlinear response. This makes them promising candidates for orbital-current rectification. Because these transport phenomena originate from the orbital magnetic moment of magnons, they connect magnonics and orbitronics. More generally, we find that odd-parity-wave magnets with coplanar ground states host magnons with zero spin magnetic moment. Instead the magnons carry a collinear, out-of-plane orbital magnetic moment, realizing an orbital version of antialtermagnetism. We establish these general results using symmetry arguments and demonstrate them explicitly for a coplanar ground state with the minimal number of sublattices, inspired by the ground state of CeNiAsO. Our conclusions hold both in absence and presence of spin-orbit coupling.

</details>


### [170] [Antiferromagnetism-altered plasmon dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03138)
*Yifan Su,Suheng Xu,Rocco A. Vitalone,Ziyu Liu,Emil Viñas Boström,Na Wu,Chun-Ying Huang,Zhuquan Zhang,Jaehoon Jung,Daniel G. Chica,Vinícius da Silveira Lan Avelar,Yiping Wang,Takashi Taniguchi,Kenji Watanabe,James C. Hone,Xiaoyang Zhu,Cory R. Dean,Xavier Roy,Angel Rubio,D. N. Basov*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本研究首次在二维（2D）材料体系中构建了基于石墨烯/NiPS₃界面的混合等离激元-磁振子平台，通过散射式扫描近场光学显微镜（s-SNOM）结合太赫兹辐射，直接观测到等离激元-极化激元色散在NiPS₃反铁磁序建立时的重整化，为二维材料中混合磁振子-等离激元相互作用提供了坚实实验证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 等离激元（plasmon）与磁振子（magnon）的耦合是长期追寻的物理现象，对基础物理和自旋电子学（spintronics）具有重要意义。三维系统中两者能量尺度差异过大从而抑制耦合；尽管二维中无能隙色散的等离激元理论上可与磁振子光谱重叠，但实验观测一直未获成功，该研究旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 研究人员基于二维材料（graphene）与NiPS₃构建首个混合等离激元-磁振子平台，采用散射式扫描近场光学显微镜（s-SNOM）结合太赫兹辐射，对石墨烯/NiPS₃界面传播的等离激元波包进行成像，并追踪其横跨NiPS₃反铁磁相变的动力学行为；同时辅以拉曼散射（Raman scattering）和纳米太赫兹光谱进行机制验证。

Result: 研究发现等离激元-极化激元（plasmon-polariton）色散在NiPS₃反铁磁有序建立时出现清晰重整化（renormalization），并观测到谱权重再分布和介电屏蔽变化，这可能与多磁振子连续谱（multi-magnon continuum）相关。

Conclusion: 实验结果为等离激元与反铁磁序之间的耦合提供了坚实证据，标志着二维材料中混合磁振子-等离激元相互作用平台的里程碑式进展，为相干自旋-等离激元器件和可调太赫兹自旋电子元件的开发开辟了道路。

Abstract: The interaction between plasmons and magnons is a long-sought phenomenon with implications for fundamental physics and spintronics applications. In three-dimensional systems, this coupling is suppressed by the large mismatch in energy scales, but two-dimensional (2D) plasmons with gapless dispersion can overlap with magnons over a broad spectral range. Despite numerous theoretical predictions, experimental observation of magnon-plasmon interaction has remained elusive. In this work, we study a first-of-its-kind hybrid plasmon-magnon platform based on 2D materials. By deploying scattering-type scanning near-field optical microscopy (s-SNOM) with terahertz radiation, we image propagating plasmon wavepackets at a graphene/NiPS$_3$ interface and track their dynamics across the antiferromagnetic transition of NiPS$_3$. We observe a clear renormalization of the plasmon-polariton dispersion concurrent with the onset of antiferromagnetic order. With complementary Raman scattering and nano-terahertz spectroscopy, we unveil spectral weight redistribution and dielectric screening changes, potentially associated with the multi-magnon continuum, as the underlying mechanism. These results provide solid evidence of coupling between plasmon and antiferromagnetic order, marking a cornerstone for a potential platform for hybrid magnon-plasmon interactions in 2D materials, opening avenues for coherent spin-plasmon devices and tunable terahertz spintronic components.

</details>


### [171] [Symmetry-breaking line defects embedded to a 3D $O(N)$ critical bulk](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03289)
*Shuai Yang,Liang-dong Hu,Yan Chen,W. Zhu*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文利用模糊球面（fuzzy sphere）技术，研究嵌入三维 $O(N)$ Wilson-Fisher 临界体中的钉扎场（pinning-field）线缺陷，发现其存在稳定的缺陷共形不动点（defect conformal fixed points），并通过 $g$-函数证明了重整化群稳定性；进一步分析表明，缺陷畴壁算符在 $N\gtrsim3$ 时不可相关（irrelevant），而在 $N<3$ 时可相关（relevant），从而揭示了不同 $N$ 下对称性自发破缺缺陷固定点的存在条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 一维经典系统中短程相互作用的离散对称性无法自发破缺，但嵌入体临界性中的线缺陷被认为可能承载稳定的离散对称性自发破缺。本文旨在通过数值手段验证并阐明这一设想的可行性与条件。

Method: 采用模糊球面技术模拟三维 $O(N)$ Wilson-Fisher 临界体中的钉扎场线缺陷；通过 $g$-函数比较缺陷固定点与体固定点的有效自由度数量，并检查是否存在相关算符以判断重整化群稳定性；同时分析不同 $N$ 值下缺陷畴壁算符（defect domain wall operator）的可相关性。

Result: 证明了稳定缺陷共形固定点的存在，且其重整化群稳定的关键在于不存在相关算符且有效自由度少于体固定点。缺陷畴壁算符在 $N<3$ 时可相关，但在 $N\gtrsim3$ 时不可相关，说明对称性自发破缺缺陷固定点仅在 $N\gtrsim3$ 时才能稳定存在。

Conclusion: 当临界体耦合一维缺陷时，对于 $N<3$ 的 Wilson-Fisher 普适类，畴壁增殖（domain wall proliferation）导致无法支持稳定的对称性自发破缺缺陷固定点；而在 $N\gtrsim3$ 情形下，对称性破缺的缺陷固定点则是可能的。

Abstract: While spontaneous breaking of a discrete symmetry in one-dimensional classical systems with short-range interactions is absent, it is expected that a line defect embedded in a bulk criticality exhibits a stable discrete symmetry spontaneous breaking. Here, we investigate the behavior of a pinning-field line defect immersed in a 3D bulk that remains tuned to the $O(N)$ Wilson-Fisher critical point. Employing the fuzzy sphere technique, we provide convincing evidence of the existence of stable defect conformal fixed points, and we demonstrate their renormalization group stability by showing no relevant operator and less effective degrees of freedom than that at bulk fixed point via $g$-function. Moreover, we investigate the defect domain wall operator for various $N$, and we identify that it becomes irrelevance for $N\gtrsim 3$ but it is relevant for $N<3$.These evidence indicate that a one-dimensional defect coupled to a critical bulk cannot support a stable symmetry spontaneously broken defect fixed point due to domain wall proliferation for $N<3$ Wilson-Fisher universality, while in the case of $N \gtrsim 3$ a symmetry broken defect is possible.

</details>


### [172] [Moiré-induced altermagnetism from nonmagnetic constituents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03271)
*Jingtian Shi,Maxim Khodas,Ivar Martin*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文提出一种非磁性材料通过与非磁性衬底的莫尔（moiré）干涉产生交替磁性（altermagnetic）序的机制，该机制由晶格失配的莫尔方格子中结构弛豫和莫尔畴壁的自发扭转驱动，在每莫尔畴注入一个电子时打开关联绝缘隙，并依据莫尔势能极小值位置展示d波或g波交替磁性劈裂。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索非磁性材料如何在不引入磁性元素或衬底的条件下形成交替磁性序，为设计和调控新型磁性材料提供新的物理机制和途径。

Method: 通过构造晶格失配的莫尔方格子与非磁性衬底之间的莫尔干涉，利用结构弛豫和莫尔畴壁中自发扭转作为驱动机制；在未掺杂和每莫尔畴注入一个电子的情形下分析电子相互作用打开的关联绝缘隙，并考察莫尔势能极小值位置、扭转角及应变对交替磁性劈裂对称性（d波或g波）的影响。

Result: 当每莫尔畴掺杂一个电子时，电子相互作用可打开关联绝缘隙；依据莫尔势能极小值的位置，莫尔能带可呈现d波或g波交替磁性劈裂；前者可通过有限扭转角增强，后者对应变敏感并可驱动其向d波态转变。

Conclusion: 莫尔应变工程提供了一条无需磁性成分即可在非磁性体系中生成和调控交替磁性序的可行路径，其对称性（d波或g波）可通过扭转角与应变加以调节。

Abstract: We propose a mechanism for nonmagnetic materials to develop altermagnetic order by moiré interference with nonmagnetic substrate, which is driven by structural relaxation and spontaneous twirls in moiré domain walls of lattice-mismatched moiré square lattices. When doped with one electron per moiré domain, a correlated insulating gap is opened by electron interaction. Depending on the location of the moiré potential minima, the moiré bands can show d-wave or g-wave altermagnetic splitting. The former can be enhanced by a finite twist angle; the latter is sensitive to strains that drive a transition to d-wave.

</details>


### [173] [Resolving the Magnetic Ground-State Controversy in RuO2 through A Flat Magnetic Energy Landscape](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03274)
*Tianxiao Liang,Fanhan Kong,Jijun Zhao,Xue Jiang*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文开发了一个广义环境依赖的自旋-晶格（spin-lattice）框架，统一了局域海森堡交换、巡游斯通纳磁性和自旋-轨道耦合相互作用，并将其应用于金红石相RuO2。大规模蒙特卡洛模拟揭示该材料具有异常平坦的磁性能量景观，非磁态与多种反铁磁交替磁序（altermagnetic）构型近简并，从而统一解释了此前相互矛盾的实验观测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金红石相RuO2是交替磁（altermagnetism）领域的重要候选材料，但其磁性基态存在高度争议——实验报告要么是非磁态要么是交替磁序。作者旨在揭示这一矛盾的微观根源。

Method: 发展了一个广义环境依赖的自旋-晶格（spin-lattice）框架，统一描述局域海森堡交换（Heisenberg exchange）、通过朗道自旋涨落体现的巡游斯通纳磁性（itinerant Stoner magnetism）以及自旋-轨道耦合介导的自旋-晶格相互作用。框架参数通过机器学习从高通量第一性原理数据库拟合获得，并用大规模蒙特卡洛（Monte Carlo）模拟求解。

Result: 框架揭示了RuO2中异常平坦的磁性能量景观：非磁态与多种交替磁构型近乎简并。材料扰动（以本征缺陷为例）主要通过修改局域海森堡交换来筛选不同磁性基态，扰动诱导的巡游斯通纳极化提供重要的次级贡献。自旋-轨道耦合控制Néel矢量的取向与稳定性，但并不决定长程磁有序的出现。

Conclusion: 上述结果统一解释了实验中相互矛盾的观测，并建立了理解材料扰动如何在近平坦磁性能量景观系统中筛选竞争磁性基态的通用微观框架。

Abstract: Rutile RuO2 is a prominent candidate for altermagnetism, yet its magnetic ground state remains highly controversial, with experiments reporting either a nonmagnetic state or altermagnetic order. Here, we develop a generalized environment-dependent spin-lattice framework that unifies localized Heisenberg exchange, itinerant Stoner magnetism via Landau spin fluctuations, and spin-orbit-coupling-mediated spin-lattice interactions. Parameterized from a high-throughput first-principles database using machine-learning and solved by large-scale Monte Carlo simulations, the framework reveals an exceptionally flat magnetic energy landscape in RuO2, where the nonmagnetic state lies nearly degenerate with multiple altermagnetic configurations. We find that material perturbations, exemplified by intrinsic defects, select distinct magnetic ground states primarily by modifying the localized Heisenberg exchange, with perturbation-induced itinerant Stoner polarization provides an essential secondary contribution. Spin-orbit coupling controls the orientation and stability of the Néel vector, but does not determine the emergence of long-range magnetic order. These results provide a unified explanation for the conflicting experimental observations and establish a general microscopic framework for understanding how material perturbations select competing magnetic ground states in systems with nearly flat magnetic energy landscapes.

</details>


### [174] [Studying line defect at Deconfined Quantum Criticality via fuzzy sphere regularization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03465)
*Shutao Liu,Shuai Yang,Jie Lou,Yan Chen*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文通过模糊球面正则化方法，数值研究了耦合于(2+1)维去禁闭量子临界体的(0+1)维钉扎场缺陷，首次给出了去禁闭量子临界点上线缺陷共形场论数据的数值表征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 体态临界涨落与非平凡拓扑的相互作用可以丰富缺陷物理并产生新的缺陷普适类（defect universality classes），理解这类缺陷的行为是重要的开放问题。

Method: 研究一个简单设定：将(0+1)维钉扎场缺陷耦合到(2+1)维去禁闭量子临界体。利用模糊球面（fuzzy-sphere）正则化进行数值研究，提取缺陷所描绘的缺陷共形不动点的普适量。

Result: 提取了若干表征缺陷共形不动点的普适量，包括缺陷改变（创造）算符的标度维数（scaling dimensions）以及缺陷g函数（defect g-function）。这些结果首次确立了去禁闭量子临界下线索缺陷共形数据的数值表征。

Conclusion: 所建立的量子临界超导体/量子临界物质中的缺陷临界现象可供进一步研究，研究结果可能激发对拓扑量子临界物质中缺陷临界现象的进一步探索。

Abstract: The interplay between bulk critical fluctuations and nontrivial topology can enrich defect physics and give rise to novel defect universality classes. Understanding the fate of such defects therefore constitutes an important open problem. In this work, we studied a particularly simple setting: a (0+1)-dimensional pinning-field defect coupled to a (2+1)-dimensional deconfined quantum critical bulk. Using the fuzzy-sphere regularization, we numerically investigated the defect operator spectrum and extracted several universal quantities characterizing the defect conformal fixed point, including the scaling dimensions of defect-changing(creating) operators and the defect \(g\)-function. These results establish the first numerical characterization of line-defect conformal data at deconfined quantum criticality and may stimulate further investigations of defect critical phenomena in topological quantum critical matter.

</details>


### [175] [Such stuff as magic is made on: compact operator algebra, stabilizer polytope and the structure of reduced density matrices in a Kitaev spin liquid](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03485)
*Snigdh Sabharwal,Nic Shannon,Paul Skrzypczyk*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文通过研究Kitaev蜂窝模型（Kitaev honeycomb model）中亚系统的“魔法”（magic）开端，揭示了局部系统在低温下获取魔法时涌现出的紧凑算子空间结构，该结构与其对称性相关并能刻画有限温度下的量子资源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子多体系统行为复杂，需要新的诊断工具来刻画其特征。魔法（magic）作为一种新兴的诊断量，可与其他特征如多体纠缠互补，本文旨在探索魔法在量子多体系统子系统中的起始行为及其蕴含的物理结构。

Method: 以Kitaev蜂窝模型为研究对象，考察其中子系统（subsystem）内魔法的出现温度阈值；分析最优魔法见证算子（optimal magic witness）在魔法起始处揭示的算子空间及其随局域热态演化的持续性；对六位点六边形边缘（six-site hexagonal marginal），从局域边缘的对称性独立推导出对称分辨的斑块代数（symmetry-resolved plaquette algebra）；并将相同约化描述扩展至其他局域量子资源如真正的多体纠缠（genuine multipartite entanglement）。

Result: 研究识别出局域子系统获得魔法的温度阈值；发现魔法起始处被最优见证激活的紧凑算子空间能持续刻画后续演化的局域热态。在六位点六边形边缘情形下，该算子空间与对称性独立导出的斑块代数一致。当对称性被精确实现时，局域态完全位于该代数内，且约化鲁棒性等于完整鲁棒性。这些算子空间具有有限维欧几里得约当代数（finite-dimensional Euclidean Jordan algebras）的结构，可自然解释为键算子和键-环算子。

Conclusion: 该通用方法论展示了局部魔法的出现如何揭示有限温度Kitaev自旋液体（Kitaev spin liquid）下紧凑且具有物理意义的算子结构，为多体系统中量子资源的约化描述开辟了新途径。

Abstract: Quantum many-body systems exhibit rich and complex behaviour. Magic has recently emerged as a powerful new diagnostic for probing such systems, complementing other key features such as many-body entanglement. Here, starting from an investigation of the onset of magic within subsystems of a larger many-body quantum system, we show that intricate structures emerge which shed important light on the underlying many-body physics. Focusing on the Kitaev honeycomb model, we first identify the temperature below which local subsystems acquire magic. Remarkably, we find that the optimal magic witnesses active at the onset of magic reveal compact operator spaces that continue to capture the local thermal states throughout their subsequent evolution. For the six-site hexagonal marginal, this connection can be made stronger: the same operator space emerges independently from the symmetries of the local marginal and forms the symmetry-resolved plaquette algebra. When these symmetries are realized exactly, the local state lies entirely within this algebra and the reduced robustness of magic is equal to the full robustness of magic. The same reduced description can also be applied to other local quantum resources, which we illustrate using genuine multipartite entanglement. Furthermore, the operator spaces possess a rich algebraic structure in the form of finite-dimensional Euclidean Jordan algebras, with a natural interpretation in terms of bond and bond-cycle operators. Our general methodology therefore show how the onset of local magic can reveal a compact, physically meaningful operator structure underlying the finite-temperature Kitaev spin liquid, and opens up a new avenue towards reduced descriptions of quantum resources in many-body systems.

</details>


### [176] [Competing lattice structures induced by Sn substitution in CsV$_3$Sb$_5$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03513)
*Anshu Kataria,Ilija K. Nikolov,Armando Consiglio,Giuseppe Allodi,Ginevra Corsale,Andrea Capa Salinas,Stephen D. Wilson,Domenico Di Sante,Vesna F. Mitrović,Samuele Sanna,Pietro Bonfà*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本研究结合$^{121}$Sb核四极共振（NQR）测量与密度泛函理论（DFT）计算，揭示了CsV$_3$Sb$_{5-x}$Sn$_x$中Sn取代对kagome金属电子相图的影响机制：低Sn浓度时产生局部结构畸变，而在完全掺杂情形（$x=1$）下，kagome晶格动力学不稳定性导致两种近简并平衡结构的形成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解化学取代对kagome金属竞争相的影响，对于厘清局部结构畸变与电子不稳定性之间的相互作用至关重要。作者指出Sn取代显著改变了电子相图，但其微观机制仍不明确，这是本研究的核心动机。

Method: 作者将$^{121}$Sb核四极共振（nuclear quadrupole resonance, NQR）测量与密度泛函理论（density functional theory, DFT）计算相结合，系统考察了CsV$_3$Sb$_{5-x}$Sn$_x$中Sn取代的原子尺度效应。

Result: 在低Sn浓度下，观测到卫星NQR峰，表现出由Sn取代诱导的局部结构畸变特征，与计算分析定性一致；这些杂质诱导特征在整个实验掺杂范围（至$x=0.65$）内持续存在并可观测至室温。在完全掺杂情形（$x=1$）下，kagome晶格的动力学不稳定性预测了两种以V三聚体为特征的近能量简并平衡结构的稳定化，二者通过沿$c$轴的相邻层间零相移或$\pi$相移区分。

Conclusion: Sn取代在钒基kagome化合物中驱动了局部杂质诱导畸变与竞争性结构不稳定性之间的复杂相互作用，为理解该类材料中取代掺杂的微观效应提供了关键实验与理论依据。

Abstract: Understanding the effect of chemical substitution on competing phases of kagome metals is crucial for disentangling the interplay between local structural distortions and electronic instabilities. In \cvs, Sn substitution strongly modifies the electronic phase diagram, yet the microscopic mechanism driving this remains unclear. Here, we combine $^{121}$Sb nuclear quadrupole resonance (NQR) measurements and density functional theory calculations to investigate the atomic-scale effects of Sn substitution in CsV$_3$Sb$_{5-x}$Sn$_x$. At low Sn concentrations, the observed satellite NQR peaks exhibit signatures of local structural distortion induced by Sn substitution, qualitatively consistent with our computational analysis. These impurity-induced features persist across the entire experimentally investigated doping range, up to $x$ = 0.65, and remain observable up to room temperature. For $x=1$, the fully doped idealized case, the estimated dynamical instabilities of the kagome lattice suggest the stabilization of two nearly energy-degenerate equilibrium structures characterized by V-trimers, distinguished by a zero- or $π$-phase shift between adjacent layers along the $c$-axis. Together, these results show that Sn substitution drives a complex interplay between local impurity-induced distortions and competing structural instabilities in vanadium-based kagome compounds.

</details>


### [177] [Non-Resonant Impulsively Stimulated Raman Scattering by a Terahertz Field: a Case Study of 1T-TaS2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03519)
*Haotian Zhang,Yuheng Guo,Zidu Yu,Yongbo Lv,Yiting Wang,Liwen Feng,Jiaying Xu,Tianlong Xia,Xinbo Wang,Hao Chu*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本工作以具有丰富电荷密度波（charge-density-wave, CDW）相图的1T-TaS2为研究对象，通过太赫兹泵浦-光探测和太赫兹三次谐波产生实验，阐明了中心对称材料中拉曼活性声子模式与宽带/窄带太赫兹场耦合的机制，提出由双光子吸收介导的修正型脉冲受激拉曼散射（modified impulsively stimulated Raman scattering）是集体模相干激发与观测的物理基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 太赫兹超快非线性光谱技术已广泛用于凝聚态体系中集体激发的探测，但在中心对称体系中这些集体模式通常具有拉曼活性，不直接与太赫兹电场耦合，其光-物质相互作用的实现机制尚未被明确阐释，作者旨在填补这一理论空白。

Method: 作者对单层状材料1T-TaS2（其CDW相图包含公度、近公度和非公度相）执行太赫兹泵浦-光探测（terahertz pump-optical probe）与太赫兹三次谐波产生实验，并分别使用宽带单周期（broadband monocycle）与窄带多周期（narrowband multicycle）太赫兹场，考察各CDW相之间的转变对动力学拉曼响应的影响。

Result: 实验结果表明，一种涉及双光子吸收（two-photon absorption）的修正型脉冲受激拉曼散射（即非共振拉曼散射）机制是拉曼活性声子与太赫兹场耦合并实现相干激发和观测的物理基础，且CDW不同相态间的转变在动力学拉曼响应中留下了清晰特征。

Conclusion: 该研究为利用低能太赫兹场对集体模式进行光谱探测与相干控制，以及固体的腔电动力学（cavity electrodynamics）修饰提供了参考，对未来相关光谱学与相干操控研究具有借鉴意义。

Abstract: Time-domain ultrafast and nonlinear terahertz spectroscopy techniques are recently applied to many condensed matter systems for investigating their collective excitations. In centrosymmetric systems, these collective modes are typically Raman-active and therefore do not couple directly to the terahertz electric field. The mechanism by which light-matter interaction realizes in these studies has not been explicitly discussed in detail. In this work, we perform terahertz pump - optical probe and terahertz third harmonic generation investigations on 1T-TaS2, a material exhibiting a rich charge-density-wave (CDW) phase diagram including the commensurate, nearly-commensurate and incommensurate CDW phases. The transition between these distinct states leaves a clear signature on the dynamical Raman response. We investigate how the Raman-active phonons couple to a broadband monocycle terahertz field as well as a narrowband multicycle terahertz field. Our results indicate that a modified impulsively stimulated Raman scattering mechanism involving two-photon absorption, also known as non-resonant Raman scattering, underlies the coherent excitation and observation of the lattice modes. These results are relevant for future spectroscopy investigation and coherent control of collective modes using low-energy terahertz field as well as cavity electrodynamical dressing of solids.

</details>


### [178] [Hidden Magnetic Octupolar Order driven by Spin-Orbit coupling in KFeF$_3$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03684)
*C. A. Crawford,C. I. Hiley,N. C. Bristowe,C. Stock,J. Gainza,C. Ritter,M. R. Lees,R. I. Walton,M. S. Senn*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文通过粉末衍射、磁测量、对称性分析和第一性原理计算等多种手段，证明了3d钙钛矿KFeF₃中在40 K处存在一种由磁八极（magnetic octupole）隐序驱动的结构相变，该相变不伴随传统的杨-泰勒（Jahn-Teller）畸变或平移对称性破缺，并揭示了自旋-轨道耦合（spin-orbit coupling）抑制竞争性的JT不稳定从而促进高秩磁多极自由度涌现的机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相变通常由破缺电子简并度（electronic degeneracy）的对称性破缺结构畸变驱动，但一些情况下相变可能由不具传统结构特征的隐序（hidden order）驱动；此前缺少此类隐不稳定的明确实验例证。

Method: 综合运用高分辨率粉末衍射、磁测量（magnetometry）、基于对称性的分析和第一性原理计算（first principles calculations），并结合基于对称性的朗道分析（Landau analysis）和密度泛函计算（density functional calculations）进行交叉验证。

Result: 发现KFeF₃在90 K处立方到菱面体结构相变源于伴随反铁磁序（antiferromagnetic order）的常规磁致伸缩；而在40 K处还发生一次降低到单斜对称性的相变，不伴随可检测的Jahn-Teller畸变或平移对称性破缺。朗道分析支持中心磁八极序参量及其非本征耦合机制；DFT计算表明自旋-轨道耦合抑制竞争的JT不稳定性，从而有利于更高秩的磁多极自由度。

Conclusion: KFeF₃被确立为自旋-轨道耦合与轨道简并（orbital degeneracy）之间竞争能量尺度导致隐多极序（hidden multipolar order）涌现的模型体系；控制此类磁多极序原则上可为诱导交变磁性（altermagnetism）提供途径。

Abstract: Phase transitions are typically driven by symmetry-breaking structural distortions that lift electronic degeneracies, yet in some cases, these transitions may be driven by a hidden order without conventional structural signatures. Here, we demonstrate that the 3$d$ perovskite, KFeF$_3$, hosts such a hidden instability. Using a combination of high-resolution powder diffraction, magnetometry, symmetry-based analysis and first principles calculations, we reveal that while the 90 K cubic to rhombohedral transition arises from conventional magnetostriction accompanying antiferromagnetic order, a second transition at 40 K lowers the symmetry to monoclinic without any detectable Jahn-Teller distortion or translational symmetry-breaking. Symmetry-based Landau analysis supports a hidden zone centered magnetic octupole order parameter, whose improper coupling accounts for the weak ferromagnetism and pronounced symmetry-breaking strain. Density functional calculations show that spin-orbit coupling suppresses the competing Jahn-Teller instability, and thereby favors higher rank magnetic multipolar degrees of freedom. These findings establish KFeF$_3$ as a model system in which the competing energy scales between spin-orbit coupling and orbital degeneracy result in the emergence of hidden, multipolar order. We show that the control of such magnetic, multipolar order could, in principle, provide a route to induce altermagnetism.

</details>


### [179] [Fermi-Point Topology Determines Emergent Conformal Criticality in Extended Quantum Spin Chains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03708)
*Mohammad Abbasi,Saeed Mahdavifar*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文在一维扩展量子自旋链中建立了费米点拓扑（Fermi-point topology）、共形临界性与量子纠缠之间的直接对应关系，发现相互作用与磁场驱动的Lifshitz相变可生成有效中心荷（central charge）为1/2、1、3/2、2和3的共形临界相，并提出中心荷由独立的低能连续谱扇区数目决定而非简单的格点能隙闭合数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 一维量子系统的量子临界性由涌现的共形场论（CFT）刻画，其中心荷计数独立的无间隙自由度。然而，在该普适共形结构与底层准粒子谱的动量空间拓扑之间建立微观联系仍具挑战性，本文旨在弥合这一鸿沟。

Method: 研究涉及具有竞争性簇相互作用（cluster interactions）、交换各向异性（exchange anisotropy）和横向磁场的扩展量子自旋链，通过分析相互作用和场驱动的Lifshitz相变来研究共形临界相。作者在计算有效中心荷时计入晶格对称性、倒格矢识别和模等价关系，以确定独立的低能连续谱扇区，并利用实空间和动量空间的纠缠谱提供互补的微观特征。

Result: 研究揭示Lifshitz相变可生成有效中心荷分别为1/2、1、3/2、2和3的共形临界相，包括一个Ising与Luttinger液体扇区共存的多临界点。关键发现是中心荷并非由格点能隙闭合数或费米点数简单决定，而是由计入晶格对称性后独立低能连续谱扇区的数目及共形内容决定。Lifshitz相变可能不改变共形反常，也可能改变中心荷，取决于谱重建是否产生新的独立连续谱扇区。

Conclusion: 本文建立了一个将费米点拓扑与涌现CFT相联系的微观框架，证明相互作用驱动的谱重建可生成高中心荷临界性和非常规多临界行为，实空间与动量空间的纠缠谱可作为揭示共形内容与临界模式动量空间组织的微观探针。

Abstract: Quantum criticality in one-dimensional quantum systems is characterized by emergent conformal field theories (CFTs), whose central charge counts independent gapless degrees of freedom. Establishing a microscopic connection between this universal conformal structure and the momentum-space topology of the underlying quasiparticle spectrum remains challenging. Here, we uncover a direct correspondence between Fermi-point topology, conformal criticality, and quantum entanglement in an extended quantum spin chain with competing cluster interactions, exchange anisotropy, and a transverse magnetic field. We show that interaction- and field-driven Lifshitz transitions generate conformal critical phases with effective central charges $c_{\rm eff}=1/2$, $1$, $3/2$, $2$, and $3$, including a multicritical point where Ising and Luttinger-liquid sectors coexist. Importantly, the central charge is not determined simply by the number of lattice gap closings or Fermi points, but by the number and conformal content of independent low-energy continuum sectors after accounting for lattice symmetries, reciprocal-lattice identifications, and mode equivalences. Thus, Lifshitz transitions may leave the conformal anomaly unchanged or modify the central charge depending on whether spectral reconstruction generates new independent continuum sectors. Real- and momentum-space entanglement spectra provide complementary microscopic signatures, revealing both the conformal content and momentum-space organization of critical modes. Our results establish a microscopic framework linking Fermi-point topology to emergent CFTs and show how interaction-driven spectral reconstruction can generate higher-central-charge criticality and unconventional multicritical behavior.

</details>


### [180] [Ground-State Phase Diagram, Higher-Winding Topology, and Lifshitz Criticality in an Anisotropic Four-Spin XX Chain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03766)
*Mohammad Abbasi,Faraz Valeh,Saeed Mahdavifar*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文研究横向磁场中具有各向异性四自旋簇相互作用的自旋-1/2 XX链的量子临界与拓扑性质，揭示了多个带隙拓扑相、Lifshitz型相变及非常规多临界点，并发现由扩展簇相互作用诱导的高绕数（winding number）拓扑相（ν = ±3）及其丰富的拓扑结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索低维量子系统中各向异性多自旋相互作用如何产生更高绕数的拓扑相与非常规量子临界的可能性，弥补对扩展簇相互作用在量子自旋链中拓扑和临界性质研究的不足。

Method: 针对自旋-1/2 XX链结合各向异性四自旋簇相互作用与横向磁场，通过分析低能费米点结构和动量空间拓扑（winding number），采用数值或精确方法表征量子相图、Lifshitz型转变及纠缠谱简并模式。

Result: 相图包含多个带隙拓扑相，其绕数为ν = 0, ±1, −2和±3；识别出由费米点重构引起的多种Lifshitz型转变，包括各临界支相交的非常规多临界点。各向同性极限下呈现常规Lifshitz转变，展示同一模型中费米点重构的两种不同机制。ν = ±3相具有比常规短程Kitaev型链更丰富的拓扑结构，并通过场与相互作用驱动的能隙闭合与其他相分离。

Conclusion: 各向异性多自旋相互作用为在一维量子系统中实现更高绕数拓扑相和非常规多临界性提供了通用途径，并通过体纠缠谱的低能简并模式进一步区分不同拓扑扇区。

Abstract: We investigate the quantum critical and topological properties of a spin-$1/2$ XX chain with anisotropic four-spin cluster interactions in a transverse magnetic field. The interplay of exchange anisotropy, cluster interactions, and the external field produces a rich phase diagram with multiple gapped topological phases and distinct quantum critical boundaries. We identify several Lifshitz-type transitions arising from reconstructions of the low-energy Fermi-point structure, including an unconventional multicritical point where distinct critical branches intersect and the momentum-space topology undergoes a singular reorganization. In the isotropic limit, a conventional Lifshitz transition emerges through the merging and annihilation of Fermi points, revealing distinct mechanisms of Fermi-point reconstruction within the same model. The gapped phases are characterized by quantized winding numbers $ν=0,\pm1,-2,$ and $\pm3$, with the higher-winding phases induced by the extended cluster interactions. The $ν=\pm3$ phases exhibit a richer topological structure than conventional short-range Kitaev-type chains and are separated from other sectors by field- and interaction-driven gap closings. These topological distinctions are further reflected in characteristic degeneracy patterns of the lowest levels of the bulk entanglement spectrum. Our results demonstrate that anisotropic multispin interactions provide a versatile route to realizing higher-winding topological phases and unconventional multicriticality in one-dimensional quantum systems.

</details>


### [181] [Universal Driven Critical Dynamics of Entanglement Entropy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03854)
*Chang-Yu Shen,Shuai Yin,Zi-Xiang Li*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文建立了一个适用于量子纠缠非平衡动力学的普适有限时间标度理论（finite-time scaling theory）。通过无偏量子蒙特卡洛模拟，研究者发现(2+1)维相互作用的狄拉克费米子从有序相向量子临界点驱动的过程中，角纠缠熵精确服从由驱动速率和系统尺寸控制的普适驱动标度，且该动力学纠缠对驱动速率呈对数依赖，可据此稳健地提取底层共形场论的普适角系数，从而将Kibble-Zurek机制从局部可观测量推广至非局域量子信息度量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Kibble-Zurek机制（KZM）与有限时间标度（FTS）为驱动临界动力学提供了基础框架，但其预测能力长期局限于局部可观测量。本文的动机在于将这些框架推广至本质非局域的量子信息度量——即量子纠缠——以建立更普适的非平衡标度理论。

Method: 作者采用无偏量子蒙特卡洛（quantum Monte Carlo）模拟方法，研究了(2+1)维相互作用的狄拉克费米子体系，考察其从有序相（可以是完全带隙的或具有无能隙Goldstone模的）被驱动向量子临界点过程中的角纠缠熵（corner entanglement entropy）的动力学行为。通过同时考虑驱动速率与系统尺寸的标度分析，构建了通用的驱动临界标度框架。

Result: 研究发现，角纠缠在驱动过程中精确遵守普适的有限时间标度律，该标度律由驱动速率和系统尺寸共同决定，且无论初始有序态是完全带隙的还是带有无能隙Goldstone模的，该规律均成立。更重要的是，动力学纠缠随驱动速率呈对数依赖关系，由此可以稳健地从非平衡态中提取底层共形场论（conformal field theory）的普适角系数。

Conclusion: 这些结果将Kibble-Zurek机制从局部可观测量推广至内在非局域的量子信息度量，为在可编程量子模拟器上刻画量子临界性与纠缠特性提供了实用的蓝图。

Abstract: The Kibble-Zurek mechanism (KZM) and finite-time scaling (FTS) provide a foundational framework for driven critical dynamics, yet their predictive power has been largely confined to local observables. Here, we establish a universal finite-time scaling theory for the nonequilibrium dynamics of quantum entanglement. Using unbiased quantum Monte Carlo simulations, we investigate the corner entanglement entropy of (2+1)-dimensional interacting Dirac fermions driven from ordered phases toward a quantum critical point. We find that the corner entanglement accurately obeys a universal driven scaling governed by the driving rate and system size, persisting whether the initial ordered state is fully gapped or hosts gapless Goldstone modes. Crucially, this dynamical entanglement exhibits a logarithmic dependence on the driving rate, from which the universal corner coefficient of the underlying conformal field theory can be robustly extracted far from equilibrium. These results generalize the KZM from local observables to the intrinsic nonlocal quantum information measures, offering a practical blueprint for characterizing quantum criticality and entanglement on programmable quantum simulators.

</details>


### [182] [Evolution of electron spin resonance through a metallic quantum critical phase diagram](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03994)
*Marc Scheffler,Jörg Sichelschmidt,Conrad Clauss,Mojtaba Javaheri Rahim,Boris I. Kochelaev,Cornelius Krellner,Christoph Geibel,Frank Steglich,Martin Dressel*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文通过一种新的实验方法在极低温度下、大频率和磁场范围内观测重费米子金属YbRh2Si2中的电子自旋共振（ESR），覆盖从顺磁费米液体相到反铁磁有序相及量子临界区的相图。研究发现ESR的g因子和线宽在三个区域呈现不同行为，且在向量子临界点降温过程中二者均持续减小，场调制与温度调制的g因子行为高度吻合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在重费米子（heavy-fermion）金属YbRh2Si2中，被抑制的反铁磁（antiferromagnetic）序附近的量子临界性（quantum criticality）由局域磁矩（local magnetic moments）与巡游传导电子（itinerant conduction electrons）的相互作用主导，理解这一物理机制需要能够覆盖宽参数范围的实验手段。

Method: 采用一种新的实验方法，在极低温度下于宽频率和磁场范围内观测电子自旋共振（electron spin resonance, ESR），从而覆盖从顺磁费米液体（paramagnetic Fermi-liquid）相到反铁磁有序相并包含量子临界区的相图区域。

Result: ESR的g因子和线宽在顺磁费米液体相、反铁磁有序相和量子临界区等三个区域呈现不同行为；当沿固定磁场直接降温逼近量子临界点时，g因子和线宽均持续减小；此外，场调制与温度调制两种路径逼近量子临界点时g因子行为高度匹配。

Conclusion: 实验结果在强关联电子系统（strongly correlated electron systems）中ESR的现有理论框架下得到分析与讨论，为理解量子临界点附近的物理提供了新的实验证据与见解。

Abstract: In the heavy-fermion metal YbRh2Si2, quantum criticality at a suppressed antiferromagnetic order is governed by the interplay of local magnetic moments and itinerant conduction electrons. We demonstrate how this can be investigated by a new experimental approach that enables the observation of electron spin resonance (ESR) across a broad range of frequencies and fields at very low temperatures. This allowed us to cover a large part of the phase diagram from the paramagnetic Fermi-liquid phase to the phase with antiferromagnetic order and including the quantum-critical regime. Both the ESR g-factor and the linewidth present distinct behaviors in these three regimes, providing further insight into the physics across a quantum critical point. Notably, when cooling down at a field directly towards the quantum critical point, both g-factor and linewidth continuously decrease. Furthermore, we observe a very good matching of the g-factor behavior upon field-tuning and temperature-tuning towards the quantum-critical point. We analyze and discuss the results in the context of present theories on ESR in strongly correlated electron systems.

</details>


### [183] [HyperDet Wavefunction: A Phase-Agnostic Ansatz for Strongly Correlated Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04146)
*Xiaodong Hu,Guan-Lin Lin,Ying Ran,Di Xiao*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文提出超行列式（hyperdeterminant, HyperDet）波函数，作为一种不依赖特定相位假设的变分拟设，可同时描述玻色与费米量子多体系统中的对称性破缺、分数化和拓扑有序等多种竞争相位；通过可学习的融合张量将辅助费米子部分子（parton）Slater行列式融合到物理轨道，经变分蒙特卡罗优化后，单一的HyperDet架构在整个分数陈绝缘体（fractional Chern insulator）相位及邻近竞争相位中均能达到≥99.9%的精确对角化基态重叠度，并可用于相位诊断和拓扑数据提取。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强关联系统不同竞争相位的描述常依赖从特定相位假设出发的试波函数，缺乏统一且相位无关的拟设框架，难以同时精确刻画具有对称性破缺、分数化和拓扑有序等不同特征的量子多体系统。

Method: 将辅助费米子部分子Slater行列式通过全可学习的融合张量（fusion tensor）F融合到物理轨道中，构建HyperDet波函数作为玻色与费米多体系统的相位无关拟设；采用变分蒙特卡罗（variational Monte Carlo）进行优化，并引入二分融合矩阵（bipartite fusion matrix）的奇异值谱作为融合张量结构的诊断工具。

Result: 单一的HyperDet架构在整个分数陈绝缘体相位及其邻近竞争相位中对玻色和费米模型的精确对角化基态能达到≥99.9%的重叠度；奇异值谱的重新分布能够追踪多体相变而无需计算相位特定观测量；优化后的融合张量再现了玻色和费米FCI态的预期部分子陈数，并通过其内在规范结构恢复玻色FCI的部分子平移分数化性质。

Conclusion: HyperDet波函数有望成为强关联系统基态搜索和相图探索的通用变分平台，同时为从部分子层面微观物理到场论描述的跨尺度因果链提供可解释的理论洞察。

Abstract: Describing competing phases of strongly correlated systems often requires trial wave functions built from phase-specific assumptions. We propose the \emph{hyperdeterminant (HyperDet) wavefunction} as a phase-agnostic ansatz for both bosonic and fermionic quantum many-body systems exhibiting spontaneous symmetry-breaking order, fractionalization, and/or topological order with anyonic excitations. The HyperDet structure emerges naturally by fusing auxiliary fermionic parton Slater determinants into physical orbitals through a fully learnable \emph{fusion tensor} $\mathcal F$. Optimized using variational Monte Carlo, a single HyperDet architecture can achieve exceptionally high overlaps $\geq 99.9\%$ with exact-diagonalization ground states throughout the entire fractional Chern insulator phase in both bosonic and fermionic models, and across their nearby competing phases. We introduce the singular-value spectrum of the \emph{bipartite fusion matrix} as a structural diagnostic of fusion tensor, and find that its redistribution tracks many-body phase transitions without computing phase-specific observables. The optimized fusion tensor also encodes the parton-level topological data: it reproduces the parton Chern numbers expected for the bosonic and fermionic FCI states, completing their field-theory descriptions and the resulting topological order. Its intrinsic gauge structure further determines whether physical symmetries admit virtual lifts and, when faithful lifts exist, extracts their projective class; for the bosonic FCI, this recovers the expected parton translation fractionalization. We thus anticipate the HyperDet wavefunction to be a promising variational platform for both accurate ground-state searches and phase-diagram explorations across strongly correlated phases, and for providing interpretable theoretical insights from parton-level microscopics to field-theory descriptions.

</details>


### [184] [Non-uniform quantum geometry stabilizes generalized Wigner crystals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04149)
*Nicolás Morales-Durán,Tobias M. R. Wolf,Jingtian Shi,Tomohiro Soejima,Andrew J. Millis,Jennifer Cano*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本研究针对莫尔材料中分数量子霍尔态与电子晶体之间的竞争机制，在具有理想但动量依赖的量子几何（Aharonov-Casher能带）的Chern能带中，通过构建广义维格纳晶体的试探波函数并与Laughlin类态能量比较，绘制了填充因子ν=1/m下的相图。研究发现量子几何控制的电荷密度零点涨落是决定广义维格纳晶体相对稳定性的机制，并由此提出了一种类似量子林德曼准则的晶体-液体相变判据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 莫尔材料中分数量子霍尔绝缘体（fractional Chern insulator）与电子晶体（electron crystal）在相近条件下共存，但二者之间的选择机制尚不明确。作者希望阐明这一竞争关系中量子几何（quantum geometry）的作用。

Method: 作者在具有理想但动量依赖的量子几何的Chern能带（Aharonov-Casher能带）中，提出广义维格纳晶体（generalized Wigner crystal）的尝试波函数（ansatz wave function），通过将其能量与竞争的Laughlin类态（Laughlin-like state）能量进行比较，在填充分数ν=1/m下以几何非均匀度（geometric non-uniformity）为参数绘制相图。

Result: 研究识别出由量子几何控制的广义维格纳晶体电荷密度零点涨落（zero point fluctuations）是调控其相对稳定性的核心机制，并据此提出了一种用于晶体-液体相界判定的量子林德曼准则（quantum Lindemann criterion）。

Conclusion: 量子几何的非均匀性通过控制电荷密度的零点涨落来决定广义维格纳晶体相对于Laughlin类态的稳定性，为莫尔材料中电子晶体与关联拓扑态之间的竞争提供了新的理论框架。

Abstract: Moiré materials host fractional Chern insulators and electron crystals in close proximity, but the mechanism selecting between them remains an open question. We address this competition in Chern bands with ideal but momentum-dependent quantum geometry -- Aharonov-Casher bands. We present an ansatz wave function for generalized Wigner crystals and, by comparing its energy to that of the competing Laughlin-like state, map out the phase diagram at filling fraction $ν=1/m$ as a function of the degree of geometric non-uniformity. Our work identifies quantum geometry-controlled zero point fluctuations of the charge density of the generalized Wigner crystal as the mechanism controlling its relative stability, implying a kind of quantum Lindemann criterion for the crystal-liquid phase boundary.

</details>


### [185] [Wavefunctions for Anyon Superconductors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04187)
*Donghae Seo,Taegon Lee,Gil Young Cho*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文提出了一套体系的层次波函数构造方法，用于描述由母体拓扑序（parent topological order）衍生而来的任意子超导态，并通过等离子体类比与拓扑场论刻画了其离域长程序、凝聚荷（condensate charge）、手征中心荷（chiral central charge）及残余拓扑序。结果表明该框架统一了任意子凝聚、超导序与拓扑场论三个层面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 任意子超导（anyon superconductivity）源于可移动任意子的凝聚（condensation）而非传统Cooper不稳定性，但目前缺乏系统的波函数描述，这构成了本工作的核心动机。

Method: 作者构建了源于母体拓扑序的层次波函数（hierarchy-wavefunction）构造方法，借助等离子体类比（plasma analogy）与拓扑场论（topological field theory）识别超导态的关键性质，并在理想Chern带与MoTe₂扭角双层莫尔带体系中进行验证。

Result: 构造了从semion态、ν=2/3层次态、ν=1/3 Laughlin态、ν=1整数量子霍尔态及Pfaffian态出发的阿贝尔与非阿贝尔超导实例。特别地，semion情形下多任意子超导波函数等价于填充两个有效Landau能级的费米化任意子态，还原了Laughlin对semion超导的原始构造。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了统一的波函数层面框架，将任意子凝聚、超导序与拓扑场论联系起来，并表明层次波函数在任意子希尔伯特空间公式的稀释长距离极限中自然涌现。

Abstract: Anyon superconductivity arises from the condensation of mobile anyons rather than from a conventional Cooper instability, yet a systematic wavefunction description remains lacking. We develop a hierarchy-wavefunction construction for superconducting states derived from parent topological orders and identify their off-diagonal long-range order, condensate charge, chiral central charge, and residual topological order through the plasma analogy and topological field theories. We construct Abelian and non-Abelian examples descending from the semion state, a $ν=2/3$ hierarchy state, the $ν=1/3$ Laughlin state, $ν=1$ integer quantum Hall state, and Pfaffian state. Remarkably, for the semion case, the superconducting many-semion wavefunction is equivalent to a state of fermionized anyons filling two effective Landau levels, recovering Laughlin's original construction of semion superconductor. Finally, we show that hierarchy wavefunctions emerge naturally in the dilute, long-distance limit of the anyon-Hilbert-space formulation, which applies to ideal Chern bands and moiré bands of twisted bilayer MoTe$_2$. Our results establish a unified wavefunction-level framework linking anyon condensation, superconducting order, and topological field theory.

</details>

### [186] [Reconstruction of Shower-like Events in NEON Using Likelihood and Graph Neural Network Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03417)
*Sujie Lin,Weiqin Huang,Yihan Liu,Chengyu Shao,Lili Yang,Huiming Zhang*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文首次为南海中微子观测站（NEON）建立了簇射状事件（shower-like events）的完整重建框架，包含基于物理的最大似然估计（MLE）方法和基于数据的图神经网络（GNN）方法，在模拟中实现了 MLE 方法 4.19° 的角分辨率与 GNN 在低至中间能区的最佳性能，并为 NEON 的有效面积和点源发现潜力评估提供了基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NEON 是拟建于南海的深海微子望远镜，簇射状事件的精确重建对微子能量测量和多信使天文学（multi-messenger astronomy）至关重要，但海水光学衰减、不规则探测器几何以及 $^{40}\mathrm{K}$ 环境本底使重建面临重大挑战。此前尚无针对 NEON 的全面簇射重建框架。

Method: 提出双路径重建框架：(1) 物理驱动的最大似然估计（MLE）方法，整合空间-等时命中选择、基于时间残差 M 估计量（M-estimator）最小化的顶点重建、以及基于预计算光子分布表的解耦方向与能量估计，并在似然函数中引入 PMT 角接受度、时间游走校正（time slewing）和有效线源簇射扩展物理标定；(2) 两阶段图神经网络（GNN），用以捕获 DOM 内部 PMT 关联与距离加权的 DOM 间拓扑模式。

Result: 模拟结果表明，MLE 方法在 1 TeV 至 1 PeV 能区实现总体中位角分辨率 4.19° 与 25%-37% 的能量分辨率且系统偏差可忽略；GNN 在低至中间能区进一步提升重建精度，在 30 TeV 处实现中位角分辨率 1.8°，在 40-300 TeV 内能量分辨率约 20%。基于这些性能评价了 NEON 的有效面积（effective area）与点源发现潜力（discovery potential）。

Conclusion: 该框架为 NEON 确立了关键的重建基准（benchmark），并为未来下一代深海微子望远镜提供了实用方法论。

Abstract: The Neutrino Observatory in the Nanhai (NEON) is a proposed deep-sea neutrino telescope deployed in the South China Sea. Accurate reconstruction of shower-like events is crucial for neutrino energy measurements and multi-messenger astronomy, yet it poses significant challenges due to seawater optical attenuation, irregular detector geometry, and substantial $^{40}\mathrm{K}$ ambient background. In this work, we present the first comprehensive reconstruction framework for shower-like events in NEON, encompassing both a physics-driven maximum likelihood estimation (MLE) method and a data-driven Graph Neural Network (GNN). The traditional MLE framework integrates spatial-isochronic hit selection, vertex reconstruction via time-residual M-estimator minimization, and decoupled directional and energy estimation based on pre-computed photon distribution tables. Physical calibrations, including PMT angular acceptance, hit-level time slewing corrections, and an effective line-source shower extension, are incorporated into the likelihood formulation. In parallel, a two-stage GNN is developed to capture intra-DOM PMT correlations and distance-weighted inter-DOM topological patterns. Simulation studies show that the MLE method achieves an overall median angular resolution of $4.19^\circ$ and an energy resolution of 25\%-37\% over 1 TeV to 1 PeV with negligible systematic bias. The GNN further improves reconstruction fidelity in the low-to-intermediate energy regime, achieving a median angular resolution of $1.8^\circ$ at 30 TeV and an energy resolution of $\sim$ 20\% between 40 and 300 TeV. Based on these reconstruction performances, the effective area and point-source discovery potential of NEON are evaluated. This framework establishes an essential reconstruction benchmark for NEON and provides practical methodologies for future next-generation deep-sea neutrino telescopes.

</details>


### [187] [The influence of standard relativistic effects on Gaia-like astrometric catalogs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02922)
*Gabriel Rodríguez-Moris,Sergei A. Klioner*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文研究未完全计入的广义相对论效应（general relativistic effects）对Gaia类天体测量望远镜所测源天体天体测量参数（astrometric parameters）的影响。通过AGISLab模拟工具，对使用不同不完整相对论模型的观测进行拟合，发现由于对每个源的多重观测取平均效应，参数误差远小于单次观测中的相对论效应幅度，从而为未来更高精度的天体测量项目简化相对论模型提供了可能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着Gaia等高精度天体测量空间望远镜的观测精度提升，牛顿天文框架已不再适用，需在广义相对论框架下建模。然而不同相对论效应对不同观测的影响比例可能极小，需要评估未计入的相对论效应会如何影响由多次观测拟合得到的天体测量源参数精度。

Method: 在标准Gaia相对论框架下识别出影响最显著的情况，然后利用模拟工具AGISLab，对若干源产生的Gaia类观测数据，用不同完整程度（不同缺省元素）的Gaia类相对论天体测量模型进行拟合，得到参数误差；并给出简化的解析误差描述并加以讨论。

Result: 研究表明，由于每个源的多重观测取平均效应，天体测量参数中的误差远小于单次观测中相应相对论效应的大小。误差的大幅减小为在追求高于Gaia精度的未来天体测量计划中简化相对论模型提供了途径。

Conclusion: 未计入的相对论效应对多观测取平均后的天体测量参数影响有限，说明在更高精度目标下仍有简化相对论模型的空间，但该简化需依据对误差幅度的定量认识来设计。

Abstract: With the observational accuracy of astrometric space telescopes such as Gaia, the Newtonian theoretical framework for positional astronomy is no longer appropriate. To model all effects relevant at the corresponding levels of accuracy, a more sophisticated description in the framework of General Relativity is required. It is generally known how various relativistic effects affect a given astrometric observation. In particular, the maximal light deflection of a given Solar System body can be easily estimated for a given observer. However, astrometric parameters for a given source are computed from a series of observations of that source. Depending on the relativistic effect, the fraction of observations affected, at some given level of accuracy, by that relativistic effect can vary and also be very small. Our goal is to investigate the impact of unaccounted relativistic effects on the astrometric parameters of a source observed by a Gaia-like astrometric telescope. The standard relativistic framework for Gaia is used to highlight such effects in some cases where they are most significant. Then, using the simulation tool AGISLab, we fit a set of Gaia-like observations of a given number of sources to the standard Gaia-like astrometric model for astrometric source parameters, but using different incomplete relativistic models. In this way we obtain the errors of the astrometric parameters if one were to drop different elements of the relativity model. A simplified analytical description of the errors is also provided and discussed. We demonstrate that, due to averaging over many observations per source, the errors in the astrometric parameters are drastically smaller than the corresponding relativistic effects in individual observations. This opens a way to potentially simplify the relativistic model for future astrometric projects aiming for higher accuracy than Gaia.

</details>


<div id='math.AP'></div>

# math.AP [[Back]](#toc)

### [188] [Standing waves for Schrodinger equations with Kato class potentials and $L^\infty$-bounded nonlinearities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03098)
*Aleksander Ćwiszewski,Piotr Kokocki*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文针对具有Kato类（Kato class）势能和有界非线性项的非线性薛定谔方程，建立了非平凡驻波（standing waves）的存在性，涵盖非共振与共振情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在证明一类具有Kato类势能和非线性项的非线性薛定谔方程（nonlinear Schrödinger equation）存在驻波解，同时处理非共振（nonresonant）与共振（resonant）两种情况。

Method: 方法基于Conley指标理论（Conley index theory），用于研究由方程诱导的抛物半流（parabolic semiflow）的不变集。利用具有Kato类势能的薛定谔半群性质（该性质源于其Feynman-Kac表示），在L∞和L²范数下对有界解建立先验估计，并在Sobolev空间中获得正则性界。

Result: 获得了确保平稳解（stationary solutions）之间存在连接轨道（connecting orbits）的条件，由此导出非平凡驻波的存在性。

Conclusion: 在实际应用中验证了条件，从而证明非平凡驻波的存在性；结论适用于非共振与共振情形，且方法依赖于Conley指标理论与薛定谔半群的正则性估计。

Abstract: We establish the existence of standing waves for a nonlinear Schrodinger equation with potentials belonging to the Kato class and an $L^\infty$-bounded nonlinearity, whose Lipschitz constant is smaller than the distance from zero to the essential spectrum of the linear part. We consider both the nonresonant and resonant cases. Our approach is based on the Conley index theory applied to study invariant sets of the associated parabolic semiflow. Using properties of the Schrodinger semigroup with Kato-class potentials, which follow from its Feynman-Kac representation, we derive a priori estimates for bounded solutions in the $L^\infty$ and $L^2$ norms, as well as regularity bounds in Sobolev spaces. As a consequence, we obtain conditions ensuring the existence of connecting orbits between stationary solutions, which in turn yield the existence of nontrivial standing waves.

</details>


### [189] [The mean-field limit of the Schrödinger-Lohe model and emergent dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03848)
*François Golse,Seung-Yeal Ha*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究薛定谔-洛赫（Schrödinger-Lohe, SL）模型的严格平均场极限（mean-field limit），给出了单粒子分布在2-瓦瑟斯坦距离下的定量波动估计，并为推导出的动力学SL方程提供了实现完全同步和实际同步的充分条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SL模型作为量子同步的无穷维对应物（infinite-dimensional counterpart of the Lohe matrix model），描述分量波函数的时间-空间演化，但其平均场极限的理论严格性和同步条件尚需深入研究。

Method: 论文采用严格数学分析的方法，在2-瓦瑟斯坦距离（2-Wasserstein distance）下对单边际分布（one-marginal distribution）与单粒子分布（one particle distribution）之间的波动（fluctuations）进行定量估计，从而建立平均场极限。

Result: 作者成功推导出动力学SL方程（kinetic SL equation），并给出了导致完全同步（complete synchronization）和实际同步（practical synchronization）成立的充分条件。

Conclusion: 该研究成果为SL模型的平均场极限提供了严格的数学基础，并阐明了其同步行为发生的条件，深化了对量子同步现象的理解。

Abstract: The Schrödinger-Lohe (SL) model is a coupled system of nonlinear Schrödinger equations describing the temporal-spatial evolution of the component wave functions, and it corresponds to the infinite-dimensional counterpart of the Lohe matrix model for quantum synchronization. In this paper, we study a rigorous mean-field limit of the SL model and provide a quantitative estimate on the fluctuations between one-marginal distribution and one particle distribution in 2-Wasserstein distance. For the derived kinetic SL equation, we present sufficient conditions leading to the complete and practical synchronizations.

</details>


<div id='q-bio.CB'></div>

# q-bio.CB [[Back]](#toc)

### [190] [Modeling Tissue Detachment and Rupture Using an Extended Vertex Model with T2-inverse Transitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03691)
*Shota Nishimoto,Yuichi Togashi*

Main category: q-bio.CB

TL;DR: 本文提出顶点模型（vertex model）的最小扩展方案，通过引入经典T2转变（T2 transition）的逆操作——T2-inverse拓扑变换，实现细胞脱离（cell detachment）的建模。当作用在顶点上的张力不平衡超过阈值时，T2-inverse将顶点分裂为多个顶点并形成闭合并入的伪细胞（pseudo-cell），从而在拉伸细胞片时模拟力诱导的局部脱离累积导致宏观组织破裂的过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统顶点模型假设所有细胞紧密排列并始终与邻居共享边，无法表达局部脱离（local detachment）或间隙形成（gap formation）等上皮组织中的关键机械现象，因此需要扩展模型的有效表征能力。

Method: 在传统顶点模型基础上引入一种新的拓扑变换——T2-inverse（即经典T2转变的逆操作），通过定义张力度量（tension metric）T来衡量作用在顶点上的力不平衡程度；当T超过设定阈值时，T2-inverse将顶点分裂为多个顶点并创建一个闭合多边形作为伪细胞，从而实现细胞脱离的拓扑表征。

Result: 利用该框架模拟细胞片拉伸实验，结果显示力诱导的局部脱离事件能够逐步累积，最终产生宏观尺度的组织破裂（tissue rupture），验证了模型对组织级破坏过程（包括脱离与撕裂）的描述能力。

Conclusion: 该模型将顶点模型的应用范围扩展至组织层面的分解与破坏过程，为研究更广泛的上皮机械现象（epithelial mechanical phenomena）提供了基础工具。

Abstract: The vertex model is widely used to describe the mechanics of epithelial tissues, but its conventional formulation assumes that all cells remain tightly packed and always share edges with their neighbors, making it difficult to represent local detachment or gap formation. Here, we propose a minimal extension of the vertex model that enables cell detachment by introducing a new topological transformation, T2-inverse, which acts as the inverse of the classical T2 transition. When the imbalance of forces acting on a vertex, quantified by a tension metric $T$, exceeds a threshold, the T2-inverse splits the vertex into multiple vertices and creates a closed polygon that is incorporated as a pseudo-cell. This operation allows the model to represent the emergence and propagation of local detachment events. Using this framework, we simulate the stretching of a cell sheet and show that force-induced local detachments can accumulate to produce macroscopic tissue rupture. These results demonstrate that the proposed model extends the capability of the vertex model to describe tissue-level breakdown processes, including detachment and tearing, and provides a foundation for studying a broader class of epithelial mechanical phenomena.

</details>

# math.NT [[Back]](#toc)

### [191] [Poissonian pair correlations for the three-particle Sutherland model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03112)
*Christopher Lutsko*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 该论文为排斥性三粒子三角Sutherland哈密顿量（trigonometric Sutherland Hamiltonian）证明了Berry-Tabor定理：在固定总动量下，其相对谱是正定$A_2$-型的仿射平移；对于Diophantine耦合，去对称化谱具有泊松型对关联（Poissonian pair correlation），证明将问题归结为Marklof关于非齐次二次型定理的有限同余和Weyl扇区版本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证Berry-Tabor猜想在可积多体量子系统中的成立性，即经典可积系统对应的量子能谱应呈现泊松统计而非混沌系统的Wigner-Dyson统计。

Method: 通过证明谱的强去对称化性质，将三粒子系统的能级统计问题约化到非齐次二次型（inhomogeneous quadratic forms）上的有限同余结构与Weyl扇区版本的Marklof定理。

Result: 对排斥性三粒子三角Sutherland哈密顿量证明了Berry-Tabor定理，确立其去对称化谱在Diophantine耦合参数下具有泊松型对关联。

Conclusion: 该结果为可积量子系统的谱统计理论提供了严格的新证明实例，并建立了可积多体系统与二次型数论之间的联系。

Abstract: We prove a Berry-Tabor theorem for the repulsive three-particle trigonometric Sutherland Hamiltonian. For fixed total momentum, its relative spectrum is an affine translate of the positive-definite $A_2$-form. For a Diophantine coupling, the desymmetrized spectrum has Poissonian pair correlation. The proof reduces the problem to a finite-congruence and Weyl-sector version of Marklof's theorem on inhomogeneous quadratic forms.

</details>

# physics.gen-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [192] [Observational Viability of $φ^{2}$-Superpotential Inflation with GUP-Induced Corrections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02912)
*Narges Rashidi,Maryam Roushan*

Main category: physics.gen-ph

TL;DR: 本研究在一个受广义不确定原理（GUP）启发的量子引力框架内，研究了具有φ²超势的暴胀（inflation）模型。通过超势改写动力学并使用二次型超势假设，推导出GUP修正的标量谱指数与张标比，并将其预测与多组最新观测数据对比，发现模型在适度的负GUP参数β和小于1的超势参数C取值下可落于观测置信区间内。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者希望检验量子引力修正（尤其是GUP效应）能否将标准二次型超势暴胀模型原本与观测偏离的预测（ns, r）修正到当前观测数据所偏好的区域，从而为扩展暴胀模型提供理论支持。

Method: 采用视界热力学（horizon thermodynamics）方法，结合温度-表面引力关系与修正的熵-面积定律得到GUP修正的Friedmann方程；通过超势W(φ)并取φ=φ(a)改写动力学，推导慢滚参数及GUP修正的标量谱指数ns和张标比r；采用二次型假设W(φ)=½m²φ²计算可观测预言，并与Planck 2018、DESI+CMB+DESY5、Planck+ACT等多组数据集给出的置信区域进行比较。

Result: 分析表明，当有效GUP参数β取中等负值且唯象超势参数C取小于1的数值时，模型预测能够落入当前观测数据的68%及95%置信区间内；GUP修正可将二次型超势的(ns, r)预测向观测偏好的方向推移。此外，GUP效应导致标准的暴胀一致性关系（consistency relation）发生形变。

Conclusion: GUP启发的修正能够有效改变二次型超势暴胀模型的可观测预言，使其与最新宇宙学观测相容。这暗示量子引力修正可能是解释观测数据与简单暴胀模型预期差异的一个可行途径。

Abstract: We study a $φ^{2}$-superpotential inflationary model within a GUP-inspired quantum-gravity framework. Using horizon thermodynamics, we review the GUP-corrected Friedmann equations obtained by combining the temperature--surface-gravity relation with a modified entropy--area law. Reformulating the dynamics via a superpotential $W(φ)$ with $φ=φ(a)$, we obtain the corresponding slow-roll parameters and derive GUP-modified expressions for the scalar spectral index and the tensor-to-scalar ratio, leading to a deformation of the standard inflationary consistency relation. Adopting the quadratic ansatz $W(φ)=\tfrac{1}{2}m^{2}φ^{2}$, we evaluate the observables $(n_{s},r)$ and compare them with the Planck 2018 TT, TE, EE + lowE + lensing + BK18 + BAO, DESI + CMB + DESY5, and Planck2018 + ACT + lensing + BK18 + BAO (from DESI) datasets. The analysis shows that, for moderately negative values of the effective GUP parameter $β$ and sub-unity values of the phenomenological superpotential parameter $C$, the model predictions can fall within the $68\%$ and $95\%$ confidence regions of current observations. These results indicate that GUP-inspired corrections can shift the $(n_{s},r)$ predictions of a quadratic superpotential toward the observationally favored region.

</details>
