### [193] [FrOGS: Discrete Neural Sampler for Independent Alloy Configurations Across Chemical Conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02948)
*Kyucheol Min,Elyssa Hofgard,Tess Smidt*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种混合离散神经采样器——自由能生成采样器（Free energy Offering Generative Sampler, FrOGS），将自回归模型与连续时间马尔可夫链（continuous-time Markov chain, CTMC）耦合，在单一共享损失函数下联合训练，能够在广泛的化学条件下以统一的绝对自由能尺度进行无偏估计。该方法在2D伊辛模型（Ising model）及AgPd和CuAu相图上与精确结果和参考相图匹配，且未出现模式坍缩（mode collapse）；相比基准模型SEGAL，FrOGS能正确恢复CuAu3相的稳定范围。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预测合金的热力学性质需要跨多种化学条件采样构型，并在统一的绝对尺度上恢复自由能。传统马尔可夫链蒙特卡洛（Markov chain Monte Carlo, MCMC）方法需在不同条件下分别模拟，且需借助热力学积分等辅助方法对齐自由能尺度，而现有离散神经采样器以反向KL散度为优化目标，往往具有模态偏向或系统性偏差。

Method: FrOGS将自回归模型与连续时间马尔可夫链耦合，在单一共享损失下联合训练。该混合采样器能够生成独立同分布（i.i.d.）的构型样本，提供配分函数（partition function）的无偏估计，并给出热力学可观测量的一致估计。单个模型在广泛的化学条件范围内训练，以产生统一绝对自由能尺度上的估计结果。

Result: FrOGS在2D伊辛模型上匹配精确有限尺寸结果，在AgPd和CuAu体系上复现参考相图，且未出现模式坍缩。与公开的自回归基线SEGAL相比，仅FrOGS能够恢复CuAu3相的稳定范围。

Conclusion: FrOGS作为一种混合离散神经采样器，通过单一模型在多种化学条件下提供一致且无偏的热力学估计，避免了传统MCMC方法多轮模拟和对齐的繁琐流程，且相比现有神经采样基线展现出更强的相图预测能力。

Abstract: Predicting the thermodynamic properties of an alloy requires sampling its configurations across many chemical conditions and recovering free energies on a common absolute scale. Markov chain Monte Carlo (MCMC) is the standard tool, but it requires separate simulations at different conditions, and auxiliary free-energy methods such as thermodynamic integration are used to place results on a common absolute scale. Modern discrete neural samplers typically use reverse KL divergence as the objective and can be mode-seeking or biased. We present Free energy Offering Generative Sampler (FrOGS), a hybrid discrete neural sampler that couples an autoregressive model to a continuous-time Markov chain (CTMC) to be trained jointly under a single shared loss. FrOGS draws i.i.d. configurations, returns an unbiased estimate of the partition function, and gives consistent estimates of thermodynamic observables. We train a single model across a wide range of chemical conditions to produce estimates on a common absolute free-energy scale. FrOGS matches exact finite-size results on the 2D Ising model and reference phase diagrams for AgPd and CuAu, without mode collapse. We additionally compare to SEGAL, a published autoregressive baseline, and find that only FrOGS recovers the stability range of the CuAu$_3$ phase.

</details>


### [194] [Physics-Informed Neural Network Surrogate for Oxygen Vacancy Dynamics in epitaxial $\mathrm{SrTiO_3}$ on Si memristors via Dynamic Spectral Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02966)
*Rodion Podorozhny,Nikoleta Theodoropoulou,Jelena Tešić*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出一种级联物理信息神经网络（cascaded PINN）架构，结合自定义的二阶第二类切比雪夫多项式谱优化器（DSO V2 Hybrid），用于模拟Pt/SrTiO₃/Si忆阻异质结构中的离子-电子漂移扩散输运。模型通过将势、载流子密度和空位输运分解为四个顺序训练的子神经网络，规避了超过10¹⁶的条件数，且在连续空间上严格满足泊松一致性，替代传统有限元求解器实现可微逆参数估计和线性时间推断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准物理信息神经网络（physics-informed neural networks, PINNs）在模拟半导体器件时受限于强数值刚性和多尺度空间差异，无法有效处理氧化物异质结构固有的问题。传统求解器（如COMSOL）虽能求解但难以进行可微逆参数估计，需要一种兼具物理一致性和计算效率的替代方案。

Method: 采用级联PINN架构，将势能、载流子密度和空位输运分解为四个顺序训练的子神经网络，每个子网络独立处理不同物理量以隔离数值尺度差异。配合自定义的第二类切比雪夫二阶多项式谱优化器（DSO V2 Hybrid）进行训练，在20 nm SrTiO ₃薄膜叠加380 μm硅衬底的跨尺度域上求解漂移-扩散方程，无需算子分裂（operator splitting）即可绕过超过10¹⁶的条件数。

Result: 训练后的代理模型在导电原子力显微镜（conductive-AFM）电流-电压滞回数据上复现实验结果的判定系数R²大于0.96，并在连续空间上严格保证泊松方程（Poisson equation）一致性。与传统的有限元求解器相比，PINN代理模型支持可微逆参数估计，且推断时间为线性复杂度。

Conclusion: 级联PINN结合谱优化策略可有效克服氧化物异质结构模拟中的多尺度数值挑战，为半导体器件的物理一致性建模提供了一种优于传统有限元方法的高效替代工具，尤其在逆问题求解和快速推断方面具有明显优势。

Abstract: Physics-informed neural networks (PINNs) offer a promising framework for modeling semiconductor devices, yet standard architectures struggle with severe numerical stiffness and multiscale spatial discrepancies inherent to oxide heterostructures. Here, we demonstrate a cascaded PINN architecture coupled with a custom second-order Chebyshev second kind polynomial spectral optimizer (DSO V2 Hybrid) to model ion-electronic drift-diffusion transport in Pt/SrTiO$_3$/Si memristive heterostructures across a 20 nm STO film on a 380 $μ$m Si substrate. By isolating potential, carrier density, and vacancy transport into four sequentially trained sub-neural-networks, our model circumvents condition numbers exceeding $10^{16}$ without operator splitting. The trained surrogate reproduces experimental conductive-AFM current-voltage hysteresis ($R^2 > 0.96$) while ensuring strict Poisson consistency across continuous space. Compared to conventional finite-element solvers (e.g., COMSOL), the PINN surrogate enables differentiable inverse parameter estimation and linear time inference.

</details>


### [195] [Battery open-circuit voltage is not purely chemical](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02968)
*Andrea Giudici,Christoph Pohl,Alberto Salvadori,Colin Please,Manuel Landstorfer,Jon Chapman*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文指出开路电压（OCV）并非电极材料的纯粹化学本征属性，而是依赖于力学状态与微观结构的化学-力学（chemo-mechanical）特性。通过有限元模拟与渐近分析（asymptotic analysis），作者证明颗粒膨胀与外载导致颗粒间接触产生压应力，进而改变插入离子的化学势，使OCV产生遵循赫兹接触标度的非线性修正，且该修正与颗粒排布有关。在完整电池中，电极通过共同堆叠应力发生机械耦合，因此电池OCV是整个电池架构的化学-力学属性，而非仅由化学性质决定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统观点将OCV曲线视为电极材料固有的化学性质，但这一认知不完整——在离子嵌入电池（ion-insertion battery）中，OCV还取决于力学状态与微结构。论文旨在揭示这一被忽视的力学耦合机制及其对OCV的影响。

Method: 采用有限元模拟（finite-element simulation）与渐近分析相结合的方法，研究颗粒溶胀（particle swelling）和外加载荷如何促进颗粒间接触，从而在颗粒间产生压应力并改变插入离子的化学势。模型中考虑了不同颗粒排列方式及全电池层面的堆叠应力耦合效应。

Result: OCV修正为非线性的，遵循赫兹接触标度（Hertzian contact scaling），且强烈依赖颗粒排布方式。因此，相同材料在不同电极微结构中可呈现不同的OCV曲线。在全电池中，正负极通过公共的堆叠应力在力学上相互耦合，进一步影响整体OCV行为。

Conclusion: 电池OCV并非仅由化学决定的材料属性，而是整个电池架构的化学-力学特性；电极微观结构和电池力学边界条件均可显著改变OCV响应。

Abstract: Open-circuit-voltage (OCV) curves are commonly treated as intrinsic chemical properties of electrode materials. However, this view is incomplete. In ion-insertion batteries, OCV also depends on mechanical state and microstructure. Using finite-element simulations and asymptotic analysis, we show that particle swelling and external loads promote particle--particle contact that generates compressive stresses, shifting the inserted-ion chemical potential. The OCV correction is nonlinear, follows Hertzian contact scaling, and depends on particle arrangement. Identical materials can therefore exhibit different OCV curves in different electrode microstructures. Furthermore, in full cells, electrodes are mechanically coupled through the common stack stress. Thus, cell OCV is a chemo-mechanical property of the entire battery architecture, not chemistry alone.

</details>


### [196] [IDMate: Window-resolved self-consistent-field proposal screening with finite-temperature response bounds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02979)
*Peng Kang,Da Wan,Shulin Bai,Zhen Li,Yu Liu,Lei Zheng,Li-Dong Zhao,Huibin Xu*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: IDMate是一个基于Kohn-Sham（科恩-沈）框架的筛选工具，用于对近似自洽场（self-consistent-field, SCF）态进行有限温度响应界约束和确定性参考映射恢复。该框架通过电荷中性分解与固定点保持的秩一干预检验动力学线性化，并基于Mermin自由能泛函的强凸性给出了密度矩阵的后验谱窗界。在3586次应力试验中，1942个提议被接受且无一超出窗口判据；一侧面95%零事件上限为8.4×10⁻⁴（按独立梯次计为4.2×10⁻²）。系统在三个参考构型中替代了十次参考映射评估并满足全部终端比较判据，但预定义跨谱系工作模型未达接受标准，生产实现成本为对照方案的7.87倍，论文据此明确了净加速的全空间与实现目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在近似自洽场计算中，需要一种能够有效筛选候选态并控制误差的方法，同时需验证筛选规则的可靠性并量化其在生产环境中的净加速效应。

Method: 提出IDMate框架：采用电荷中性因子分解将电子条件化与混频器放大分离；利用固定点保持的秩一干预在线性化动力学中检验各环节作用。对固定哈密顿量、精确电子数与温度，利用Mermin自由能泛函的强凸性导出选定谱窗内的后验密度矩阵界，并定义集成式筛选规则用于参考映射恢复。

Result: 在3586次应力试验中1942个提议被接受且无一超出窗口判据；独立假设下一侧95%零事件上限为8.4×10⁻⁴（相关梯次级为4.2×10⁻²）。系统在三个参考构型中替代十次参考映射评估，还原了参考评分轨迹并满足全部终点比较判据。窗口与全空间偏差相差6至11个数量级，全空间距离判别的描述性合并AUC为0.694（95%区间[0.544,0.831]）。但预定义跨谱系工作模型未达接受标准，生产实现成本为全参考对照的7.87倍。

Conclusion: IDMate建立了严格的可精确追踪的窗口界及经测试的接受或恢复规则，明确指出了为达成净加速所需的全空间与实现层面的改进目标。

Abstract: We introduce IDMate, a Kohn--Sham framework for screening approximate self-consistent-field (SCF) states with a finite-temperature response bound and deterministic reference-map recovery. A charge-neutral factorization separates electronic conditioning from mixer amplification, while fixed-point-preserving rank-one interventions test their roles in the linearized dynamics. For a fixed Hamiltonian, exact electron number, and temperature, strong convexity of the Mermin free-energy functional yields an a posteriori density-matrix bound in a selected spectral window. Across $3{,}586$ stress trials, $1{,}942$ proposals were accepted and none exceeded the window criterion. Under trial independence, the one-sided $95\%$ zero-event upper bound is $8.4\times10^{-4}$; treating the 70 correlated ladders as independent units gives $4.2\times10^{-2}$ per ladder. In three reference configurations, the integrated screening rule replaced ten reference-map evaluations, reproduced the reference-scoring trajectories, and satisfied every terminal comparison criterion. Window and full-space deviations differ by six to eleven orders of magnitude; full-space distance classifies the window decisions with a descriptive pooled AUC of $0.694$ (row-bootstrap $95\%$ interval $[0.544,\,0.831]$). A predefined cross-lineage work model does not meet its acceptance criterion, and serial proposal construction makes the production implementation $7.87$ times as costly under the predefined charged-cost accounting as its all-reference counterfactual. IDMate establishes a rigorous exact-trace window bound, a tested accept-or-recover rule, and explicit full-space and implementation targets for net acceleration.

</details>


### [197] [Interplay between Isomerization and Spin Crossover in 1D Fe-Indigo Coordination Polymers on Ag substrates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03041)
*Ritam Chakraborty,Hongxiang Xu,Biao Yang,Harshdeep Singh Chhabra,Joachim Reichert,Johannes V. Barth,Anthoula C. Papageorgiou,Shobhana Narasimhan*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文通过第一性原理密度泛函理论（DFT+U）研究Ag(111)和Ag(100)表面上实验合成的一维（1D）Fe-靛蓝配位聚合物链，揭示配体场异构化（isomerization）与自旋交叉（spin crossover）之间的相互作用，为异构化控制的自旋态切换提供了微观解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自旋交叉（SCO）化合物在低维体系中可提供可切换的分子功能，但一维SCO链由于能研究除典型八面体配体场以外的配体场，研究相对较少。作者旨在通过DFT+U计算理解实验合成的Fe-靛蓝配位聚合物链中配体场变化与自旋态切换的关联机制。

Method: 采用第一性原理DFT+U方法研究在Ag(111)和Ag(100)衬底上实验合成的一维Fe-靛蓝配位聚合物链；使用玩具模型（toy models）和自旋分辨的Fe 3d投影态密度（projected densities of states）解释结果，并通过自由站立链（freestanding-chain）计算揭示应变敏感的低自旋-高自旋（LS-HS）竞争。

Result: 在Ag(111)上，表面上异构化可在(N,O)螯合的trans构型与(N,N)/(O,O)螯合的cis构型之间转换。在0.66<U<3.00 eV区间内，能量最低的trans和cis解具有不同的自旋构型；在参考值U=1 eV时，优先的trans解为混合LS-LS-HS构型，而cis解为LS-LS-LS构型。在0.88<U<3.75 eV范围内重现了实验观察到的Ag(111)上偏好cis链、Ag(100)上偏好trans链的现象。

Conclusion: 这些结果为1D配位聚合物中由异构化控制的自旋态切换提供了微观物理解释，表明配体场变化（异构化）可作为调控一维自旋交叉体系自旋态的有效手段。

Abstract: Spin-crossover (SCO) compounds offer a route to switchable molecular functionality in reduced dimensions. However, one-dimensional (1D) SCO chains, which offer the possibility to study ligand fields other than the paradigmatic octahedral field, remain comparatively little studied. Here, we use first-principles density functional theory (DFT+$U$) to investigate Fe-indigo coordination-polymer chains synthesized experimentally on Ag(111) and Ag(100) substrates. These display a rich interplay between changes in ligand field (isomerization) and spin crossover. On-surface isomerization on Ag(111) interconverts (N,O)-chelated \textit{trans} configuration and (N,N)-/(O,O)-chelated \textit{cis} configurations at the Fe centers. The lowest-energy \textit{trans} and \textit{cis} solutions on Ag(111) have different spin configurations over the interval $0.66<U<3.00$~eV. At the reference value $U=1$~eV, the preferred \textit{trans} solution is the mixed LS--LS--HS configuration, whereas the preferred \textit{cis} solution is LS--LS--LS. The experimentally observed preference for \textit{cis} chains on Ag(111) and \textit{trans} chains on Ag(100) is reproduced for the range $0.88<U<3.75$~eV. To interpret these results, toy models and spin-resolved Fe $3d$ projected densities of states are used, while freestanding-chain calculations reveal a strain-sensitive LS--HS competition. These results provide a microscopic explanation for isomerization-controlled spin-state switching in a 1D coordination polymer.

</details>


### [198] [An Ultrathin Laterally Conductive Mesh Interphase Enables Spatially Extended Zinc Deposition for Aqueous Zinc Batteries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03176)
*Yirong Zhao,Xingyuan Chu,Bing Wu,Pavel Khavlyuk,Johannes Kresse,Yue Dong,Jingwei Du,Xinmei Song,Songshan Bi,Vlastimil Mazanek,Xia Wang,Xiaodong Li,Shilei Liu,Shuangying Wei,Jan Luxa,Junming Zhang,Zdenek Sofer,Alexander Eychmuller*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本研究开发了一种超薄二维金气凝胶网格中间相（AuMI），通过横向电子再分配与开放离子通道的协同作用，均一化锌/电解液界面的电场与电流分布，从而抑制枝晶生长，使Zn对称电池稳定运行3000小时（1 mA cm⁻²/1 mAh cm⁻²），全电池循环超过5000次后容量保持率达80.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 锌金属负极在循环过程中常因界面反应不均匀导致沉积不均匀和枝晶生长，现有的人工中间相（artificial interphase）虽能缓解副反应或调控成核，但难以在动态演化的锌/电解液界面调控界面电子/场分布以维持均匀沉积。

Method: 作者开发了Au网格中间相（Au mesh interphase, AuMI），这是一种超薄二维金气凝胶网络，其导电骨架可横向分散锌表面的电子传输，同时多孔网格保留Zn²⁺的离子通路；通过实验与模拟验证其对界面电场和电流分布的均一化效果。

Result: AuMI有效均一化界面电场与电流分布，促进均匀的锌沉积/剥离并抑制枝晶生长。AuMI Zn对称电池在1 mA cm⁻²/1 mAh cm⁻²条件下稳定运行3000小时，在10 mA cm⁻²/10 mAh cm⁻²条件下稳定运行1100小时；AuMI Zn||NVO全电池在1 A g⁻¹下循环超过5000次后容量保持率为80.8%。

Conclusion: 该研究强调了人工中间相中结合超薄结构、横向电子传输与开放的Zn²⁺通路对于实现稳定水系锌金属负极（aqueous Zn metal anodes）的重要性。

Abstract: Zn metal anodes often suffer from nonuniform interfacial reactions during cycling, resulting in uneven deposition and dendrite growth. Existing artificial interphases can mitigate side reactions or regulate nucleation, but rarely achieve regulation of the interfacial electron/field distribution to sustain uniform deposition at the evolving Zn/electrolyte interface. Here, we develop an Au mesh interphase (AuMI), an ultrathin two-dimensional Au aerogel network that couples lateral electron redistribution with open pathways for ions. During Zn plating/stripping, the conductive AuMI distributes electron transport across the Zn surface, while its porous mesh preserves Zn$^{2+}$ access, enabling more uniform interfacial reactions. Experiments and simulations show that AuMI homogenizes the interfacial electric field and current distribution, promotes more uniform Zn plating/stripping, and limits dendrite growth. As a result, this regulated interfacial reaction mode enables AuMI Zn symmetric cells to operate stably for 3000 h at 1 mA cm$^{-2}$/1 mAh cm$^{-2}$ and for 1100 h at 10 mA cm$^{-2}$/10 mAh cm$^{-2}$, while AuMI Zn||NVO (NaV$_3$O$_8$\cdot$1.5H$_2$O) full cells retain 80.8 % capacity after over 5000 cycles at 1 A g$^{-1}$. These findings highlight the importance of combining ultrathin architecture, lateral electron transport, and open Zn2+ access in artificial interphases for stable aqueous Zn metal anodes.

</details>


### [199] [Thermal history controls the optoelectronic response of lead halide perovskites through structure and dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03211)
*Milos Dubajic,Xia Liang,Johan Klarbring,Yang Lu,Thomas A. Selby,Erik Fransson,Philippe Holzhey,Benjamin M Gallant,Qichun Gu,Ganbaatar Tumen-Ulzii,Khasim Saheb Bayikadi,Isaiah Gilley,Martin v. Zimmermann,Christian Orr,Chwenhaw Liao,Josh S. Moon,Jacek Jasieniak,Makhsud Saidaminov,Michael P. Nielsen,Tom Wu,Stephen P. Bremner,Anita Ho-Baillie,Julia Wiktor,Paul Erhart,Steve Albrecht,Mercouri Kanatzidis,Henry J Snaith,Aron Walsh,Samuel D. Stranks*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本研究通过单晶X射线与中子漫散射、机器学习辅助分子动力学等手段，揭示卤化铅钙钛矿（lead halide perovskite）几乎所有组分中均存在以动态纳米域（dynamic nanodomains）形式呈现的平衡态局域结构涨落——尺寸仅数纳米的关联八面体倾斜（correlated octahedral tilts）会局域地破坏晶体学对称性。研究发现A位阳离子、卤素及热历史（thermal history）三重因素共同调控这些隐藏的局域无序结构，其中热历史可单独影响跨相变过程中的光致发光量子效率（photoluminescence quantum efficiency），应将其作为与组分并列的设计变量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 卤化铅钙钛矿的光伏效率已逼近细致平衡极限（detailed-balance limit），稳定性成为其应用的主要障碍；然而已有研究聚焦于固定均匀晶格内的离子运动，忽略了晶格动力学中隐藏的另一类内在结构不稳定性来源。

Method: 研究采用单晶X射线与中子漫散射（diffuse scattering）、机器学习辅助分子动力学（machine-learning-assisted molecular dynamics）、唯象八面体倾斜模型和超光谱光致发光（hyperspectral photoluminescence）等手段，系统测绘了含铯、甲基铵和甲脒的A位组分搭配Cl、Br、I及混合X位在各可达相中的结构行为。

Result: 几乎所有钙钛矿组分均存在平衡态局域结构涨落；A位阳离子决定纳米域的对称性、形状和各向异性，卤素调控动态无序度与相变序列，热历史则使名义相同的组分进入不同晶相并携带各自的隐藏局域序——加热速率单独即可改变MAPbI3跨相变的光致发光量子效率。

Conclusion: 由于这些相变落在器件工作温度范围内（从地面热循环到空间极端条件），热历史可贯穿制备与运行全程塑造局域结构及光电响应，应将其确立为与组分并列的设计变量。

Abstract: Lead halide perovskites are promising optoelectronic materials for photovoltaics, light emission and detection. Their efficiencies in PV now approach the detailed-balance limit, leaving stability as the principal barrier. The intrinsic instabilities studied to date centre on ionic motion within a fixed, homogeneous lattice. Here we identify a further source of intrinsic structural instability, hidden in the lattice dynamics. Mapping caesium, methylammonium and formamidinium-based compositions with Cl, Br, I and mixed X-sites through all accessible phases, using single crystal X-ray and neutron diffuse scattering, machine-learning-assisted molecular dynamics, a phenomenological octahedral tilt model and hyperspectral photoluminescence, we find that nearly every composition hosts equilibrium local structural fluctuations: dynamic nanodomains of correlated octahedral tilts, a few nanometres in size, that locally break the crystallographic symmetry. Three complementary levers control them. The A-site cation sets their symmetry, shape and anisotropy, from sparse, isotropic and tetragonal in formamidinium-based compositions to dense, anisotropic and orthorhombic in nominally cubic caesium-based ones, the most locally disordered we studied. The halide controls the dynamic disorder and the phase-transition sequence. Thermal history is the third: different ramp rates drive nominally identical compositions into distinct crystallographic phases, each with its own hidden local order. In MAPbI3, the heating rate alone changes the photoluminescence quantum efficiency across the phase transition. Because these transitions lie within device operating ranges, from terrestrial thermal cycling to the extremes of space, thermal history may shape the local structure, and hence the optoelectronic response, throughout fabrication and operation, establishing it as a design variable alongside composition.

</details>


### [200] [A Strategy Toward Room Temperature Topological Hall Effect via Local Moment Magnetism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03223)
*Karthik Rao,Kevin Allen,Yuxiang Gao,Arushi,Sanu Mishra,Birender Singh,Kenneth S. Burch,Liangzi Deng,Shanta R. Saha,Johnpierre Paglione,Minseong Lee,Vivien Zapf,Emilia Morosan*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种在高温下实现拓扑自旋织构（topological spin textures）的策略，通过识别在室温附近或室温下有序的稀土（rare earth, R）磁体来实现。以六方Gd₅Pb₃（居里温度T_C = 285 K）为例，通过拓扑霍尔效应（topological Hall effect）证实其在100–200 K区间出现拓扑自旋织构，比已知其他稀土基层系高一个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 局域矩体系中的拓扑自旋织构因其大磁矩、强自旋轨道耦合（spin-orbit coupling, SOC）和高可调性而具有重要技术应用前景，但寻找在室温附近稳定存在的新型自旋织构是发挥其应用潜力的关键瓶颈。现有稀土基体系中的拓扑自旋织构出现温度过低，亟需新的策略来提高其稳定温度。

Method: 提出一种通过识别在室温或近室温有序的稀土磁体来实现高温拓扑自旋织构的策略，并在候选材料六方Gd₅Pb₃（T_C = 285 K）中验证该策略的可行性。利用拓扑霍尔效应（THE）作为拓扑自旋织构的实验指示，并结合体系的SOC、各向异性交换（anisotropic exchange）、几何阻挫（geometric frustration）及磁相互作用进行分析。

Result: 在Gd₅Pb₃中观测到拓扑霍尔效应出现在T = 100–200 K区间，该温度范围比此前其他稀土基体系报道的结果高出一个数量级，表明拓扑自旋织构在该材料中能在更高温度下稳定存在。

Conclusion: 研究结果为探索SOC、各向异性交换、几何阻挫和磁相互作用在稳定拓扑自旋织构中的作用提供了机会，并为在稀土基磁体中于近室温条件下实现拓扑自旋织构提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Topological spin textures in local moment systems hold great promise for technological applications due to their large magnetic moments, strong spin-orbit coupling (SOC), and high tunability. Finding new spin textures that are stable near room temperature is paramount to maximizing their potential for applications. Here, we provide a strategy for realizing topological spin textures at high temperatures by identifying rare earth ($R$) magnets ordering at or near room temperature. We demonstrate the feasibility of this strategy in one of these magnets, hexagonal Gd$_5$Pb$_3$, which orders at $T_C$ = 285 K. The indication for topological spin textures comes from topological Hall effect (THE), which, in Gd$_5$Pb$_3$, occurs between $T$ = 100 - 200 K, an order of magnitude higher temperature than in other reported $R$-based systems. Our results present an opportunity to explore the role of SOC, anisotropic exchange, geometric frustration, and magnetic interactions in stabilizing topological spin textures, and provide a pathway toward realizing them near room temperature in $R$-based magnets.

</details>


### [201] [Grain boundary evolution in nanoparticles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03307)
*Manoj Settem,Pranav Kumar,Ajeet K. Srivastav*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文通过原子模拟（atomistic simulations）研究纳米颗粒中低能晶界（grain boundary, GB）演化的原子尺度机制，识别出两种基本原子位移——柱状位移（column shift, C）和螺旋位移（screw shift, S）——它们以不同方式组合，驱动晶界迁移、结构转变及结（junction）演化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纳米颗粒中的晶界演化是驱动结构重排和催化的关键过程，但受额外自由度、形状弛豫及自由表面终止等条件影响，其原子尺度机制仍较块体材料缺乏理解，因而需要阐明晶界演化的基本过程。

Method: 该方法利用原子模拟技术，聚焦纳米颗粒结构演化中持久出现的紧凑低能晶界——Σ3（共格孪晶界）和Σ11——系统追踪这些晶界及其结演化过程中重复出现的原子位移模式。

Result: 该文识别出柱状位移和螺旋位移两种基本原子位移，二者经由不同原子路径发生并以多种组合驱动晶界迁移、结构转变和结演化；同一初始与终态晶界构型可通过不同原子路径连接，且柱状位移可表现为整体位移或经由不连续扭结（disconnection kinks）实现；即使在晶界特征变化（如Σ11向Σ3转变）时，C和S仍保持可识别性。

Conclusion: 理解这些晶界演化的基本原子过程有助于发展控制纳米颗粒中晶界演化的策略，进而实现晶界结构工程（grain boundary engineering）。

Abstract: Grain boundary evolution is a key dynamical process that enables structural rearrangements in nanoparticles and drives them towards low energy configurations. Grain boundaries can also enhance catalytic properties, making it important to understand the elementary processes underlying their evolution for grain boundary engineering in nanoparticles. Compared with bulk materials, nanoparticles have additional rotational and translational degrees of freedom, can accommodate structural changes through shape relaxation, and grain boundaries terminate at free surfaces. However, the atomic-scale mechanisms of GB evolution under these less constrained conditions remain comparatively less understood than in bulk materials. Here, using atomistic simulations, we focus on low energy $\Sigma3$ (coherent twin boundary) and $\Sigma11$ grain boundaries, which are among the persistent compact GB structures that emerge during nanoparticle structural evolution. We identify two fundamental atomic displacements, column shift (C) and screw shift (S), that recur during the evolution of these grain boundaries and their junctions. These displacements occur through different atomic pathways and combine in different ways to generate grain boundary migration, structural transformations, and junction evolution. In particular, the same initial and final grain boundary configurations can be connected through different atomic pathways, and a column shift can occur either as a full shift or through disconnection kinks. C and S remain identifiable even when the grain boundary character changes, for example during a $\Sigma11$ to $\Sigma3$ transformation. An understanding of these elementary GB processes can help identify strategies to control grain boundary evolution and thereby engineer GB structures in nanoparticles.

</details>


### [202] [Polaron Self-Trapping Rates from First Principles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03336)
*Mark E. Turiansky,Joel B. Varley,Audrius Alkauskas,Chris G. Van de Walle*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文基于第一性原理方法，系统计算了多种材料中极化子（polaron）自陷（self-trapping）的形成时间，覆盖11种材料体系，计算寿命跨越7个数量级（10⁻¹至10⁶ ps），与实验观测一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 极化子形成类似于点缺陷或杂质处的非辐射载流子捕获过程，但其形成时间此前缺乏从第一性原理出发的系统计算方法。本文旨在建立严格的第一性原理形式体系（formalism），从基本原理出发确定小空穴极化子或电子极化子的形成所需时间。

Method: 采用基于Koopmans相容杂化泛函（Koopmans compliant hybrid functional）的精确、完全第一性原理方法。自陷速率分解为两个成分的乘积：非辐射捕获系数（nonradiative capture coefficient），采用一维近似评估；以及极化子位点最大密度（maximum density of polaron sites），其物理机制基于超胞（supercell）中的有限尺寸相互作用进行阐明。

Result: 将方法应用于已知承载空穴极化子的多种技术相关材料（Ga₂O₃、Al₂O₃、BeO、KBr、MgO、NaCl、SiO₂、SnO₂、TiO₂、ZnO）以及金红石TiO₂中的电子极化子。此外，研究新兴半导体金红石GeO₂，发现极化子形成可能阻碍其p型导电。计算的自陷寿命跨越7个数量级（10⁻¹至10⁶ ps），与可获得实验数据吻合。

Conclusion: 所发展的第一性原理方法能够可靠预测极化子形成时间，为理解固体中载流子局域化与弛豫动力学提供了细致的洞见，并揭示了极化子形成过程对新兴半导体导电性能的潜在影响。

Abstract: Polaron formation, also known as self-trapping, is a process akin to nonradiative carrier capture at point defects or impurities. In this work, we develop the formalism to determine how long it takes to form a small hole or electron polaron from first principles. We employ an accurate, fully first-principles approach based on a Koopmans compliant hybrid functional. The self-trapping rate is the product of two components: the nonradiative capture coefficient, which we evaluate using a one-dimensional approximation, and the maximum density of polaron sites, whose physics we elucidate based on finite-size interactions present in supercells. We apply our methodology to several technologically relevant materials known to host hole polarons, Ga$_2$O$_3$, Al$_2$O$_3$, BeO, KBr, MgO, NaCl, SiO$_2$, SnO$_2$, TiO$_2$, and ZnO, and to an electron polaron in rutile TiO$_2$. We also study an emerging semiconductor, rutile GeO$_2$, where we find that polaron formation could hamper $p$-type conductivity. The calculated self-trapping lifetimes span 7 orders of magnitude, from $10^{-1}$ to $10^6$~ps, in agreement with experiments where available, and providing detailed insight into the dynamics of carrier localization and relaxation in solids.

</details>


### [203] [Interplay of B-Site Off-Centering and Molecular Orientations in the Mixed Hybrid Perovskite MAGe1xSnxI3](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03510)
*Erik Fransson,Apinya Ngoipala,Oskar Öjstedt,Maths Karlsson,Paul Erhart,Julia Wiktor*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本研究利用机器学习势（machine-learned interatomic potential）驱动的大规模分子动力学模拟，系统绘制了无铅杂化钙钛矿MAGe1-xSnxI3在全组分范围内的结构行为图谱，揭示了Ge位偏心占据、Sn位八面体倾斜与反极性偏心之间的竞争关系及其对甲基铵取向序的调控机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: B位混合是调控卤化物钙钛矿（halide perovskite）性质的常用策略，但在无铅体系中，B位混合会引入倾斜与偏心运动的竞争，理解全组分范围内的结构演化规律是材料设计的关键前提。

Method: 采用机器学习势驱动的分子动力学（molecular dynamics）模拟，对MAGe1-xSnxI3体系进行大规模计算，跨越从纯Ge到纯Sn的全组分范围，系统表征其结构行为、B位偏心状态与MA取向序随组分和温度的变化。

Result: MAGeI3具有强烈的极性B位偏心，其幅度在立方转变前几乎保持不变，同时伴有随加热逐渐减弱的MA取向序；MASnI3则表现为八面体倾斜与较弱且以反极性为主的偏心共存。合金化后Ge类行为在低Sn含量下保持，至约65% Sn后才转变为Sn类行为。高温相中B位阳离子保持局域偏心但方向无序；在Ge富集侧，畸变的无机框架将软MA取向景观约束至有限的方向子集。

Conclusion: 研究表明，无机亚晶格的组分可直接调控杂化钙钛矿中有机分子的取向序，为通过B位工程实现对无铅杂化钙钛矿结构与性质的定制提供了新的设计思路。

Abstract: B-site mixing is a common strategy for tuning properties of halide perovskites. In the lead-free system MAGe1-xSn_xI3, it brings tilting and off-centering into competition. Using large-scale molecular dynamics driven by a machine-learned interatomic potential, we map the structural behavior across the full composition range. MAGeI3 exhibits strong polar B-site off-centering that remains nearly constant up to the cubic transition, together with methylammonium (MA) orientational order that weakens progressively on heating. By contrast, MASnI3 combines octahedral tilting with weaker, predominantly antipolar off-centering. Ge-like behavior persists upon alloying and gives way to Sn-like behavior only beyond roughly 65 % Sn. In the high-temperature phases, the B-site cations remain locally off-centered but directionally disordered. On the Ge-rich side, the distorted inorganic framework biases the soft MA orientational landscape toward a restricted set of preferred directions. This coupling shows how the composition of the inorganic sublattice can tune molecular ordering in lead-free hybrid perovskites.

</details>


### [204] [Electron-phonon coupled hydrodynamics in semimetal TaAs_2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03517)
*Shan Jiang,Wei Xie,Xiaokang Li,Kamran Behnia,Zengwei Zhu*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文通过在具有不同杂质水平的TaAs2晶体上开展电学和热学输运测量，在5至15 K温区发现了电子-声子双流体（e-ph bifluid）行为的实验证据。晶格热导率呈现快于T³的温度依赖并伴随非单调且依赖纯度的声子平均自由程，表明出现了声子Poiseuille流（Poiseuille flow）特征；同时强电子-声子耦合抑制了弹道输运区间的出现，声子介导的动量交换显著增强了Wiedemann-Franz定律的破坏，并导致量子寿命与输运寿命之间两个数量级的偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 耦合电子-声子流体动力学（coupled electron-phonon hydrodynamics）是凝聚态输运研究的新兴前沿方向，此前流体动力学修正主要针对单一载流子（电子或声子）体系展开，缺乏实验中电子与声子耦合流体的直接证据，因而亟需在真实材料中系统检验该效应。

Method: 研究对不同杂质水平的TaAs2单晶进行电学输运与热学输运（thermoelectric transport）联合测量，重点在5–15 K温区分析晶格热导率的温度依赖关系、声子平均自由程（phonon mean free path）的纯度依赖性、Wiedemann-Franz定律的偏离程度，以及晶格热导率中的量子振荡。

Result: 实验观测到：5–15 K温区内晶格热导率呈快于T³的温度依赖，对应非单调且依赖杂质水平的声子平均自由程；强电子-声子耦合抑制弹道输运的出现，并以晶格热导率中的量子振荡（quantum oscillations）为佐证；声子介导的电子-声子动量交换导致Wiedemann-Franz定律显著破坏，量子寿命与输运寿命相差约两个数量级，这些特征共同构成了半金属中电子流体动力学（electron hydrodynamics）的指纹证据。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，在低温声子波长与费米波长（Fermi wavelength）相匹配的电子-声子耦合优化半金属中，电子库与声子库之间的动量和能量交换频率可与其各自库内部的交换频率相当，从而在5–15 K温度窗口同时浮现电子和声子的流体动力学行为。

Abstract: Hydrodynamic corrections to diffusive transport can arise when momentum-conserving collisions between quasiparticles become prominent, and they have been documented for both electrons and phonons. An emerging frontier topic is coupled electron-phonon (e-ph) hydrodynamics. Here, through electrical and thermal transport measurements on TaAs2 crystals with different impurity levels, we document the emergence of an e-ph bifluid in the temperature window of 5 to 15 K. Within this range, the lattice thermal conductivity exhibits a faster-than-T^3 temperature dependence, as a consequence of non-monotonic and purity-dependent phonon mean free paths, a signature of phonon Poiseuille flow. However, strong e-ph coupling impedes the emergence of a ballistic regime. This is corroborated by the observation of quantum oscillations in the lattice thermal conductivity. Prominent phonon-mediated momentum exchange between electrons amplifies the violation of the Wiedemann-Franz law and yields a two-order-of-magnitude discrepancy between quantum and transport lifetimes, a signature of electron hydrodynamics in semimetals. Our results imply that in semimetals with optimized e-ph coupling, thanks to matching between the cryogenic phonon wave?length and the Fermi wavelength, momentum and energy flow between the electron and phonon reservoirs as frequently as within each reservoir.

</details>


### [205] [Orbital-Free DFT-Assisted Machine-Learned Molecular Dynamics for Electric-Field-Driven Ionic Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03518)
*Yusuke Nishiya,Hiroya Nakata,Yosuke Harada,Yu-ichiro Matsushita*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于无轨道密度泛函理论（OFDFT）辅助的机器学习分子动力学方法，利用机器学习势评估原子间作用力，并从OFDFT计算获取依赖局部环境的原子电荷，从而实现外加电场下低计算成本的分子动力学模拟。在β-Li3PS4体相和S8/Li3PS4异质结构中验证了该方法，成功模拟了电场驱动的离子迁移和界面电荷态变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电场驱动的离子输运是固态电池等电化学系统的关键过程，但现有方法要么依赖Kohn-Sham密度泛函理论（KSDFT）分子动力学而计算成本高昂，要么在电场下需要额外训练材料特定的电荷预测模型。作者旨在开发一种无需额外训练即可处理电场下离子迁移和界面电荷演化的通用方法。

Method: 该方法将OFDFT获得的原子电荷（通过Bader划分电子密度得到）乘以电场矢量并叠加到机器学习势所计算的原子间作用力上。分子动力学仿真在外加电场条件下进行，通过场独立（field-independent）的机器学习势与OFDFT电荷相结合来驱动离子运动。

Result: 在Li3PS4体相中，OFDFT获得的Li电荷和PS4单元总电荷与KSDFT结果一致。在S8/Li3PS4异质结构中，Li离子在约170 ps内从Li3PS4区域迁移至富S区域，伴随这一迁移，原本形成S8环的S原子平均Bader电荷从近中性变为负电荷；与KSDFT的代表性界面结构对比也定性再现了该结果。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，利用通用机器学习势无需训练额外的材料特定电荷预测模型即可处理电场驱动的离子输运和界面电荷态变化，为低压成本电场模拟提供了可行的途径。

Abstract: We propose an orbital-free density functional theory (OFDFT)-assisted machine-learned molecular dynamics method in which field-independent interatomic forces are evaluated using a machine-learned potential and atomic charges that depend on the local environment are obtained from OFDFT calculations. The atomic charges obtained by Bader partitioning of the OFDFT electron density are multiplied by the electric-field vector and added to the forces from the machine-learned potential. This approach enables molecular dynamics simulations under an electric field at a lower computational cost than Kohn-Sham DFT (KSDFT)-based molecular dynamics.
  As a proof of concept, the method was applied to beta-Li3PS4 bulk and an S8/Li3PS4 heterostructure under periodic boundary conditions. For Li3PS4 bulk, OFDFT yielded Li charges and a total charge of the PS4 unit consistent with those obtained using KSDFT. In the heterostructure, Li ions migrated from the Li3PS4 region into the S-rich region within a simulation time of approximately 170 ps. Accompanying this migration, the mean Bader charge of the S atoms that initially formed S8 rings changed from nearly neutral to negative. A comparison with KSDFT for a representative interfacial structure also confirmed that the negative charging of the S atoms upon the arrival of Li and the magnitude of the Li charges were qualitatively reproduced. These results demonstrate the possibility of treating field-driven ionic transport and changes in interfacial charge states using a universal machine-learned potential without training an additional material-specific charge-prediction model.

</details>


### [206] [SALTED: a symmetry-adapted machine-learning program for predicting electron-densities in molecules and materials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03576)
*Zekun Lou,Alan M. Lewis,Théophane Bernhard,Lukas Seifert,Agustin Salcedo,Florian Kleemiss,Mariana Rossi,Andrea Grisafi*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: SALTED是一个开源Python包，基于输入原子坐标和物种种类，利用高斯过程回归学习的对称自适应扩展来预测分子和凝聚相体系中的量子力学电子密度，并支持学习其对电场的一阶响应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对分子与凝聚相体系中量子力学电子密度的机器学习预测需求，开发一个开源、可移植且能与现有电子结构程序接口的工具。

Method: 采用原子中心线性分解的电子密度表示方法，保证跨不同原子构型的可移植性；学习算法基于高斯过程回归（Gaussian process regression）的对称自适应扩展，在小数据量（small-data）条件下尤其高效；并通过矢量场核函数（vector-field kernel functions）实现对外加电场下电子密度一阶响应的学习。

Result: SALTED可与基于原子轨道的电子结构程序（CP2K、FHI-aims、PySCF）直接接口生成参考数据并训练模型；在实际计算工作流中已展示出广泛应用价值，包括极化矢量与极化率张量的计算、QM/MM分子动力学中库仑力的准确评估，以及大规模二维材料的电子结构研究。

Conclusion: SALTED作为一个开源工具，通过对称自适应的机器学习方法在电子密度及其电场响应预测方面展现了良好的适用性，已在多个计算化学与材料科学场景中得到验证。

Abstract: SALTED provides an open-source Python package for machine learning the quantum-mechanical electron density, $n(\mathbf{r})$, in molecular and condensed-phase systems based on input atomic coordinates and species. The program adopts a linear atom-centered decomposition of the electron density, which makes it highly transferable across diverse atomistic configurations sharing similar chemical environments. Because of this representation choice, SALTED is naturally interfaced with state-of-the-art electronic-structure programs based on atomic orbitals, namely CP2K, FHI-aims, and PySCF, from which reference electron-density data can be generated and used to train a model. The learning algorithm is based on a symmetry-adapted extension of Gaussian process regression, making SALTED especially efficient in small-data regimes. Thanks to the implementation of vector-field kernel functions, SALTED can also learn the first-order response of the electron density to applied electric fields, $\partial n(\mathbf{r})/\partial \mathbf{E}$. The application of SALTED within computational workflows has already shown its utility in a wide variety of contexts, including the calculation of polarization vectors and polarizability tensors, the accurate evaluation of Coulomb forces in QM/MM molecular-dynamics simulations, and electronic-structure studies of large-scale 2D materials.

</details>


### [207] [High-Throughput Computational Discovery of Inverted Resistive Switching in Two-Dimensional Materials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03578)
*Sanchali Mitra,Arnab Kabiraj,Benjamin W. J. Chen,Han Zhang,Haiyu Meng,Shi-Jun Liang,C. S. Lau,Lei Shen,Lain-Jong Li,Kah-Wee Ang,Yee Sin Ang*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本研究通过结合密度泛函理论、机器学习分子动力学与量子输运模拟的高通量计算框架，对约2900种可剥离单层材料进行空位介导电阻开关筛选，识别出17种热稳定的原子级忆阻器（atomristor）候选材料，并将其划分为两类机制截然不同的开关行为，包括此前未报道的倒置开关现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前原子级忆阻器设计仅限于MoS2和h-BN等少数材料，制约了功能多样性与设计灵活性。作者旨在通过计算筛选扩展可用的二维单层材料空间，并揭示新的开关机制以推动忆阻器与神经形态计算的发展。

Method: 采用高通量计算框架，集成密度泛函理论（density functional theory）、机器学习分子动力学（machine-learning molecular dynamics）和量子输运模拟（quantum transport simulations），对约2,900种可剥离单层材料进行空位介导电阻开关（vacancy-mediated resistive switching）筛选，并结合迁移势垒计算分析金（Au）原子迁移行为。

Result: 识别出17种热稳定候选材料，分为两类。第一类（Class 1）如GaS，Au在本征空位处吸附引入导电态使绝缘体从高阻态（HRS）转为低阻态（LRS）；第二类（Class 2）如BiOCl，表现为倒置开关：空位释放电子使费米能级进入导带而原生处于LRS，Au吸附重新局域化载流子并将费米能级推回带隙，实现LRS到HRS的转换。量子输运模拟确认两种机制，迁移势垒计算表明电极-二维层间距是调控Au迁移与电阻窗口的关键参数。

Conclusion: 该工作扩展了原子级忆阻器的材料版图，并提出互补性开关（complementary switching）作为多功能存储与神经形态硬件的设计范式。

Abstract: Atomristors, non-volatile resistive switching devices based on two-dimensional (2D) monolayers, are promising building blocks for energy-efficient memory and neuromorphic computing. However, their design remains restricted to a few materials such as MoS2 and h-BN, limiting functional diversity and design flexibility. Here, a high-throughput computational framework combining density functional theory, machine-learning molecular dynamics, and quantum transport simulations screens about 2,900 exfoliable monolayers for vacancy-mediated resistive switching, identifying 17 thermally stable candidates in two mechanistically distinct classes. In Class 1 monolayers, such as GaS, Au adsorption at the native vacancy introduces conducting states, switching the insulating monolayer from a high- to a low-resistance state (HRS-to-LRS). Class 2 monolayers, comprising ionically bonded metal oxyhalides and nitrohalides such as BiOCl, exhibit previously unreported inverted switching. Vacancy-released electrons delocalize and push the Fermi level into the conduction band, placing the device natively in the LRS; Au adsorption re-localizes these carriers and returns the Fermi level to the gap, driving LRS-to-HRS switching. Quantum transport simulations confirm both mechanisms, while migration-barrier calculations identify the electrode-2D separation as a key parameter governing Au migration and the resistance window. These findings expand the atomristor landscape and establish complementary switching as a design paradigm for multifunctional memory and neuromorphic hardware.

</details>


### [208] [Gd-4f Exchange Splitting and Mo-4d Crystal-Field Redistribution in Gd/W Co-doped La2Mo2O9: A DFT+U Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03717)
*Amogh U. Lanjewar,Saurabh Shiwankar,Smita Acharya*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究采用自旋极化密度泛函理论并加Hubbard U修正，系统研究了La2Mo2O9（LAMOX）材料本征态及Gd/W共掺杂体系La1.6Gd0.4Mo1.7W0.3O9的电子结构。结果表明Gd/W共掺杂引入了强交换分裂的Gd-4f能带（主次自旋分裂约1011 eV），显著增强了Mo-4d晶场分裂，并降低了价带顶O-2p贡献。这些电子结构特征为掺杂诱导的相稳定和氧化物离子电导率抑制提供了原子尺度的微观解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LAMOX材料是面向中温固体氧化物燃料电池（solid oxide fuel cells）的有前景的氧化物离子导体，但其实际应用受到一级单斜-立方相变（alpha→beta）的限制；理解掺杂如何调控电子结构以稳定相和影响离子输运，是材料优化的关键。

Method: 采用含Hubbard U修正的自旋极化密度泛函理论（spin-polarized DFT+U）计算本征La2Mo2O9与Gd/W共掺杂LAMOX的电子结构，对局域化Gd-4f态施加在位库仑修正，并通过投影态密度（projected density of states）分析各轨道的电子贡献与分裂特征。

Result: 本征LAMOX为O-2p/Mo-4d电荷转移型氧化物，La对带边几乎无贡献；Gd/W共掺杂引入强交换分裂的Gd-4f轨道（主次自旋分离约1011 eV），将Mo-4d晶场分裂增大三倍，并使价带顶O-2p贡献从78%降至66%。这些电子结构变化与实验观察到的晶格收缩、MoO拉曼模式软化及共掺杂系列中氧化物离子电导率的非单调演变一致。

Conclusion: 最重掺杂水平下显著的晶场分裂与局域Gd磁性为离子电导率的抑制提供了微观电子层面解释；该结果建立了掺杂诱导相稳定与离子输运的原子级电子结构框架，为改进固体氧化物燃料电池材料提供了设计原则。

Abstract: La2Mo2O9 (LAMOX) is a promising oxide-ion conductor for intermediate-temperature solid oxide fuel cells, but its practical application is limited by a first-order monoclinic-to-cubic (alpha to beta) phase transition. Here, we investigate the electronic structure of pristine La2Mo2O9 and the Gd/W co-doped composition La1.6Gd0.4Mo1.7W0.3O9 using spin-polarized density functional theory with an on-site Hubbard correction for the localized Gd-4f states. The projected density of states reveals that pristine La2Mo2O9 is an O-2p/Mo-4d charge-transfer oxide in which La contributes negligibly near the band edges. Gd/W co-doping introduces a strongly exchange-split Gd-4f manifold with a majority-minority separation of approximately 1011 eV, substantially redistributes the Mo-4d electronic states through a threefold increase in crystal-field splitting, and reduces the O-2p contribution near the valence-band maximum from 78% to 66%. These electronic signatures are consistent with the experimentally observed lattice contraction, MoO Raman-mode softening, and the non-monotonic evolution of oxide-ion conductivity across the co-doped series. In particular, the pronounced crystal-field splitting and localized Gd magnetism at the highest doping level provide a microscopic electronic explanation for the observed suppression of ionic conductivity. The present results establish an atomistic electronic-structure framework for understanding dopant-induced phase stabilization and oxide- ion transport in Gd/W co-doped LAMOX electrolytes, providing design principles for improved solid oxide fuel cell materials.

</details>


### [209] [Multimodal and Multiscale Interrogation of a Mechanically Tough Glass Forming Copper-Based Metal-Organic Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03725)
*Mounir El Skafi,Guo-Qiang Li,Sophie R. Thomas,James D. Taylor,Mark Frogley,Gianfelice Cinque,Marc Pignitter,Michael Reithofer,Jia-Min Chin,Sebastian Henke,Jin-Chong Tan*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文采用溶剂-凝胶法（sol-gel process）合成了铜基金属有机框架（metal-organic framework, MOF）Cu(Im)2，并在240 °C以上通过熔融淬火（melt-quenching）制得MOF玻璃。通过多模态、多尺度的表征手段系统研究了纳米晶和玻璃的结构、热学与力学性能，发现所得玻璃具有约10 GPa的高弹性模量和迄今报道的MOF玻璃中最高断裂韧性（K_1c ~ 0.5 MPa m^1/2）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MOF玻璃作为新兴材料，其机械鲁棒性和抗裂性对其实际加工与成型至关重要，但目前对MOF玻璃的综合性能尤其是力学性能的理解仍有限，需要系统性的多技术联合表征来阐明其结构-性能关系。

Method: 采用溶剂-凝胶法合成Cu(Im)2纳米晶，并在240 °C以上加热熔融后淬火制成玻璃。利用X射线衍射（X-ray diffraction）、原子力显微镜（atomic force microscopy）和电子显微镜表征形貌与尺寸；热重分析（thermogravimetric analysis）与差示扫描量热法（differential scanning calorimetry）用于研究熔融过程；同步辐射对分布函数（pair distribution function）分析验证熔融后框架结构保持情况；近场红外纳米光谱（nearfield infrared nanospectroscopy）提供局部化学结构信息；纳米压痕（nanoindentation）用于力学性能测试。

Result: 表征显示晶体熔融形成玻璃后框架结构得以保持。力学测量表明所得MOF玻璃表现出约10 GPa的高弹性模量，断裂韧性约为0.5 MPa·m^1/2，为迄今文献报道的MOF玻璃中最高值。

Conclusion: 该工作在保持机械鲁棒性与抗裂性的前提下，为MOF基材料的加工与成型这一新兴领域提供了关键支撑，证明Cu(Im)2玻璃兼具结构完整性和优异的综合力学性能。

Abstract: A copper-based metal-organic framework, Cu(Im)2, was synthesized using a sol-gel process and subsequently melt-quenched into glass upon heating above 240 °C. In this paper, we present a multimodal, multiscale interrogation of the MOF nanocrystals and the resulting glasses. Structural characterization using X-ray diffraction, atomic force microscopy, and electron microscopy was performed to understand the morphology and size of the synthesized nanocrystals and glasses. Thermogravimetric analysis and differential scanning calorimetry were employed to understand the melting process of the crystals to form the glass. Synchrotron pair distribution function analysis was performed to verify that the framework structure is maintained upon melting, and nearfield infrared nanospectroscopy provided insight into the local chemical structure of the materials. The mechanical characterization via nanoindentation revealed that the resulting glasses exhibit an appreciably high elastic modulus (~10 GPa) and the highest fracture toughness (K_1c ~ 0.5 MPa m^1/2) yet reported for MOF glasses. This development is central to the emerging field of MOF-based materials processing and shaping, while upholding mechanical robustness and resistance to cracking.

</details>


### [210] [Ion-Engineered Insulator-to-Semiconductor Transition in Natural 2D Biotite](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03757)
*Dipanwita Mitra,Raphael B. de Oliveira,Guilherme S. L. Fabris,Debkanta Ghosh,AyonJyoti Karmakar,Raphael M. Tromer,Marcelo L. Pereira Junior,Douglas S. Galvão,Chandra Sekhar Tiwary,Prasanta Kumar Datta*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文通过受控NaOH处理，将液相剥离的黑云母纳米片转化为可调谐的二维半导体，实现绝缘体-半导体转变，并系统表征了其光学、电学及超快动力学特性，提出化学缺陷与离子工程可作为将天然层状矿物转化为电子可调二维材料的有效途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 天然层状硅酸盐作为一类储量丰富但尚未充分开发的二维材料，其绝缘特性严重限制了功能化应用。研究者旨在开发一种化学策略，将这些天然矿物转化为具有电子可调性质的半导体材料，以拓展其在光电子学与超快光子学领域的应用潜力。

Method: 采用可控NaOH处理对液相剥离的黑云母纳米片进行化学改性，结合结构与化学分析（表征晶格畸变、层间重组、羟基化及Na+-K+部分交换）、光学/电学测量及超快瞬态吸收光谱，辅以密度泛函理论（density functional theory, DFT）计算，系统研究绝缘体-半导体转变的微观机制与载流子动力学。

Result: NaOH处理使黑云母发生绝缘体到半导体的转变，机制涉及Na掺入、缺陷生成和局部结构重构。光学带隙从约5.2 eV降至3.2–3.5 eV，引入约1.12–1.17 eV的低能跃迁，吸收从约221 nm扩展至约280和975 nm。电流可达近10 μA，呈现非线性输运与活化载流子传导。超快动力学显示载流子冷却（0.16–0.38 ps）后伴随快（35–60 ps）和长寿命（336–491 ps）的陷阱介导复合弛豫，高载流子密度下存在热声子瓶颈效应。

Conclusion: 化学缺陷与离子工程是将储量丰富的天然层状矿物转化为电子可调二维材料的强大策略，所得材料在新型光电子学与超快光子技术中具有潜在应用前景。

Abstract: Naturally occurring layered silicates offer an abundant yet unexplored class of 2D materials, but their insulating nature limits their functional utility. Here, we demonstrate a chemical strategy that transforms liquid-phase-exfoliated biotite nanosheets into a tunable 2D semiconductor through controlled NaOH treatment. The resulting insulator-to-semiconductor transition originates from Na incorporation, defect generation, and local structural reconstruction while largely preserving the layered framework. Structural and chemical analyses reveal lattice distortion, interlayer reorganization, hydroxylation, and partial Na+-K+ exchange, establishing the origin of the electronic restructuring. This transformation broadens the optical response, shifting the approximately 221 nm absorption toward approximately 280 and 975 nm, reducing the optical bandgap from approximately 5.2 to 3.2-3.5 eV, and introducing low-energy transitions at approximately 1.12-1.17 eV. Electrical measurements reveal nonlinear transport with currents reaching close to 10 microA, demonstrating activated carrier conduction. Ultrafast transient absorption reveals pronounced excited-state absorption, with carrier cooling (0.16-0.38 ps) followed by fast (35-60 ps) and long-lived (336-491 ps) relaxation associated with trap-mediated recombination. Fluence-dependent dynamics reveal a hot-phonon bottleneck at elevated carrier densities. Together with density functional theory calculations, these results establish chemical defect and ion engineering as a powerful route for converting naturally abundant layered minerals into electronically tunable 2D materials for emerging optoelectronic and ultrafast photonic technologies.

</details>


### [211] [Heesch Nodal Lines in Inadmissible Achiral Antiferromagnets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03772)
*Xing-Yao Guo,Chung-Yuen Chan,Zi-Ting Sun,Kam Tuen Law*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文发现了一类新型磁性拓扑材料——Heesch节线反铁磁体（Heesch nodal line antiferromagnets），其具有连接相反拓扑荷Weyl点的长双简并节线，并伴随大数量二维无质量Dirac锥及特殊的三阶非线性反常霍尔效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期发现的Heesch Weyl半金属具有被钉扎在高对称动量点上的Weyl点，由于钉扎效应，相反拓扑荷的Weyl点无法像常规Weyl半金属那样相遇并湮灭，作者希望探究这一限制下在镜像或旋转反演（rotoinversion）对称性恢复时系统呈现的拓扑结构。

Method: 作者通过对称性分析表明，当镜像或旋转反演对称性恢复使点群变为非手性（achiral）时，连接相反拓扑荷Weyl点的长双简并节线（Heesch nodal lines, HNLs）得以出现；随后采用第一性原理计算验证了代表性共线（collinear）与非共线（noncollinear）反铁磁材料MnTe、CrSb和Mn₃GaN中HNLs的存在，并研究了应变破坏对称性时的效应。

Result: HNLs在与节线相交的布里渊区平面上产生大量二维无质量Dirac锥；相当一部分HN反铁磁体的最低阶非零非线性反常霍尔效应从三阶开始。第一性原理计算证实了MnTe、CrSb和Mn₃GaN中存在HNLs。应变打破不可容许对称性后，HNL的双简并被解除，关联的Dirac锥打开能隙，从而为实现可观的反常霍尔效应提供了途径。

Conclusion: 所有不具有宇称-时间（parity-time）对称性的不可容许反铁磁体都是拓扑的，它们要么是Heesch Weyl反铁磁体，要么是Heesch节线反铁磁体。

Abstract: Recently, a new class of Weyl semimetals in antiferromagnets named Heesch Weyl semimetals was discovered, which have inadmissible chiral magnetic point group symmetries (inadmissible magnetic point groups are incompatible with ferromagnetic order) and distinctive surface Fermi arcs. In Heesch Weyl semimetals, the Weyl points are pinned at high symmetry momenta with two-dimensional irreducible corepresentations in the Brillouin zone. As the Weyl points are pinned, the Weyl points with opposite topological charges cannot emerge or be brought together for creation and annihilation as in conventional Weyl semimetals. In this work, we show that when mirror or rotoinversion symmetries are restored so that the point group becomes achiral, long doubly degenerate lines connecting Weyl points with opposite topological charges emerge. We call these lines the Heesch nodal lines (HNLs) and their host materials the Heesch nodal line antiferromagnets. HNLs result in a large number of two-dimensional massless Dirac cones for planes intercepting the HNLs in the Brillouin zone. Moreover, a large subset of the HNL antiferromagnets has the special property that the lowest nonvanishing order of the nonlinear anomalous Hall effect starts with the third order. First-principles calculations on representative collinear and noncollinear antiferromagnets, such as MnTe, CrSb, and Mn$_3$GaN, confirm our predictions on the presence of HNLs. When the inadmissible symmetry is broken by strain, the double degeneracy of the HNLs is lifted and the associated massless Dirac cones are gapped out, providing a route to realizing sizable anomalous Hall effects in antiferromagnetic crystals. We conclude that all inadmissible antiferromagnets without parity-time symmetry are topological. They are either Heesch Weyl antiferromagnets or Heesch nodal line antiferromagnets.

</details>


### [212] [Exciton multipolarity controls coherent and squeezed phonons in van der Waals heterostructures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03795)
*Indrajit Maity,Arash A. Mostofi,Johannes Lischner,Ángel Rubio*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文通过MoSe₂/WSe₂多层膜体系的原子级模拟，揭示激子多极性（exciton multipolarity）可作为调控光致声子从相干态到压缩态的设计原则，并证明垂直电场可实现压缩态与相干态之间的开关切换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管光激发相干声子已被广泛观测到，但缺乏控制其特性的设计原则。二维材料及其异质结构为调控声子提供了有力平台，亟需建立系统的设计框架。

Method: 对交替堆叠的MoSe₂/WSe₂多层膜进行详细的原子模拟（atomistic simulations），分析层间呼吸模式在偶极激子与四极激子激发下的声子态演化，并通过外加垂直电场调控激子多极性实现声子态切换。

Result: 偶极激子以线性耦合产生相干态，四极激子以二次耦合产生压缩态；外加垂直电场可将四极激子转换为偶极激子，使声子态从压缩切换为相干。在三层WSe₂/MoSe₂/WSe₂中，1.04 THz呼吸模式在零场下为压缩态，施加垂直电场后切换为相干态。声子态可通过超快X射线或电子衍射直接探测，亦可通过瞬态反射率间接探测。

Conclusion: 该工作为皮秒时间尺度上晶格与电子动力学的超快调控开辟了新途径，对太赫兹量子声子学（quantum phononics）、纳米光子技术及量子噪声极限传感具有潜在应用意义。

Abstract: Photoexcitation-driven changes in the electronic distribution displace atoms, generating coherent phonons on ultrafast timescales. Two-dimensional (2D) materials and their heterostructures offer a powerful platform for engineering these phonons. Yet, despite the widespread observation of photoexcited coherent phonons, a design principle for controlling their character remains elusive. Here, using detailed atomistic simulations of multilayers of alternating MoSe$_2$ and WSe$_2$, we reveal exciton multipolarity as a design principle for tuning photoinduced phonons from coherent to squeezed. These phonons are interlayer breathing modes, with dipolar excitons coupling linearly to generate coherent states and quadrupolar excitons coupling quadratically to produce squeezed states. Moreover, an out-of-plane electric field enables switch-like control, converting quadrupolar excitons into dipolar excitons and switching the phonon state from squeezed to coherent. For example, in trilayer WSe$_2$/MoSe$_2$/WSe$_2$, the photoexcited 1.04-THz breathing mode switches from a squeezed state at zero field to a coherent state under an applied vertical field. Experimentally, these phonon states can be directly probed by ultrafast X-ray or electron diffraction and indirectly through transient reflectivity. Our results open new avenues for ultrafast control of lattice and electronic dynamics on picosecond timescales, with implications for THz quantum phononics, nanophotonic technologies, and quantum-noise-limited sensing.

</details>


### [213] [Performance of Nanoring-based Transparent Conductors: a Computational Investigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03876)
*Gijs Vanoppen,Jef Hooyberghs,Wim Deferme,Bart Cleuren*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本研究通过计算模拟系统考察金属纳米环网络（metallic nanoring networks）作为柔性透明电极的电气导电性能、光学性能及电损伤下的失效行为，发现多个参数组合可优于当前广泛使用的氧化铟锡（ITO）透明电极，并观察到网络退化曲线的普适性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金属纳米环网络有望用作柔性透明电极，在太阳能电池、触摸屏和显示器等领域具有广泛应用前景；然而，对其电气与光学性能以及电损伤导致失效的机理仍缺乏系统的计算理解。

Method: 采用计算模拟方法，将纳米环自身的电阻以及环之间接触点（junction）的电阻均纳入考量，分别研究(i)五个参数对电学薄层电阻（sheet resistance）与光学透过率的影响，以及(ii)电损伤下网络的退化行为。

Result: 研究发现多种参数组合在性能上优于目前应用最广泛的透明电极材料氧化铟锡；在电损伤条件下，纳米环网络会形成一条平行于施加电压端子的裂纹，且不同填充因子（filling factor）的网络展现出相同的退化曲线，体现了普适性效应。

Conclusion: 金属纳米环网络在特定参数组合下可作为高性能柔性透明电极的候选材料，同时其电损伤退化行为表现出与填充因子无关的普适规律，可为可靠性与耐久性设计提供理论依据。

Abstract: Metallic nanoring networks can serve as promising flexible transparent electrodes. These materials are crucial components in a wide range of applications, including solar cells, touchscreens and displays. In this work, a computational investigation considers in detail (i) the electrical conductance and optical performance of nanoring networks and (ii) the breakdown of these networks due to electrical damage. The electrical resistance of both the nanorings and the contacts between the rings (junctions) is taken into account. In part (i), the effects of 5 parameters on the electrical sheet resistance and optical transparency are presented. It is shown that several parameter combinations achieve better performance in comparison to indium tin oxide, currently the most widely used transparent electrode. In part (ii), due to electrical damage, the nanoring systems display the formation of a crack, running parallel to the vertical terminals, where a voltage difference is applied. The network degradation is measured by its sheet resistance, and a universal effect is observed: networks with varying filling factors exhibit the same degradation profile.

</details>


### [214] [Experimentally constrained modeling of the Pockels response of KNbO3 and KTaNbO3](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03888)
*Virginie de Mestral,Lorenzo Bastonero,Petr Bednyakov,Fedir Borodavka,Simon Mellaerts,Tetyana Ostapchuk,Alex Pescaru,Jiri Hlinka,Mathieu Luisier*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文通过结合密度泛函理论（density functional theory）级别的从头计算与远红外测量，发现谐波近似配合半局域交换关联泛函无法准确捕获铌酸钾和钽铌酸钾（KTN）中主导电光系数r51的软横光学Slater模，并提出用实验测量的模频率替代计算值以显著改进Pockels响应的预测精度。研究还首次从远红外反射谱中提取了KTN的TO Slater模频率，凸显了KTN电光调制器作为锂铌酸盐和BTO技术替代方案的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电光氧化物钙钛矿中的软模（soft-mode）对其Pockels响应至关重要，而KTN表现出比BTO大2.5倍的内在Pockels响应，有望用于高性能电光应用。然而，现有理论计算方法无法准确预测这一关键响应，因此需要发展更精确的预测策略。

Method: 采用密度泛函理论（DFT）框架下的从头计算结合谐波近似与半局域交换关联泛函来描述KTN和铌酸钾的软TO Slater模；同时利用远红外（far-IR）反射率测量提取此前未见报道的KTN TO Slater模频率，并将实测频率替代计算值以约束和改进Pockels响应的理论预测。

Result: 研究发现谐波近似结合半局域交换关联泛函无法准确捕获铌酸钾和KTN的软TO Slater模频率及相应的r51 Pockels系数；采用实验测量的模频率后，Pockels响应的预测精度显著改善。研究还首次报道了KTN的TO Slater模频率，表明KTN基电光调制器兼具更低的能耗和更小的器件尺寸优势。

Conclusion: KTN有望成为标准锂铌酸盐（lithium niobate）和BTO电光技术的可行替代方案，其在器件能耗和占用面积方面具有潜在优势；同时，实验约束模频率的方法为准确预测电光性能提供了有效的替代途径。

Abstract: The soft-mode of electro-optic (EO) metal-oxide perovskites plays a critical role in determining their Pockels responses. This is the case of potassium tantalate niobate (KTN), which exhibits an intrinsic Pockels response 2.5 times larger than that of state-of-the-art barium titanate (BTO), highlighting its potential for high-performance EO applications. By combining ab initio calculations at the density-functional theory level and far-IR measurements, we reveal that the harmonic approximation combined with semi-local exchange-correlation functionals fails to accurately capture the soft transverse optical (TO) Slater mode of both potassium niobate and KTN, which dominates the technologically relevant r51 Pockels coefficient. Replacing the calculated mode frequency with its measured value provides an experimentally constrained approach that substantially improves the predicted Pockels response. For that purpose, the previously unreported TO Slater-mode frequency of KTN is extracted from far-IR reflectivity measurements. The results emphasize the potential of KTN-based EO modulators as an alternative to standard lithium niobate and BTO technologies, with a potentially lower energy consumption and device footprint.

</details>


### [215] [Hierarchical automation of scanning probe microscopy through agentic orchestration and algorithmic control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04015)
*Boris N. Slautin,Sheryl L. Sanchez,Aidan Swanger,Yu Liu,Gerd Duscher,Vladimir V. Shvartsman,Mahshid Ahmadi,Sergei V. Kalinin*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种分层自主实验架构，将智能体（agentic）推理与确定性算法（deterministic algorithms）分离：智能体负责解释科学意图、构建实验表征并选择高层动作，而算法负责数值分析、坐标选择、验证与物理执行。该架构在压电力显微镜（piezoresponse force microscopy）中实现验证，能自主完成从科学问题到实验终止的完整流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管智能体人工智能（agentic artificial intelligence）能解释开放式目标并动态调整策略，但物理实验中许多任务（如定量分析、优化、空间定位、验证和仪器执行）更适合以确定性或算法化方式处理以保证可靠性。作者由此提出需要一种将智能体推理与确定性操作分离的层次化架构。

Method: 提出一种层次化自主实验架构（hierarchical architecture for autonomous experimentation）：智能体组件负责解释科学意图、构建任务相关的实验表征（experimental representations）、评估累积证据并选择高层动作；确定性算法组件则执行数值分析（numerical analysis）、坐标选择（coordinate selection）、验证（validation）和物理执行（physical execution）。该架构在压电力显微镜中得到实现，处理从多通道成像构建空间描述符、选择并分析局部滞回测量、自适应调整光谱波形到终止条件判定的全链路任务。

Result: 该系统从关于局部畴结构与极化翻转（polarization switching）关系的广泛科学问题出发，实现了全自主实验流程。自主轨迹还识别出极化态与畴壁邻近性之间的混淆关系（confounding relationship），并发现所请求的对比度在可用视野内并非独立表征。实验在附加测量不再提供新证据时自动终止。

Conclusion: 研究展示了一种科学自主性的实现路径：智能体（agents）决定需要哪些证据，而算法（algorithms）决定如何以可重复且满足已验证物理约束的方式获取这些证据。

Abstract: Rapid advances in agentic artificial intelligence enable scientific systems to interpret open-ended objectives, combine heterogeneous information, invoke specialized tools, and revise experimental strategies as evidence accumulates. However, physical experimentation also contains many tasks for which agentic reasoning provides little advantage and can reduce reliability. Quantitative analysis, optimization, spatial targeting, validation, and instrument execution are often better posed as deterministic or algorithmic operations with explicit objectives and verifiable outputs. Here, we introduce a hierarchical architecture for autonomous experimentation that separates these roles. Agentic components interpret scientific intent, construct task-dependent experimental representations, evaluate accumulated evidence, and select high-level actions, whereas deterministic algorithms perform numerical analysis, coordinate selection, validation, and physical execution. We implement this architecture in piezoresponse force microscopy. Starting from a broad scientific question concerning the relation between local domain structure and polarization switching, the system constructs spatial descriptors from multichannel imaging, selects and analyzes local hysteresis measurements, adapts the spectroscopy waveform, and terminates the experiment when additional measurements cease to provide new evidence. The autonomous trajectory also identifies a confounding relationship between polarization state and domain-wall proximity and recognizes that the requested contrast is not independently represented within the available field of view. These results demonstrate a route toward scientific autonomy in which agents determine what evidence is required while algorithms determine how that evidence is acquired reproducibly and within validated physical constraints.

</details>


### [216] [Synthesis and Characterization of Compositionally Complex (Gd/Ho/Er/Dy)2Zr2O7 Thin Film Combinatorial Library](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04074)
*Dalton A. Pearl,Jade Holliman,Reece Emory,Joshua Safin,Aditya Raghavan,Kamyar Barakati,Andrew H. Jones,Ethan A. Scott,Jack C. Lasseter,Adam Corrao,Daniel Olds,Bruce Ravel,Sergei K. Kalinin,Patrick E. Hopkins,Katharine Page,Philip D. Rack*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本研究通过组合磁控反应溅射法制备了(GdDyHoEr)2Zr2O7组合薄膜材料库，并采用稳态热反射映射等方法进行多模态表征，发现热导率最小值出现在Dy/Gd富集象限，其与最大晶格参数（而非最大阳离子尺寸无序度）对应，揭示了萤石晶格容纳组分复杂性的方式比阳离子尺寸无序更能描述热输运行为，建立了适用于成分复杂陶瓷的高通量发现工作流。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 加速下一代热障材料（thermal barrier materials）的发现，需要开发具有改进的热机械性能和相稳定性的新型陶瓷材料，而传统试错法效率低下，亟需高通量合成与表征方法。

Method: 采用组合磁控反应溅射（combinatorial magnetron reactive sputtering）以稀土/锆合金靶材制备(GdDyHoEr)2Zr2O7组合薄膜材料库，随后对四组分成分空间进行结构、化学与热性能的系统表征，并将各组分特征与稳态热反射（steady state thermoreflectance）热输运测量结果相关联。

Result: 热导率在Dy/Gd富集象限出现显著最小值，该最小值既不对应等原子成分，也不对应预测的最大阳离子尺寸无序区域，而是对应实验观测到的最大晶格参数（尽管偏离Vegard类化学平均），且与晶粒尺寸和全图案微观应变无关。

Conclusion: 萤石晶格容纳组分复杂性的方式比阳离子尺寸无序度本身提供了更具信息量的热输运描述符。该工作建立了组合薄膜合成与多模态表征的高通量工作流，可快速识别成分复杂陶瓷中此前难以企及的结构-性能关联。

Abstract: High-throughput synthesis and characterization of novel ceramic materials with improved thermomechanical properties and phase stability are needed to accelerate the discovery of next-generation thermal barrier materials. A combinatorial thin film material library of (GdDyHoEr)2Zr2O7 were created via combinatorial magnetron reactive sputtering with rare-earth/zirconium alloy targets. Structural, chemical, and thermal property characterization mapping across the four component composition space was performed and correlated with thermal transport measurements. Steady state thermoreflectance mapping identifies a pronounced minimum in thermal conductivity within the Dy/Gd-rich quadrant. This minimum does not coincide with either the equiatomic composition or the region predicted to exhibit maximum cation size disorder. Instead, it corresponds to the largest experimentally observed lattice parameter, despite deviating from Vegard-like chemical averaging, and is independent of grain size and whole-pattern microstrain. These observations suggest that the way the fluorite lattice accommodates compositional complexity, rather than cation size disorder alone, provides a more informative descriptor of thermal transport. Overall, this work establishes a high-throughput workflow for combinatorial thin-film synthesis and multimodal characterization, enabling the rapid identification of previously inaccessible structure-property relationships in compositionally complex ceramics.

</details>


### [217] [Strengthened Silicate Glasses by Residual Stress: Depth of Compression and Surface Flaws Stability Conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04109)
*Guglielmo Macrelli*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本研究采用基于应力强度因子（stress intensity factor）K_I的断裂力学框架，替代传统许用应力准则，利用权函数法（Weight Function Method, WFM）对硅酸盐玻璃在非均匀残余应力场和外载作用下的二维连续表面裂纹进行稳定性评估，并建立零风险与中等风险两类设计方法下的裂纹稳定性判据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 硅酸盐玻璃在严苛服役环境中需要精确评估其机械载荷与表面摩擦条件下的结构强度，而玻璃强度由表面缺陷和微裂纹控制而非固有材料属性，因此需要基于断裂力学的评估方法来替代传统许用应力标准。

Method: 采用断裂力学框架，以临界应力强度因子K_IC（快速断裂）和阈值应力强度因子K_Ith（延迟静疲劳失效）为基本材料极限，通过权函数法（Weight Function Method, WFM）评估二维连续表面裂纹在非均匀内部残余应力场和外载作用下的应力强度因子。

Result: 建立了针对零风险与中等风险两类设计方法的裂纹稳定性判据，并评估了外部机械应力与热应力同内部残余应力场之间的相互作用与叠加效应。

Conclusion: 该研究为硅酸盐玻璃制品的验收提供了一种基于断裂力学而非传统许用应力准则的结构强度评估途径，可更准确地反映玻璃强度受表面缺陷控制的物理本质。

Abstract: The application of silicate glasses in severe service environments requires a precise evaluation of structural strength under mechanical loads and surface tribological conditions. Because glass strength is governed by surface flaws and microcracks rather than being an intrinsic material property, residual surface compression fields, balanced by interior tensile zones, are widely implemented to inhibit flaw opening. Rather than relying on conventional allowable stress criteria to establish product acceptance, this study adopts a fracture mechanics framework based on the stress intensity factor KI and fundamental material limits: the critical stress intensity factor KIC for rapid fracture and the threshold stress intensity factor KIth for time-delayed static fatigue failure. Using the Weight Function Method (WFM), KI is evaluated across generic surface flaw depths for two-dimensional continuous (2D-Continuous) surface cracks subjected to non-uniform internal residual stress fields and external loads. Flaw stability criteria are established for both zero-risk and moderate-risk design methodologies. Finally, the interaction and superposition of externally applied mechanical and thermal stresses with internal residual stress fields are evaluated.

</details>

### [218] [First Measurement of Near-Threshold J/ψ Photoproduction on the Neutron](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03579)
*R. Tyson,D. G. Ireland,B. McKinnon,P. Chatagnon,V. Kubarovsky,S. Stepanyan,A. G. Acar,P. Achenbach,J. S. Alvarado,M. J. Amaryan,W. R. Armstrong,H. Avakian,L. Barion,M. Bashkanov,M. Battaglieri,F. Benmokhtar,A. Bianconi,A. S. Biselli,A. Biswas,M. Bondi,F. Bossù,S. Boiarinov,K. -Th. Brinkmann,W. J. Briscoe,W. K. Brooks,J Bryce,N. L. Bucuru,S. Bueltmann,V. D. Burkert,T. Cao,D. S. Carman,A. Celentano,H. Chinchay,G. Ciullo,E. W. Cline,P. L. Cole,M. Contalbrigo,A. D'Angelo,N. Dashyan,R. De Vita,A. Deur,S. Diehl,C. Dilks,C. Djalali,R. Dupre,H. Egiyan,M. Ehrhart,A. El Alaoui,L. El Fassi,L. Elouadrhiri,C. Fanelli,M. Farooq,S. Fegan,I. P. Fernando,E. Ferrand,A. Filippi,C. Fogler,S. Frantzen,K. Gates,G. P. Gilfoyle,D. I. Glazier,R. W. Gothe,Y. Gotra,S. Gramigna,B. Gualtieri,K. Hafidi,H. Hakobyan,M. Hattawy,T. B. Hayward,D. Heddle,T. Hellstern,M. Hoballah,M. Holtrop,Y. Ilieva,E. L. Isupov,H. S. Jo,S. Joosten,M. Kerr,H. T. Klest,V. Klimenko,A. Kripko,C. Lama,L. Lanza,S. Lee,P. Lenisa,D. Martiryan,V. Mascagna,A. Mehta,R. G. Milner,R. Milton,M. Mirazita,V. Mokeev,E. F. Molina Cardenas,C. Munoz Camacho,P. Nadel-Turonski,T. Nagorna,K. Neupane,S. Niccolai,M. Osipenko,P. Pandey,M. Paolone,L. L. Pappalardo,R. Paremuzyan,E. Pasyuk,C. Paudel,S. J. Paul,L. Polizzi,J. Poudel,Y. Prok,A. Radic,K. Ramage,M. Ripani,M Ronayette,P. Rossi,A. A. Rusova,S. Schadmand,A. Schmidt,Y. G. Sharabian,E. V. Shirokov,S. Shrestha,E. Sidoretti,N. Sparveris,I. I. Strakovsky,S. Strauch,M. Tenorio,F. Touchte Codjo,M. Ungaro,S. Vallarino,C. Velasquez,L. Venturelli,H. Voskanyan,E. Voutier,Y. Wang,D. P. Watts,U. Weerasinghe,X. Wei,N. Wuerfel,Z. Xu,Z. W. Zhao,M. Zurek*

Main category: nucl-ex

TL;DR: 本文报告了在托马斯·杰斐逊国家加速器设施使用CLAS12探测器、以液态氘为靶的首次近阈值J/ψ光产生在束缚中子上的总截面与t微分散面测量，并同时获得了束缚质子的非相干截面，实现了束缚核子间以及与自由质子结果的首次直接比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近阈值J/ψ光产生被认为是探测核子胶子结构（gluonic structure）的敏感探针，但目前缺乏束缚中子上的测量数据，且对束缚核子中胶子结构是否受核介质修饰的认知尚不明确——这为理论框架提供了关键的实验检验机会。

Method: 实验使用CLAS12探测器搭配液态氘靶，测量了近阈值下J/ψ光产生在中子和束缚质子上的总截面及t微分散面；数据在矢量介子主导（Vector Meson Dominance）、全息QCD（holographic QCD）以及基于广义部分子分布（GPD）的框架下进行了解释。

Result: 测量获得了束缚中子和束缚质子的非相干总截面及t微分散面，实现了二者的首次直接比较；与自由质子结果的对比暗示核介质中核子胶子结构可能存在修饰，同时束缚中子与束缚质子截面的比较为近阈值J/ψ产生机制提供了新的约束。

Conclusion: 该数据为近阈值J/ψ产生机制提供了关键的新约束，是确立J/ψ光产生作为核子胶子结构探针的重要步骤；结果（在提取假设和产生机制假设下）提示核介质可能对束缚核子的胶子结构产生修饰。

Abstract: We report the first measurement of the total and t-differential cross sections for near-threshold J/$ψ$ photoproduction on the neutron, obtained with the CLAS12 detector at the Thomas Jefferson National Accelerator Facility. The measurements, performed using a liquid-deuterium target, also provide the incoherent total and t-differential cross sections on the bound proton, enabling the first direct comparison of near-threshold J/$ψ$ photoproduction on bound protons and neutrons. Interpreted within Vector Meson Dominance, holographic QCD, and GPD-based frameworks, the data allow for the investigation of the gluonic structure of bound nucleons. A comparison with free proton results reveals hints of modifications to the gluon structure of nucleons in the nuclear medium, within the assumptions of the extraction and on the underlying production mechanism. The comparison of bound neutron and bound proton cross sections provides new constraints on the near-threshold J/$ψ$ production mechanism, which is crucial in order to establish J/$ψ$ photoproduction as a probe of the nucleon's gluonic structure.

</details>

### [219] [The stealthiest particle configurations in 8 and 24 dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03121)
*Alex Cohen,Henry Cohn,Maryna Viazovska*

Main category: math.MG

TL;DR: 本文证明了$E_8$格和Leech格分别是$\mathbb{R}^8$和$\mathbb{R}^{24}$中密度为1的等距不变、局部平方可积点过程中“隐身性”（stealthiness）最优且唯一的候选者，并证明任何已知的20维格点在该意义下都不如一个20维周期构型“隐身”，后者是Vardy的20维球堆积的形式对偶（formal dual）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 隐身点过程（stealthy point process）在衍射、通信和超均匀材料等领域具有重要应用，寻找在给定密度下结构因子（structure factor）尽可能小（即最“隐身”）的点过程是一个基础而具有挑战性的问题。

Method: 采用等距不变性与局部平方可积性等数学约束下的点过程优化框架，并结合谱分析（spectral analysis）与形式对偶（formal duality）工具进行证明。

Result: 证明了$E_8$格和Leech格在$\mathbb{R}^8$与$\mathbb{R}^{24}$中分别是唯一的“隐身性最优”点过程；同时构造了一个20维周期构型，其隐身性优于所有已知的同密度20维格点，该构型是Vardy的20维球堆积的形式对偶。

Conclusion: 该结果建立了$E_8$格与Leech格在“隐身性”意义上的最优性和唯一性，并将证明技术推广到球堆积与能量最小化问题，表明相关数学工具具有更广泛的应用潜力。

Abstract: We show that the $E_8$ and Leech lattices generate the stealthiest isometry-invariant, locally square-integrable point processes of intensity $1$ in $\mathbb{R}^8$ and $\mathbb{R}^{24}$, respectively, and that they are the unique stealthiest processes. We also apply the proof technique to sphere packing and energy minimization, and we construct a $20$-dimensional periodic configuration that is stealthier than any known $20$-dimensional lattice with the same particle density. This periodic configuration is a formal dual of Vardy's $20$-dimensional sphere packing.

</details>

### [220] [Universal scaling of fluctuations and correlations across the superfluid transition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03433)
*Paul Paquiez,Géraud Dupuy,Maxime Allemand,Henri Coquinot,Tommaso Roscilde,Nicolas Dupuis,Adam Rançon,Thomas Chalopin,David Clément*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 本论文在超冷晶格玻色气体（ultracold lattice Bose gases）中实验验证了连续相变附近普遍标度律（universal scaling）的预测：利用不同熵下测得的序参数累积量（order-parameter cumulants），提取出仅依赖系统的两个尺度因子，并证明这些因子分别支配序参数分布与两点关联函数的标度函数，同时确定了三个临界指数β、γ和ν。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 宇称不变性指出，连续相变普适性不仅体现在幂律和临界指数上，更体现在所有可观测量均服从普遍标度函数，且微观细节仅由两个系统特定尺度因子捕获。此前这一更基本的预测尚缺乏直接实验验证，尤其是在量子多体体系中跨多个可观测量的同时检验。

Method: 实验在处于超流转变的超冷晶格玻色气体中开展。研究者将不同熵条件下测得的序参数累积量进行数据坍塌，提取出两个系统特定的尺度因子，并利用这些因子验证序参数分布和两点关联函数是否服从相同的标度函数，进而提取临界指数β、γ和ν。

Result: 实验成功验证了多个可观测量同时满足普遍标度律：由序参数累积量坍塌得到的尺度因子，同样支配序参数分布与两点关联函数的标度行为。基于这些标度函数的实测，确定了三个临界指数β、γ和ν的数值。

Conclusion: 该研究在量子多体系统中首次实验证明，多个可观测量可同时满足普遍标度律。这一结果深化了对相变临界现象普适性的理解，并为量子多体系统中标度函数框架的普适适用提供了直接实验依据。

Abstract: The critical behavior of a system near a continuous phase transition is commonly characterized by power laws and a set of universal critical exponents. Yet universality at phase transitions has even more fundamental manifestations. It dictates that all observables follow universal scaling functions, which may extend beyond simple power laws. Furthermore, microscopic details are captured entirely by only two system-specific scale factors, that rescale any measured quantity onto its universal form. In this work, we report the experimental validation of this prediction in ultracold lattice Bose gases undergoing the superfluid transition. We extract the scale factors by collapsing the order-parameter cumulants measured at varying entropies. We show that these factors specify scaling functions governing both the order-parameter distribution and two-point correlations. By measuring these scaling functions, we also determine three critical exponents $β$, $γ$ and $ν$. Our results provide an experimental demonstration that universal scaling is simultaneously obeyed by multiple observables in a quantum many-body system.

</details>


### [221] [Finite-temperature mass gap and quench dynamics of mobile impurities in a Fermi gas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03656)
*Baihua Gong*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 本文将质量间隙（mass gap）模型推广到有限温度，通过结合Lee–Low–Pines变换与自洽Hartree–Fock退耦研究Fermi气体中移动杂质的淬火动力学，发现有效质量间隙在特征温度T*处按(T*-T)^{1/2}关闭，对应极化子（polaron）与分子准粒子的热熔化；Ramsey响应中的长时振荡恰好在此温度以上消失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前针对Fermi气体中移动杂质的质量间隙描述仅局限于零温且未涉及动力学问题，需要将该框架推广到有限温度并研究其动力学行为，以统一热力学与动力学上极化子形成的特征。

Method: 论文采用Lee–Low–Pines变换（Lee–Low–Pines transformation）结合自洽Hartree–Fock退耦（self-consistent Hartree–Fock decoupling）处理反冲诱导相互作用，并在该框架下运用泛函行列式方法（functional-determinant approach）研究淬火动力学。

Result: 有限温度下有效质量间隙满足自洽方程Δ(T)=2U_F·tanh[Δ(T)/4k_BT]，其非零解存在于T* = U_F/(2k_B)以下，并以(T*-T)^{1/2}方式关闭；此关闭被识别为极化子与分子准粒子的热熔化（thermal melting）的均场（mean-field）信号。Ramsey响应S(t)的长时量子拍（quantum beats）振荡恰在T*以上消失。

Conclusion: 该工作将极化子形成的热力学与动力学指纹统一归结为一个依赖于温度的均场参数Δ(T)，揭示了有效质量间隙的关闭温度T*同时控制着热力学相变与动力学振荡行为的临界点。

Abstract: Recently, a mass-gap description of mobile impurities in a Fermi gas was introduced, which connects Anderson's orthogonality catastrophe for static impurities to the quasiparticle picture of Fermi polarons through a recoil-induced energy gap in the fermionic dispersion. That description, however, was restricted to zero temperature and did not address dynamics. Here we generalize the mass-gap model to finite temperature by combining the Lee--Low--Pines transformation with a self-consistent Hartree--Fock decoupling of the recoil-induced interaction, and we study the quench dynamics within this framework using the functional-determinant approach. At finite temperature the effective mass gap obeys the self-consistency equation $Δ(T)=2U_F\tanh[Δ(T)/4k_B T]$, with $U_F=k_F^2/2M$ and $M$ the impurity mass. This equation admits a nonzero solution below the characteristic temperature $T^*=U_F/(2k_B)$ and closes as $(T^*-T)^{1/2}$. We identify this closing as the mean-field signature of the thermal melting of the polaron and molecule quasiparticles. Computing the Ramsey response $S(t)$ after a sudden quench of the impurity--fermion interaction, we find that its long-time oscillations---quantum beats between the bound and in-gap states---disappear precisely above $T^*$. Our work ties the thermodynamic and dynamical fingerprints of polaron formation to a single temperature-dependent mean-field parameter.

</details>


### [222] [Three-observable measurement of the critical velocity in a paraxial superfluid of light flowing past a mobile impurity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03948)
*T. Aladjidi,M. Baker-Rasooli,T. D. Ferreira,A. Bramati,M. Albert,Q. Glorieux,P. -É. Larré*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 本文报道了在$^{87}\text{Rb}$热蒸汽中利用双波长光学方案实现的光学超流体（paraxial superfluid of light）流经可移动杂质时的临界速度测量。通过同时监测涡旋数、水动力力和位移速度三种互补可观测量，获得了一致的临界速度值，这些值系统性地低于朗道判据（Landau criterion）并随杂质半径增大而减小，与理论预期定性一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光学超流体为在量子流体动力学（quantum hydrodynamics）中研究杂质问题提供了新的平台，但此前缺乏对超临界现象的系统定量表征。作者旨在测量二维超流体流经具有强排斥势、可调大半径和有限质量的可移动杂质时的临界速度。

Method: 采用双波长光学方案在$^{87}\text{Rb}$热蒸汽中制备二维傍轴光学超流体，流经一个可移动杂质，并利用三种互补的可观测量评估临界速度：杂质尾迹中产生的涡旋（vortex）数量、杂质受到的流体动力学力（hydrodynamic force），以及杂质相对于流动的位移速度。

Result: 三种独立的测量方法给出的临界速度值彼此一致，且都系统性地低于朗道判据的预测，并随着杂质半径的增大而降低，结果与理论预期定性符合。

Conclusion: 这些结果验证了组合诊断方法的可靠性，确立了傍轴光学超流体作为研究量子流体动力学中杂质问题的多功能平台。

Abstract: We report the measurement of the critical velocity of a paraxial superfluid of light flowing past a mobile impurity, realized using a dual-wavelength optical scheme in a warm vapor of $^{87}\text{Rb}$. The superflow is two-dimensional by nature, while the impurity features a highly repulsive potential, a large tunable radius, and a finite mass. The critical velocity is evaluated by simultaneously monitoring three complementary observables: the number of vortices nucleated in the wake of the impurity, the hydrodynamic force exerted on it, and its displacement velocity relative to the flow. These independent methods yield consistent values for the critical velocity, which remain systematically below the Landau criterion and decrease with the impurity radius, in qualitative agreement with theoretical expectations. Our results demonstrate the reliability of these combined diagnostics, establishing paraxial superfluids of light as a versatile platform for exploring the impurity problem in quantum hydrodynamics.

</details>

### [223] [The finiteness conjecture for equilibria of electric fields generated by point charges of one sign](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03965)
*Alberto Enciso,Daniel Peralta-Salas*

Main category: math.DS

TL;DR: 本文证明了三维空间中由有限个同号点电荷产生的电场仅具有有限个平衡点，回答了Morse与Cairns于1969年提出的问题。对于异号电荷的混合情况，作者给出了平衡点数量的显式上界，并揭示了混合电荷情形下平衡点可以形成曲线的现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该问题源自1969年Morse和Cairns提出的悬而未决的问题，后经Eremenko在2008年重新表述，并作为猜想由Shapiro在2015年提出，作者旨在彻底解决这一经典问题。

Method: 证明方法结合了代数几何与复分析技术：利用R^3中库仑场（Coulomb field）点电荷配置的代数结构构造关联的复曲线，排除平衡点（equilibrium point）形成曲线的可能性；随后通过Bézout计数（Bézout count）导出平衡点数量的定量上界。

Result: 证明了同号电荷情形下平衡点数量有限；并推广到混合符号电荷情形，得到在S(x)非零区域内平衡点数量至多为2^{N-4}(N-1)(9N^2+9N+10)的上界，其中N为电荷数量。

Conclusion: 作者完整回答了1969年的经典问题，并确立了混合符号电荷情形下平衡点数量的定量界。同时指出，混合符号情形下库仑场可在S的零集上沿曲线消失，这与同号情形下S处处非零形成鲜明对照。

Abstract: We prove that the electric field generated in three-dimensional space by finitely many point charges of one sign has only finitely many equilibrium points, thereby answering a 1969 question of Morse and Cairns (restated by Eremenko in 2008 and, as a conjecture, by Shapiro in 2015). More generally, for nonzero charges $q_i$ of possibly mixed signs at distinct sites $\mathbf a_i\in\mathbf{R}^3$, we show that the Coulomb field has at most $2^{N-4}(N-1)(9N^2+9N+10)$ equilibria in the region where $S(\mathbf x):=\sum_iq_i|\mathbf x-\mathbf a_i|^{-3}$ does not vanish. Of course, for charges of one sign, $S$ is nonzero everywhere. The proof rules out curves of equilibria using algebraic geometry and complex analysis on an associated complex curve, and then applies a Bézout count to obtain a quantitative bound. Well-known examples show that, in the mixed-sign case, the Coulomb field can vanish on curves contained in the zero set of $S$.
